# OpenWork Agent Desktop — 详细设计文档

> **文档版本**: v4.3 (UI 栈重置 + CS 分离深化 + DDD 持久化详设 + 四能力域同步)
> **文档类型**: Detailed Design Document
> **审视视角**: 高级研发工程师 / 模块负责人
> **基于契约**: 统一设计契约 v4.3 / [08-技术选型与架构方案(实施版).md](./08-技术选型与架构方案(实施版).md) v3.8
> **关联文档**: [coding-agent-desktop-design.md](./coding-agent-desktop-design.md) v1.4 / [04-架构设计文档.md](./04-架构设计文档.md) v4.3 §11.9 DDD 持久化契约 / §16.10 Repository 接口清单 / [05-技术选型文档.md](./05-技术选型文档.md) v4.3 §22 DDD 持久化选型
> **编写日期**: 2026-07-02 (v4.0~v4.2) / 2026-07-25 (v4.3)

> **v4.3 变更说明 (UI 栈重置 + CS 分离深化 + DDD 持久化详设 + 四能力域同步)**:
> - **数据库表数对齐**: 全景数据库由 29 表更新为 **20 表** (与 01 v2.4 §4.5.1 一致)。v0.1 落地 12 表 (见 §2.1 表标注)；v0.2+ 增补表含 v4.3 新增 5 表: `providers / goals / scheduled_tasks / scheduled_task_runs / prompt_templates`。
> - **DDD Repository 详设**: §2 后增补 **§2.8 Repository 接口实现详设** (v4.3 新增)，给出 Drizzle ORM schema 同步、SqliteRepositoryProvider 实现、InMemory 单元测试 Provider、事务边界、迁移与 Infr adapt tap 与 04 §11.9 / §16.10 完整接口清单互引。
> - **四能力域详设**: §3 新增 **§3.14 定时任务与调度详设 (SCHED)**、**§3.15 目标管理详设 (GOAL)**、**§3.16 提示词管理详设 (PROMPT)**、**§3.17 Provider 抽象接入详设 (PROVIDER)** (v4.3 新增)；对应 02-PRD v2.4 新增的 REQ-SCHED/GOAL/PROMPT/PROVIDER 详情。
> - **UI 栈移除 LobeHub**: 文档 09 已删除；本文档所有引用 09 的位置定位到 04 §5 / 05 §4 / 08 §3.3；后端代码示例中 antd-style / @lobehub/* 不再出现。
> - **关联版本号同步刷新**: 01 v2.3→v2.4、02 v2.3→v2.4、04 v4.2→v4.3、05 v4.2→v4.3、08 v3.6→v3.8、coding v1.3→v1.4。

> **v4.2 变更说明** (相对 v4.1,基于 07-多角色审视评估报告 v2.0 修改清单 M1/M3/M4/M8/M11/M12/M13/M14/M15/M17/M18/M20 及 S6/S10):
> - **§2.1 数据模型**:增加"MVP 必需表标注"(v0.1 必需 12 表 vs v0.2+ 17 表),并补充 Worker SQLite 访问策略 (M1/M8)。
> - **§2.4 Migration Manager**:支持按版本阶段建表,新增 migration 文件命名规范 (V0.1__base.sql / V0.2__memory.sql) 与分阶段 DDL 规划 (M14)。
> - **§3 核心模块**:统一口径为"11 大顶层模块 + 17 子模块",新增模块映射表 (M20);新增 §3.0 API Key Vault 独立小节 (M12)。
> - **§3.5 PermissionGateway**:增加多客户端审批并发一致性机制 (乐观锁 + 版本号 + 409 Conflict) (M11)。
> - **§3.10 PluginSandbox**:增加沙箱资源限制配置表 (M13) 与 isolated-vm V8 双编译风险说明 + 回退方案 (S6)。
> - **§4.3 事件清单**:增加事件补发协议 (since 游标) 与消息 ID 幂等性设计 (M4)。
> - **§4.4 Transport**:新增 Transport 调试器详细设计 (消息日志结构 / 回放工具 / 断点配置) (S10)。
> - **§4.12 (新增) 9 层架构延迟预算**:引用 04 §3.4 延迟预算表 (M3)。
> - **§4.13 (新增) TS Project References 配置模板**:提供 tsconfig.json 模板与拓扑 (M17)。
> - **§7 测试策略**:扩展 Transport 故障注入 (断线/超时/乱序/重复) 与并发用例 (多客户端审批/并发工具调用) (M15)。
> - 关联文档版本号同步刷新:04 v4.0→v4.2、05 v4.1→v4.2、08 v3.3→v3.5、09 v1.2→v1.3、coding v1.1→v1.2 (M18)。

> **v4.1 变更说明** (相对 v4.0):
> - **§4 API 接口清单**由原 Electron IPC (contextBridge) 方案全面重构为**前后端分离**架构:后端为 headless Agent 引擎 (`@openwork/core`),前端为多个 thin client (Desktop / TUI / Web / CLI),通过统一协议 **JSON-RPC 2.0** 通信。
> - 新增多 Transport 适配层 (HTTP+SSE / WebSocket / stdio / Electron IPC / Embedded),新增客户端 SDK `OpenCodeClient`,新增工程目录多包 monorepo 划分。
> - §3 新增 §3.13「前后端边界与通信约束」,明确数据库访问仅限后端、事件经 Transport 层推送前端。
> - §2 数据库设计、§3.1-§3.12 核心模块、关键算法、错误码、测试策略保持不变。

> **v0.1 范围收缩策略** (基于 07 评估报告 §8.3 总体结论):
> v0.1 = "Desktop (嵌入式) + 4 模块 (Loop/Tool/MCP/Permission) + 12 表 + 6-8 周";TUI/Web/CLI/Service/Subagent/Multi-agent 推迟到 v0.2~v0.3。v4.1/v4.2 架构演进方向保留,但表与模块的分阶段交付在本文档中显式标注。

## 1. 文档定位

### 1.1 版本与定位

本文档是 OpenWork Agent Desktop 的**详细设计文档 v4.2**,基于统一设计契约 v4.2 编写,采用 **TypeScript 5.7 + Node.js 22 + Electron 35** 技术栈,描述了研发工程师编码所需的完整设计依据,包含:

1. **29 张表的完整 DDL**(字段、类型、约束、索引、外键、注释) — v0.1 必需 12 表已显式标注,其余 17 表为 v0.2+ 延后建表
2. **11 大顶层模块 + 17 子模块的 TypeScript 接口定义**(class / interface / type) — 模块映射见 §3.0
3. **关键算法的可执行级伪代码**(PAOR、压缩、RAG、权限、调度、整合)
4. **完整的 API 清单**(JSON-RPC 2.0 / Tool / Hook / Plugin SDK / MCP,前后端分离多 Transport)
5. **完整错误码表**与**测试策略**

> **v4.2 关键变更**:在 v4.1 前后端分离架构基础上,落实 07 评估报告"v0.1 范围收缩"策略 — v0.1 仅交付 Desktop (嵌入式) + 4 模块 (Loop/Tool/MCP/Permission) + 12 表 + 6-8 周;TUI/Web/CLI/Service/Subagent/Multi-agent 推迟到 v0.2~v0.3。架构演进方向保留,但表/模块/Transport 的分阶段交付在本文档中显式标注。

> **v4.1 关键架构变更**:自 v4.1 起,系统采用前后端分离架构。后端 (`@openwork/core`) 是无头 (headless) Agent 引擎,以多种模式运行 (嵌入式 / 服务式 / 子进程式);前端为多个 thin client (Desktop / TUI / Web / CLI),统一通过 **JSON-RPC 2.0** 协议与后端通信,不再依赖 Electron IPC 作为唯一通道。详见 §4。

### 1.3 技术栈总览(遵循 §8 决策)

| 维度 | 选型 | 版本 |
|------|------|:---:|
| 语言 | TypeScript | 5.7 |
| 运行时 | Node.js (后端可独立 / Electron 内置) | 22 LTS |
| 后端引擎 | @openwork/core (headless, 多模式) | — |
| 通信协议 | JSON-RPC 2.0 (多 Transport) | 2.0 |
| HTTP Transport | Fastify + SSE | 5 / — |
| WebSocket Transport | ws | 8 |
| stdio Transport | JSON-RPC over stdin/stdout | — |
| IPC Transport | Electron contextBridge + ipcMain/ipcRenderer | 35 |
| 桌面框架 | Electron + electron-vite | 35 / 5 |
| TUI 框架 | Ink (React for CLI) | 5 |
| CLI 框架 | Commander | 12 |
| 进程模型 | Main + Worker Threads + Plugin Sandbox | — |
| 数据库 | better-sqlite3 (WAL) — 仅后端访问 | 11 |
| 向量索引 | sqlite-vec | latest |
| 全文索引 | SQLite FTS5 | 内置 |
| 代码解析 | web-tree-sitter | 0.22 |
| 数据校验 | Zod | 3.x |
| 模板引擎 | Handlebars | 4.x |
| 插件沙箱 | isolated-vm | 5.x |
| MCP | @modelcontextprotocol/sdk | 1.0 |
| LLM SDK | openai + @anthropic-ai/sdk | 5 / 0.50 |

### 1.4 文档约定

1. 所有代码示例为 **TypeScript**(非 Rust),关键算法以伪代码 + TypeScript 形式表达
2. DDL 使用 SQLite 方言,所有时间戳为 `INTEGER` (Unix 秒)
3. ID 使用 `nanoid` 生成的 21 字符字符串
4. JSON 字段在表中以 `TEXT` 存储,应用层用 Zod 解析
5. 错误处理采用 `Result<T, E>` 模式 (自定义泛型类型,非 Rust 原生)

## 2. 数据库设计

### 2.1 完整 DDL (29 张表)

> **M1 修改 (v4.2 新增)**: 29 张表中仅 12 张为 v0.1 必需,其余 17 张为 v0.2+ 延后建表。MVP 必需表标注见下表,分阶段建表规划见 §2.6。

#### 2.1.0 MVP 必需表标注 (M1)

依据 07 评估报告 §1.3 与 §8.3 "v0.1 收缩策略",v0.1 仅交付 4 模块 (Loop/Tool/MCP/Permission),所需数据表如下:

| # | 表名 | v0.1 必需 | 用途 | 所属模块 | v0.2+ 阶段 |
|---|------|:---------:|------|----------|-----------|
| 1 | `projects` | ✅ | 项目元数据 | Loop | — |
| 2 | `sessions` | ✅ | 会话主表 | Loop | — |
| 3 | `messages` | ✅ | 对话消息 | Loop | — |
| 4 | `loop_steps` | ✅ | PAOR 步骤记录 | Loop | — |
| 5 | `tool_calls` | ✅ | 工具调用记录 | Tool | — |
| 6 | `checkpoints` | ✅ | 会话检查点 | Loop | — |
| 7 | `memories` | ✅ | 三级记忆基础表 (v0.1 仅写入门槛) | Memory (v0.1 简化) | — |
| 8 | `configs` | ✅ | 全局/项目配置 | Config | — |
| 9 | `profiles` | ✅ | 模型 Profile (Provider 配置) | Config | — |
| 10 | `audit_log` | ✅ | 审计日志 (append-only) | Permission | — |
| 11 | `events` | ✅ | 事件日志 / ring buffer 持久化 | Transport | — |
| 12 | `schema_version` | ✅ | 迁移版本记录 | Migration | — |
| 13 | `permission_decisions` | ⏸ | 权限审批决策记录 | Permission | v0.2 (v0.1 用内存缓存) |
| 14 | `model_calls` | ⏸ | 模型调用 token/成本 | Model | v0.2 |
| 15 | `cost_records` | ⏸ | 成本归集 (与 model_calls 合并审查) | Model | v0.2 |
| 16 | `index_meta` | ⏸ | 代码索引元数据 | Context | v0.2 (P1) |
| 17 | `code_chunks` | ⏸ | 代码块 | Context | v0.2 (P1) |
| 18 | `vector_chunks` | ⏸ | 向量块 (sqlite-vec) | Context | v0.2 (P1) |
| 19 | `subagents` | ⏸ | 子 Agent 元数据 | Subagent | v0.3 |
| 20 | `multiagent_sessions` | ⏸ | 多 Agent 协作会话 | Multi-agent | v0.3 |
| 21 | `agent_personas` | ⏸ | Agent 人格配置 | Subagent | v0.3 |
| 22 | `skills` | ⏸ | Skill 注册表 | Skill | v0.2 |
| 23 | `plugins` | ⏸ | 插件注册表 | Plugin | v0.2 |
| 24 | `plugin_state` | ⏸ | 插件运行状态 | Plugin | v0.2 |
| 25 | `hooks` | ⏸ | Hook 注册表 | Hooks | v0.2 |
| 26 | `mcp_servers` | ⏸ | MCP Server 连接配置 | MCP (v0.1 用 configs) | v0.2 |
| 27 | `mcp_tools` | ⏸ | MCP 工具缓存 | MCP | v0.2 |
| 28 | `memory_vectors` (虚拟表) | ⏸ | 记忆向量索引 | Memory | v0.2 |
| 29 | `code_chunks_vectors` (虚拟表) | ⏸ | 代码块向量索引 | Context | v0.2 (P1) |

> **说明**: ✅ = v0.1 必需 (12 张);⏸ = v0.2+ 延后 (17 张)。`permission_decisions` 在 v0.1 用 PermissionGateway 内存 `approvalCache` 实现,不持久化;`mcp_servers` 在 v0.1 复用 `configs` 表 (key=mcp.servers)。`memory_vectors` / `code_chunks_vectors` 为 sqlite-vec 虚拟表,见 §2.2。

> **v0.1 表数量与 07 报告对齐**: 07 §8.3 建议 "12 表",本表确认 v0.1 = 12 表 (含 schema_version);其余 17 表 (含 2 张虚拟表) 推迟到 v0.2~v0.3,通过 §2.4 Migration Manager 按版本阶段建表。

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- OpenWork Agent Desktop - 完整数据库 Schema v4.2
-- 数据库: SQLite (WAL 模式) + sqlite-vec + FTS5
-- 表数量: 29 (含 1 张 schema_version)
-- v0.1 必需: 12 张 (见 §2.1.0 标注);v0.2+ 延后: 17 张
-- 注: 以下 DDL 为完整 29 表定义,实际建表按 §2.6 分阶段执行
-- ============================================================

PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;
PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;
PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000;
PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;
PRAGMA cache_size = -64000;  -- 64MB

-- ============================================================
-- 1. projects - 项目
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
    id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                                   -- nanoid
    path            TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,                               -- 项目根绝对路径
    name            TEXT NOT NULL,                                      -- 项目名(目录名或自定义)
    description     TEXT,                                               -- 项目描述
    root_git_hash   TEXT,                                               -- 当前 HEAD commit hash
    git_branch      TEXT,                                               -- 当前分支
    language        TEXT,                                               -- 主语言: typescript | python | rust ...
    framework       TEXT,                                               -- 框架: react | vue | next | ...
    package_manager TEXT,                                               -- pnpm | npm | yarn | pip | cargo
    icon            TEXT,                                               -- 项目图标 URL 或 emoji
    color           TEXT,                                               -- 主题色 hex
    settings        TEXT,                                               -- JSON: 项目级 settings 覆盖
    last_opened_at  INTEGER NOT NULL,                                   -- 最近打开时间
    pinned          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                         -- 是否置顶 0/1
    archived        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                         -- 是否归档 0/1
    created_at      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_projects_last_opened ON projects(last_opened_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_projects_pinned      ON projects(pinned, last_opened_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- 2. sessions - 会话
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    project_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    profile_id           TEXT,                                           -- 关联 profiles.id (软引用)
    parent_session_id    TEXT,                                           -- Fork 来源
    parent_step_id       TEXT,                                           -- Fork 自哪一步
    title                TEXT,
    description          TEXT,
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active'
        CHECK(status IN ('active', 'paused', 'archived', 'forked', 'abandoned')),
    mode                 TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'build'
        CHECK(mode IN ('ask', 'plan', 'build', 'review')),              -- Agent 模式
    model_snapshot       TEXT,                                           -- JSON: { provider, model, params, router }
    profile_snapshot     TEXT,                                           -- JSON: 启动时 Profile 完整快照
    token_total_in       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    token_total_out      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    token_total_cache    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                     -- 缓存 token
    cost_total_usd       REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
    duration_total_ms    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    message_count        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    step_count           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tool_call_count      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    sub_agent_count      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tags                 TEXT,                                           -- JSON: string[]
    is_favorite          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    is_pinned            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                     -- 上下文钉选
    termination_reason   TEXT,                                           -- completed | user_stop | max_iter | cost_exceeded | error | cancelled
    termination_detail   TEXT,                                           -- 终止详情 JSON
    last_active_at       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_project    ON sessions(project_id, last_active_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_status     ON sessions(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_parent     ON sessions(parent_session_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_favorite   ON sessions(is_favorite, last_active_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_active    ON sessions(last_active_at DESC) WHERE status = 'active';

-- ============================================================
-- 3. messages - 消息
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    seq                  INTEGER NOT NULL,                              -- 会话内自增序号
    role                 TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(role IN ('user', 'assistant', 'tool', 'system', 'sub_agent')),
    sub_agent_id         TEXT,                                          -- role=sub_agent 时来源
    content              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: MessageContent { parts: ContentPart[] }
    original_content     TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 编辑前原始内容
    edited_at            INTEGER,
    edited_by            TEXT,                                          -- user | agent | system
    is_stale             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 因后续编辑标记为过期
    is_pinned            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 上下文钉选
    prompt_snapshot_hash TEXT,                                          -- 完整 prompt SHA256
    prompt_snapshot_path TEXT,                                          -- 完整 prompt 存储路径
    token_in             INTEGER,
    token_out            INTEGER,
    token_cache          INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    cost_usd             REAL,
    model_name           TEXT,
    model_provider       TEXT,
    finish_reason        TEXT,                                          -- stop | tool_calls | length | error | interrupted | cancelled
    error_code           TEXT,
    error_message        TEXT,
    metadata             TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 额外元数据
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    UNIQUE(session_id, seq)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_session    ON messages(session_id, seq);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_role       ON messages(session_id, role);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_created    ON messages(created_at);

-- FTS5 虚拟表: 消息全文搜索
-- 见 §2.3

-- ============================================================
-- 4. loop_steps - Loop 步骤 (PAOR trace)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS loop_steps (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    step                 INTEGER NOT NULL,                              -- 主循环步号
    iteration            INTEGER NOT NULL,                              -- 第几次迭代(同一 step 可能多迭代)
    phase                TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(phase IN ('perceive', 'act', 'observe', 'reflect', 'plan', 'verify', 'recover')),
    sub_phase            TEXT,                                          -- tool_call | tool_result | llm_response | self_reflection | plan_generation | test_run | error_recovery
    parent_loop_step_id  TEXT REFERENCES loop_steps(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- Sub-Agent 的父步骤
    sub_agent_id         TEXT,                                          -- 如果是 Sub-Agent 执行的步骤
    sub_agent_instance   TEXT,                                          -- Sub-Agent 实例 ID
    payload              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: TraceStepPayload
    payload_summary      TEXT,                                          -- 文本摘要(便于检索)
    token_in             INTEGER,
    token_out            INTEGER,
    cost_usd             REAL,
    duration_ms          INTEGER,
    is_checkpoint        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 该步是否为检查点
    is_sub_agent_root    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- Sub-Agent 任务根步
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'success'
        CHECK(status IN ('success', 'error', 'skipped', 'cancelled', 'pending')),
    error_code           TEXT,
    error_message        TEXT,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_loop_steps_session   ON loop_steps(session_id, step);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_loop_steps_parent    ON loop_steps(parent_loop_step_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_loop_steps_phase     ON loop_steps(session_id, phase);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_loop_steps_sub_agent ON loop_steps(sub_agent_instance);

-- ============================================================
-- 5. tool_calls - 工具调用
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_calls (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    message_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES messages(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    session_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    loop_step_id         TEXT REFERENCES loop_steps(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    sub_agent_instance   TEXT,                                          -- 来源 Sub-Agent 实例
    step_number          INTEGER NOT NULL,                              -- 所属 loop step
    call_index           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 同一消息中并发调用索引
    tool_name            TEXT NOT NULL,
    tool_source          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(tool_source IN ('builtin', 'mcp', 'plugin', 'skill', 'custom', 'sub_agent')),
    tool_source_id       TEXT,                                          -- MCP server_id / plugin_id / skill_id
    tool_version         TEXT,
    args                 TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: 入参
    args_schema_hash     TEXT,                                          -- 入参 schema hash (用于回放)
    result               TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 出参
    result_summary       TEXT,                                          -- 文本摘要(便于检索)
    original_result      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 用户修改前原始结果
    modified_result      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 用户修改后结果
    modified_by          TEXT,
    modified_at          INTEGER,
    risk_level           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(risk_level IN ('L0', 'L1', 'L2', 'L3')),
    requires_approval    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    approval_id          TEXT,                                          -- 关联 approval_requests.id
    approved_by          TEXT,                                          -- auto | user | session | project | permanent | denied
    approval_timestamp   INTEGER,
    duration_ms          INTEGER,
    tokens_included      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,                             -- 工具结果占用 token
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
        CHECK(status IN ('pending', 'awaiting_approval', 'denied', 'running',
                         'success', 'error', 'timeout', 'user_modified', 'cancelled')),
    error_code           TEXT,
    error_message        TEXT,
    retry_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    retry_parent_id      TEXT REFERENCES tool_calls(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- 重试来源
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    completed_at         INTEGER
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_session   ON tool_calls(session_id, step_number);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_message   ON tool_calls(message_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_status    ON tool_calls(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_source    ON tool_calls(tool_source, tool_source_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_loop      ON tool_calls(loop_step_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_sub_agent ON tool_calls(sub_agent_instance);

-- ============================================================
-- 6. checkpoints - Loop 检查点
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS checkpoints (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    step                 INTEGER NOT NULL,                              -- 检查点所在 step
    iteration            INTEGER NOT NULL,
    phase                TEXT NOT NULL,
    sub_agent_instance   TEXT,                                          -- Sub-Agent 检查点
    snapshot             TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: CheckpointData (state + context summary)
    snapshot_path        TEXT,                                          -- 完整快照存储路径(大对象外置)
    message_seq          INTEGER,                                       -- 当时消息序号
    token_usage          INTEGER,                                       -- 当时累计 token
    cost_usd             REAL,                                          -- 当时累计成本
    is_auto              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,                    -- 自动 vs 手动
    is_restored          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否被恢复过
    label                TEXT,                                          -- 用户标签
    description          TEXT,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_checkpoints_session ON checkpoints(session_id, step);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_checkpoints_sub     ON checkpoints(sub_agent_instance, step);

-- ============================================================
-- 7. sub_agents - Sub-Agent 定义/实例
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sub_agents (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                              -- 定义 ID (稳定)
    instance_id          TEXT,                                          -- 实例 ID (运行时生成, 派生实例时填充)
    parent_session_id    TEXT REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    name                 TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 定义名 (唯一)
    display_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    description          TEXT,
    persona              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- Persona 文本
    system_prompt_append TEXT,                                          -- 追加 system prompt
    model_override       TEXT,                                          -- JSON: { provider, model, params }
    tools_whitelist      TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: string[]
    tools_blacklist      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: string[]
    skills               TEXT,                                          -- JSON: string[] (skill ids)
    mcp_servers          TEXT,                                          -- JSON: string[] (server ids)
    max_iterations       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 15,
    max_parallel         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    context_isolation    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    context_inherit      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: { messages: N, files: bool, memory: bool }
    budget_limit_usd     REAL,
    budget_limit_tokens  INTEGER,
    timeout_secs         INTEGER DEFAULT 300,
    cost_usd             REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,                     -- 实例累计成本
    token_total          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'idle'
        CHECK(status IN ('idle', 'running', 'paused', 'completed', 'failed', 'cancelled', 'timeout')),
    started_at           INTEGER,
    completed_at         INTEGER,
    failure_reason       TEXT,
    source               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'builtin' CHECK(source IN ('builtin', 'user', 'profile', 'dynamic')),
    record_type          TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'definition'
        CHECK(record_type IN ('definition', 'instance')),              -- definition=模板, instance=运行实例
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sub_agents_name     ON sub_agents(name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sub_agents_session  ON sub_agents(parent_session_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sub_agents_instance ON sub_agents(instance_id) WHERE instance_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uniq_sub_agents_def_name ON sub_agents(name) WHERE record_type = 'definition';

-- ============================================================
-- 8. agent_messages - 多 Agent 通信
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_messages (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    from_agent_id        TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 主 Agent ID 或 sub_agent instance_id
    from_agent_type      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(from_agent_type IN ('main', 'sub_agent', 'coordinator', 'expert')),
    to_agent_id          TEXT,                                          -- NULL 表示广播
    to_agent_type        TEXT,
    message_type         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(message_type IN ('task', 'result', 'query', 'response', 'control', 'error', 'broadcast')),
    channel              TEXT,                                          -- 通道名(可选,用于多通道协作)
    subject              TEXT,                                          -- 主题
    payload              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: 消息体
    payload_summary      TEXT,                                          -- 文本摘要
    priority             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 0=普通, 1=高, 2=紧急
    requires_ack         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否需要确认
    acked_at             INTEGER,
    acked_by             TEXT,
    is_processed         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否被消费
    processed_at         INTEGER,
    ttl_secs             INTEGER,                                       -- 消息生存时间
    expires_at           INTEGER,                                       -- 过期时间
    parent_message_id    TEXT REFERENCES agent_messages(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- 关联消息(reply chain)
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_messages_session  ON agent_messages(session_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_messages_to       ON agent_messages(to_agent_id, is_processed);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_messages_from     ON agent_messages(from_agent_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_messages_channel  ON agent_messages(channel, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_messages_unprocessed ON agent_messages(is_processed, to_agent_id) WHERE is_processed = 0;

-- ============================================================
-- 9. memories - 记忆
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    scope                TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(scope IN ('work', 'project', 'global', 'session')),
    project_id           TEXT REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    session_id           TEXT REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,  -- scope=session 时
    category             TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(category IN ('decision', 'fact', 'preference', 'lesson', 'todo', 'persona', 'relationship', 'pattern')),
    key                  TEXT,                                          -- 命名记忆的 key (可选)
    title                TEXT,
    content              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 记忆正文
    content_format       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'markdown' CHECK(content_format IN ('markdown', 'plain', 'json', 'yaml')),
    embedding_id         TEXT,                                          -- 关联 memory_vectors.id (应用层维护)
    metadata             TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 额外元数据
    source_session_id    TEXT,                                          -- 来源会话
    source_step_id       TEXT,                                          -- 来源 loop step
    source_agent         TEXT,                                          -- 来源 agent (main | sub_agent:xxx)
    tags                 TEXT,                                          -- JSON: string[]
    importance           REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.5,                     -- 0.0-1.0 重要度
    confidence           REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 1.0,                     -- 0.0-1.0 置信度
    access_count         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    last_accessed_at     INTEGER,
    expires_at           INTEGER,                                       -- 过期时间(可选)
    is_consolidated      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否已被整合
    consolidated_into    TEXT,                                          -- 整合到的目标记忆 ID
    is_pinned            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 钉选不被驱逐
    is_archived          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 归档
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_scope     ON memories(scope, project_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_category  ON memories(category);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_updated   ON memories(updated_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_key       ON memories(scope, key) WHERE key IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_session   ON memories(session_id) WHERE session_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memories_pinned    ON memories(is_pinned, importance DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- 10. memory_vectors - 向量索引 (sqlite-vec 虚拟表,见 §2.2)
-- 应用层通过 sqlite-vec 扩展创建,DDL 见 §2.2
-- ============================================================

-- ============================================================
-- 11. memory_consolidation - 记忆整合任务
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_consolidation (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    scope                TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(scope IN ('project', 'global', 'session')),
    project_id           TEXT REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    session_id           TEXT REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    task_type            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(task_type IN ('summarize', 'merge', 'deduplicate', 'promote', 'decay', 'archive')),
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
        CHECK(status IN ('pending', 'running', 'completed', 'failed', 'cancelled')),
    source_memory_ids    TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: string[] 源记忆
    target_memory_id     TEXT REFERENCES memories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- 整合后目标记忆
    source_count         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    trigger              TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(trigger IN ('scheduled', 'manual', 'threshold', 'session_end', 'idle')),
    strategy             TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 整合策略参数
    result_summary       TEXT,                                          -- 整合结果摘要
    model_used           TEXT,                                          -- 使用的 LLM
    tokens_used          INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    cost_usd             REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
    error_code           TEXT,
    error_message        TEXT,
    started_at           INTEGER,
    completed_at         INTEGER,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mem_consolidation_scope   ON memory_consolidation(scope, project_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mem_consolidation_status  ON memory_consolidation(status, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mem_consolidation_target  ON memory_consolidation(target_memory_id);

-- ============================================================
-- 12. skills - Skills 定义
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS skills (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name                 TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    display_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    version              TEXT NOT NULL,
    description          TEXT,
    category             TEXT,                                          -- tdd | refactor | debug | review | scaffold | docs | security | migration | commit | pr | test
    icon                 TEXT,
    prompt_template      TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- Handlebars 模板
    system_prompt_append TEXT,                                          -- 追加到 System Prompt
    tools_whitelist      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: string[]
    tools_blacklist      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: string[]
    workflow_steps       TEXT,                                          -- JSON: WorkflowStep[]
    dependencies         TEXT,                                          -- JSON: { mcp_servers: [], skills: [], sub_agents: [] }
    script_path          TEXT,                                          -- 可选脚本路径
    parameters           TEXT,                                          -- JSON Schema: Skill 参数定义
    trigger              TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'manual'
        CHECK(trigger IN ('manual', 'auto', 'keyword', 'intent', 'schedule')),
    trigger_keywords     TEXT,                                          -- JSON: string[] 关键词
    trigger_intent       TEXT,                                          -- 意图识别 prompt
    enabled              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    priority             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
    source               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'builtin' CHECK(source IN ('builtin', 'user', 'marketplace')),
    source_url           TEXT,
    usage_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    success_count        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    failure_count        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    rating               REAL,                                          -- 用户评分 0.0-5.0
    is_builtin           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_skills_category ON skills(category);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_skills_enabled  ON skills(enabled, priority);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_skills_trigger  ON skills(trigger, enabled);

-- ============================================================
-- 13. skill_invocations - Skill 调用记录
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS skill_invocations (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    skill_id             TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES skills(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    session_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    loop_step_id         TEXT REFERENCES loop_steps(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    sub_agent_instance   TEXT,
    trigger              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- manual | auto | keyword | intent | schedule | command
    trigger_source       TEXT,                                          -- 触发来源(user | agent | hook | command)
    parameters           TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 实际传入参数
    workflow_state       TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 工作流执行状态
    current_step         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_steps          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    result               TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 最终结果
    result_summary       TEXT,
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
        CHECK(status IN ('pending', 'running', 'paused', 'completed', 'failed', 'cancelled')),
    error_code           TEXT,
    error_message        TEXT,
    duration_ms          INTEGER,
    tokens_used          INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    cost_usd             REAL DEFAULT 0.0,
    user_rating          INTEGER,                                       -- 1-5 用户事后评分
    user_feedback        TEXT,
    started_at           INTEGER,
    completed_at         INTEGER,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_skill_inv_skill   ON skill_invocations(skill_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_skill_inv_session ON skill_invocations(session_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_skill_inv_status  ON skill_invocations(status);

-- ============================================================
-- 14. plugins - 插件注册
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plugins (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name                 TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    display_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    version              TEXT NOT NULL,
    description          TEXT,
    author               TEXT,
    homepage             TEXT,
    license              TEXT,
    entry_point          TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 插件入口文件路径
    main_module          TEXT,                                          -- ESM 模块名
    manifest_path        TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- manifest.json 路径
    manifest_hash        TEXT,                                          -- manifest 内容 hash
    permissions          TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: string[] 权限声明
    contributes          TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: PluginContributes
    config_schema        TEXT,                                          -- JSON Schema: 插件配置
    default_config       TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 默认配置
    dependencies         TEXT,                                          -- JSON: { npm: {}, plugins: [] }
    enabled              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    status               TEXT DEFAULT 'installed'
        CHECK(status IN ('installed', 'enabled', 'disabled', 'error', 'uninstalling')),
    is_builtin           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    source               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'local' CHECK(source IN ('local', 'marketplace', 'git', 'npm')),
    source_url           TEXT,
    installed_version    TEXT,                                          -- 当前安装版本
    latest_version       TEXT,                                          -- 最新版本(市场查询)
    update_available     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    error_message        TEXT,
    storage_path         TEXT,                                          -- 插件数据存储路径
    installed_at         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_plugins_name    ON plugins(name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_plugins_enabled ON plugins(enabled, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_plugins_source  ON plugins(source);

-- ============================================================
-- 15. plugin_instances - 插件实例运行
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plugin_instances (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                              -- 实例 ID (运行时生成)
    plugin_id            TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES plugins(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    session_id           TEXT REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, -- NULL=全局实例
    worker_id            TEXT,                                          -- Worker Thread ID
    sandbox_id           TEXT,                                          -- isolated-vm Isolate ID
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'starting'
        CHECK(status IN ('starting', 'running', 'paused', 'stopped', 'crashed', 'error')),
    pid                  INTEGER,                                       -- 进程 PID (如果适用)
    memory_usage_bytes   INTEGER,
    cpu_time_ms          INTEGER,
    start_time           INTEGER NOT NULL,
    last_heartbeat       INTEGER,
    heartbeat_interval   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 30,                   -- 秒
    config_snapshot      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 启动时配置快照
    permissions_granted  TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 实际授予的权限
    capabilities_loaded  TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 实际加载的能力 (tools/hooks/commands)
    error_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    last_error           TEXT,
    crash_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    restart_count        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    UNIQUE(plugin_id, session_id) -- 同一插件在同一会话只一个实例(全局实例 session_id=NULL)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_plugin_instances_plugin  ON plugin_instances(plugin_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_plugin_instances_session ON plugin_instances(session_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_plugin_instances_status  ON plugin_instances(status);

-- ============================================================
-- 16. mcp_servers - MCP Server 配置
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_servers (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name                 TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    display_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    description          TEXT,
    transport            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(transport IN ('stdio', 'sse', 'http', 'websocket')),
    config               TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: MCPServerConfig (command/url/env/headers/auth)
    capabilities         TEXT,                                          -- JSON: { tools: bool, resources: bool, prompts: bool }
    enabled              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    auto_start           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,                    -- 应用启动时自动启动
    auto_restart         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,                    -- 崩溃后自动重启
    max_restart_count    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 3,
    restart_delay_secs   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 5,
    health_check_interval_secs INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 30,
    request_timeout_secs INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 60,
    status               TEXT DEFAULT 'disconnected'
        CHECK(status IN ('connected', 'disconnected', 'connecting', 'error', 'restarting')),
    last_health_check    INTEGER,
    last_connected_at    INTEGER,
    last_error           TEXT,
    tools_count          INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    resources_count      INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    prompts_count        INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    tools_cache          TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 工具清单缓存
    resources_cache      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 资源清单缓存
    protocol_version     TEXT,                                          -- MCP 协议版本
    server_info          TEXT,                                          -- JSON: server 返回的 info
    scope                TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'global'
        CHECK(scope IN ('global', 'project', 'profile')),
    scope_id             TEXT,                                          -- project_id | profile_id
    source               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user' CHECK(source IN ('builtin', 'user', 'marketplace', 'profile')),
    is_builtin           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mcp_servers_name    ON mcp_servers(name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mcp_servers_enabled ON mcp_servers(enabled, status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mcp_servers_scope   ON mcp_servers(scope, scope_id);

-- ============================================================
-- 17. hooks - Hooks 配置
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hooks (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name                 TEXT NOT NULL,
    display_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    description          TEXT,
    trigger              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- Hook 触发点标识 (见 §3.8)
    trigger_filter       TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 触发条件过滤器
    script               TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 脚本内容或路径
    script_hash          TEXT,                                          -- 脚本内容 hash
    language             TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(language IN ('typescript', 'javascript', 'python', 'shell')),
    runtime              TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'sandbox'
        CHECK(runtime IN ('sandbox', 'worker', 'main', 'external')),
    enabled              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    timeout_secs         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 30,
    on_error             TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'warn' CHECK(on_error IN ('skip', 'warn', 'abort', 'retry')),
    retry_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    can_intercept        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否可拦截/修改数据
    priority             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,                  -- 越小越优先
    async_execution      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否异步执行(不阻塞主流程)
    scope                TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'global'
        CHECK(scope IN ('global', 'project', 'profile', 'plugin')),
    scope_id             TEXT,                                          -- project_id | profile_id | plugin_id
    source               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user' CHECK(source IN ('builtin', 'user', 'plugin', 'marketplace')),
    is_builtin           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    dependencies         TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 依赖的 npm 包
    permissions          TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 权限声明
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hooks_trigger ON hooks(trigger, enabled, priority);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hooks_scope  ON hooks(scope, scope_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hooks_name   ON hooks(name);

-- ============================================================
-- 18. hook_executions - Hook 执行记录
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hook_executions (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    hook_id              TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES hooks(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    session_id           TEXT REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    loop_step_id         TEXT REFERENCES loop_steps(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    trigger              TEXT NOT NULL,
    trigger_context      TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 触发时上下文
    input_data           TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 输入数据
    output_data          TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 输出数据(可能修改)
    modified             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否修改了数据
    aborted              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否中止了主流程
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
        CHECK(status IN ('pending', 'running', 'success', 'error', 'timeout', 'skipped', 'aborted')),
    error_code           TEXT,
    error_message        TEXT,
    duration_ms          INTEGER,
    memory_usage_bytes   INTEGER,
    worker_id            TEXT,
    started_at           INTEGER,
    completed_at         INTEGER,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hook_exec_hook    ON hook_executions(hook_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hook_exec_session ON hook_executions(session_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hook_exec_status  ON hook_executions(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hook_exec_trigger ON hook_executions(trigger, created_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- 19. permission_rules - 权限规则
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS permission_rules (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name                 TEXT NOT NULL,
    description          TEXT,
    rule_type            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(rule_type IN ('tool', 'command', 'path', 'network', 'mcp', 'plugin')),
    target_pattern       TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 工具名/命令模式/路径模式/URL 模式
    target_glob          TEXT,                                          -- glob 模式 (用于路径匹配)
    action               TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(action IN ('allow', 'deny', 'ask', 'ask_once', 'dry_run')),
    risk_level_override  TEXT CHECK(risk_level_override IN ('L0', 'L1', 'L2', 'L3')),
    conditions           TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 条件表达式 (args/session/user)
    approval_required    INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    approval_scope       TEXT CHECK(approval_scope IN ('once', 'session', 'project', 'permanent')),
    approval_ttl_secs    INTEGER,                                       -- 批准有效期
    priority             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,                  -- 越小越优先
    enabled              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    scope                TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'global'
        CHECK(scope IN ('global', 'project', 'profile', 'session')),
    scope_id             TEXT,
    source               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user' CHECK(source IN ('builtin', 'user', 'plugin', 'profile', 'admin')),
    is_builtin           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    is_immutable         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 不可变规则(安全底线)
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_perm_rules_target   ON permission_rules(rule_type, target_pattern);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_perm_rules_scope    ON permission_rules(scope, scope_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_perm_rules_priority ON permission_rules(priority, enabled);

-- ============================================================
-- 20. approval_requests - 审批请求
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS approval_requests (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    loop_step_id         TEXT REFERENCES loop_steps(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    tool_call_id         TEXT REFERENCES tool_calls(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    sub_agent_instance   TEXT,
    request_type         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(request_type IN ('tool_call', 'plan', 'file_change', 'command', 'config_change', 'memory_write', 'sub_agent_spawn')),
    target_name          TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 工具名/命令名/文件路径
    target_args          TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 入参
    risk_level           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(risk_level IN ('L0', 'L1', 'L2', 'L3')),
    dry_run_result       TEXT,                                          -- JSON: dry-run 预览结果
    summary              TEXT,                                          -- 人类可读摘要
    detailed_diff        TEXT,                                          -- 详细 diff(可选)
    status               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending'
        CHECK(status IN ('pending', 'approved', 'denied', 'expired', 'cancelled', 'auto_approved')),
    decision             TEXT,                                          -- approved | denied | modified
    decision_scope       TEXT CHECK(decision_scope IN ('once', 'session', 'project', 'permanent')),
    decided_by           TEXT,                                          -- user | auto | system
    decided_at           INTEGER,
    decision_reason      TEXT,
    modified_args        TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 用户修改后的参数
    expires_at           INTEGER,                                       -- 过期时间
    notified             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否已通知 UI
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_approval_session ON approval_requests(session_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_approval_status  ON approval_requests(status, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_approval_pending ON approval_requests(status) WHERE status = 'pending';

-- ============================================================
-- 21. configs - 配置
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS configs (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    scope                TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(scope IN ('global', 'project', 'profile', 'session')),
    scope_id             TEXT,                                          -- project_id | profile_id | session_id
    path                 TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 配置路径: "model.primary" / "loop.max_iterations"
    value                TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON 值
    value_type           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(value_type IN ('string', 'number', 'boolean', 'object', 'array', 'null')),
    schema_version       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '1.0',
    schema_path          TEXT,                                          -- JSON Schema 引用
    description          TEXT,
    is_secret            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 敏感字段(加密存储)
    encrypted_value      TEXT,                                          -- 加密后的值(若 is_secret=1)
    is_readonly          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 只读(系统强制)
    is_default           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否为默认值
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    UNIQUE(scope, scope_id, path)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_configs_scope ON configs(scope, scope_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_configs_path  ON configs(path);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_configs_secret ON configs(is_secret) WHERE is_secret = 1;

-- ============================================================
-- 22. config_history - 配置历史
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS config_history (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    config_id            TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES configs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    config_path          TEXT NOT NULL,
    scope                TEXT NOT NULL,
    scope_id             TEXT,
    old_value            TEXT,                                          -- JSON
    new_value            TEXT,                                          -- JSON
    old_schema_version   TEXT,
    new_schema_version   TEXT,
    changed_by           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(changed_by IN ('user', 'system', 'profile_import', 'migration', 'plugin', 'agent')),
    changed_by_id        TEXT,                                          -- 用户 ID 或插件 ID
    change_reason        TEXT,                                          -- 变更原因
    is_rollback          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否为回滚操作
    rollback_from_id     TEXT REFERENCES config_history(id),           -- 回滚自哪次变更
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_config_history_config ON config_history(config_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_config_history_path   ON config_history(config_path, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_config_history_scope  ON config_history(scope, scope_id, created_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- 23. profiles - Profile
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS profiles (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name                 TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    display_name         TEXT NOT NULL,
    description          TEXT,
    version              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    extends              TEXT,                                          -- 父 Profile ID (继承)
    config               TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: ProfileConfig
    config_schema_version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '1.0',
    is_default           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    is_active            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 当前激活
    is_locked            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 锁定(只读)
    tags                 TEXT,                                          -- JSON: string[]
    author               TEXT,
    homepage             TEXT,
    source               TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'local' CHECK(source IN ('local', 'imported', 'marketplace', 'git', 'builtin')),
    source_url           TEXT,
    installed_version    TEXT,
    latest_version       TEXT,
    update_available     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    checksum             TEXT,                                          -- 内容校验和
    signature            TEXT,                                          -- 签名(可选)
    usage_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    last_used_at         INTEGER,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_profiles_name    ON profiles(name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_profiles_active  ON profiles(is_active);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_profiles_default ON profiles(is_default);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_profiles_source  ON profiles(source);

-- ============================================================
-- 24. code_chunks - 代码块索引
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS code_chunks (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    project_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    file_path            TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 相对项目根路径
    file_hash            TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 文件内容 hash (blake3)
    language             TEXT NOT NULL,
    chunk_type           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(chunk_type IN ('file', 'symbol', 'block', 'line_range')),
    symbol_name          TEXT,                                          -- 符号名 (function/class/var)
    symbol_kind          TEXT CHECK(symbol_kind IN ('function', 'method', 'class', 'struct', 'enum',
                                                    'interface', 'variable', 'constant', 'module', 'type',
                                                    'trait', 'import', 'namespace', 'unknown')),
    symbol_full_name     TEXT,                                          -- 完整限定名 (e.g. Module.Class.method)
    parent_symbol        TEXT,                                          -- 父符号
    start_line           INTEGER NOT NULL,
    end_line             INTEGER NOT NULL,
    start_column         INTEGER,
    end_column           INTEGER,
    source_code          TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 原始代码片段
    source_code_hash     TEXT NOT NULL,
    signature            TEXT,                                          -- 函数签名
    doc_comment          TEXT,                                          -- 文档注释
    dependencies         TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 依赖 (imports/calls)
    references           TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 被引用位置
    embedding_id         TEXT,                                          -- 关联向量 ID
    embedding_model      TEXT,                                          -- 嵌入模型
    embedding_dim        INTEGER,
    is_test              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否测试代码
    is_exported          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否导出
    complexity_score     INTEGER,                                       -- 复杂度评分
    last_accessed_at     INTEGER,
    access_count         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    UNIQUE(project_id, file_path, start_line, end_line)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_code_chunks_project  ON code_chunks(project_id, file_path);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_code_chunks_file     ON code_chunks(file_path);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_code_chunks_symbol   ON code_chunks(project_id, symbol_kind, symbol_name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_code_chunks_full_name ON code_chunks(symbol_full_name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_code_chunks_hash     ON code_chunks(file_hash);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_code_chunks_language ON code_chunks(language);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_code_chunks_updated  ON code_chunks(updated_at DESC);

-- FTS5 虚拟表: 代码块全文搜索(见 §2.3)

-- ============================================================
-- 25. index_meta - 索引元数据
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS index_meta (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    project_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    index_type           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(index_type IN ('vector', 'fts', 'symbol', 'dependency', 'call_graph')),
    backend              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- sqlite-vec | lancedb | fts5 | tree-sitter | custom
    status               TEXT DEFAULT 'idle'
        CHECK(status IN ('idle', 'indexing', 'updating', 'paused', 'error', 'corrupted')),
    last_indexed_at      INTEGER,
    last_incremental_at  INTEGER,
    total_files          INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_chunks         INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_symbols        INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_embeddings     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    index_size_bytes     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    git_commit_hash      TEXT,
    config               TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 索引配置快照
    error_message        TEXT,
    error_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    last_error_at        INTEGER,
    stats                TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 索引统计
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    updated_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    UNIQUE(project_id, index_type)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_index_meta_project ON index_meta(project_id, index_type);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_index_meta_status  ON index_meta(status);

-- ============================================================
-- 26. audit_log - 审计日志 (append-only, 不可删除)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_log (
    seq                  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    ts                   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    session_id           TEXT,                                          -- 关联会话(软引用,不级联删除)
    actor_type           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(actor_type IN ('user', 'agent', 'sub_agent', 'system', 'plugin', 'mcp', 'hook')),
    actor_id             TEXT NOT NULL,
    actor_name           TEXT,                                          -- 人类可读名
    action               TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- tool_call | file_write | config_change | ...
    action_category      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(action_category IN ('read', 'write', 'delete', 'execute', 'network', 'config', 'auth', 'system', 'approval')),
    target_type          TEXT,                                          -- tool | file | config | session | memory | mcp | plugin | user
    target_id            TEXT,
    target_path          TEXT,                                          -- 目标路径(文件/URL)
    detail               TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 详细信息
    args_summary         TEXT,                                          -- 入参摘要(脱敏)
    result_summary       TEXT,                                          -- 结果摘要(脱敏)
    risk_level           TEXT CHECK(risk_level IN ('L0', 'L1', 'L2', 'L3')),
    approved             INTEGER,                                       -- 是否经过批准 (NULL=不适用)
    approved_by          TEXT,
    approval_id          TEXT,                                          -- 关联 approval_requests.id
    ip_address           TEXT,
    user_agent           TEXT,
    duration_ms          INTEGER,
    cost_usd             REAL,
    signature            TEXT,                                          -- HMAC 签名(防篡改)
    previous_seq         INTEGER,                                       -- 前一条 seq(链式哈希)
    hash                 TEXT,                                          -- 当前条目哈希
    UNIQUE(seq)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_ts       ON audit_log(ts);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_actor    ON audit_log(actor_type, actor_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_action   ON audit_log(action);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_target   ON audit_log(target_type, target_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_session  ON audit_log(session_id, ts);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_log_risk     ON audit_log(risk_level, ts) WHERE risk_level IN ('L2', 'L3');

-- ============================================================
-- 27. model_calls - 模型调用日志
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS model_calls (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id           TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    loop_step_id         TEXT REFERENCES loop_steps(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    message_id           TEXT REFERENCES messages(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    sub_agent_instance   TEXT,
    provider             TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- anthropic | openai | ollama | ...
    model                TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- claude-sonnet-4 | gpt-4o | ...
    endpoint             TEXT,                                          -- API endpoint
    request_messages_count INTEGER NOT NULL,
    request_tokens_est   INTEGER,                                       -- 请求 token 估算
    token_in             INTEGER NOT NULL,
    token_out            INTEGER NOT NULL,
    cache_read_tokens    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    cache_write_tokens   INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    reasoning_tokens     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,                             -- o1/o3 思考 token
    cost_input_usd       REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
    cost_output_usd      REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
    cost_cache_read_usd  REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
    cost_cache_write_usd REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
    cost_total_usd       REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
    duration_ms          INTEGER NOT NULL,
    time_to_first_token_ms INTEGER,                                     -- TTFT
    stream               INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,                    -- 是否流式
    finish_reason        TEXT,                                          -- stop | tool_calls | length | error
    status               TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('success', 'error', 'fallback', 'rate_limited', 'timeout', 'cancelled')),
    error_code           TEXT,
    error_message        TEXT,
    error_type           TEXT,                                          -- network | auth | rate_limit | context_length | content_filter | server | unknown
    retry_count          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    fallback_from_id     TEXT REFERENCES model_calls(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- Fallback 来源
    fallback_from_provider TEXT,
    fallback_from_model  TEXT,
    prompt_hash          TEXT,                                          -- prompt 内容 hash (用于去重/分析)
    tools_count          INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    tool_calls_count     INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_model_calls_session  ON model_calls(session_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_model_calls_provider ON model_calls(provider, model);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_model_calls_created  ON model_calls(created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_model_calls_status   ON model_calls(status, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_model_calls_sub      ON model_calls(sub_agent_instance);

-- ============================================================
-- 28. events - 事件流 (Event Bus 持久化)
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
    id                   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    seq                  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,             -- 全局自增序列
    session_id           TEXT REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    source               TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 事件源: loop_engine | tool | mcp | hook | ...
    source_id            TEXT,                                          -- 源 ID (worker_id / plugin_id)
    event_type           TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- 事件类型 (见 §3.17)
    event_category       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(event_category IN ('agent', 'tool', 'model', 'session', 'config', 'system', 'ui', 'error', 'lifecycle')),
    payload              TEXT NOT NULL,                                 -- JSON: 事件数据
    payload_summary      TEXT,                                          -- 文本摘要
    severity             TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'info'
        CHECK(severity IN ('debug', 'info', 'warn', 'error', 'critical')),
    priority             INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    correlated_id        TEXT,                                          -- 关联事件 ID
    parent_event_id      TEXT REFERENCES events(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    trace_id             TEXT,                                          -- 分布式追踪 ID
    span_id              TEXT,                                          -- span ID
    is_persisted         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,                    -- 是否持久化(瞬时事件=0)
    is_consumed          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                    -- 是否已被消费
    consumed_by          TEXT,                                          -- JSON: 消费者列表
    subscriber_count     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    expires_at           INTEGER                                        -- 过期时间(可选,用于清理)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_seq       ON events(seq);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_session   ON events(session_id, seq);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_type      ON events(event_type, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_source    ON events(source, created_at);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_severity  ON events(severity, created_at) WHERE severity IN ('warn', 'error', 'critical');
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_trace     ON events(trace_id) WHERE trace_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_unconsumed ON events(is_consumed, created_at) WHERE is_consumed = 0;

-- ============================================================
-- 29. schema_version - Schema 版本
-- ============================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_version (
    version              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    description          TEXT NOT NULL,
    migration_script     TEXT,                                          -- 迁移脚本文件名
    checksum             TEXT,                                          -- 迁移内容 hash
    applied_at           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    applied_by           TEXT,                                          -- 用户/系统
    execution_time_ms    INTEGER,                                       -- 执行耗时
    is_reversible        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    rollback_script      TEXT                                            -- 回滚脚本文件名
);

-- 初始版本
INSERT OR IGNORE INTO schema_version (version, description, migration_script, applied_by)
VALUES (1, 'Initial schema v4.0 - 29 tables TypeScript implementation', '001_init_v4.sql', 'system');
```

### 2.2 sqlite-vec 虚拟表

`memory_vectors` 与 `code_chunks` 的向量索引使用 sqlite-vec 扩展。由于 sqlite-vec 是 SQLite 扩展,需通过 better-sqlite3 的 `loadExtension` 加载,虚拟表 DDL 在应用启动后执行。

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- memory_vectors - 记忆向量索引 (sqlite-vec 虚拟表)
-- ============================================================
-- 维度默认 1536 (text-embedding-3-small),可配置
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_vectors USING vec0(
    id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,        -- 关联 memories.id
    memory_id     TEXT,                    -- 冗余字段便于查询
    embedding     FLOAT[1536],             -- 向量数据
    scope         TEXT,                    -- work | project | global | session
    project_id    TEXT,
    category      TEXT,
    importance    FLOAT,
    created_at    INTEGER
);

-- 代码块向量索引
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS code_chunks_vectors USING vec0(
    id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,        -- 关联 code_chunks.id
    chunk_id      TEXT,                    -- 冗余字段
    embedding     FLOAT[1536],
    project_id    TEXT,
    language      TEXT,
    symbol_kind   TEXT,
    file_path     TEXT,
    created_at    INTEGER
);
```

```typescript
// src/main/db/vector-init.ts
import path from 'node:path';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

/**
 * 加载 sqlite-vec 扩展并初始化向量虚拟表
 */
export function initVectorExtension(db: Database.Database, dim = 1536): void {
  const extPath = path.join(
    process.env.APP_ROOT!,
    'node_modules',
    'sqlite-vec',
    process.platform === 'win32' ? 'vec0.dll' :
    process.platform === 'darwin' ? 'vec0.dylib' : 'vec0.so'
  );

  try {
    db.loadExtension(extPath);
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error(`Failed to load sqlite-vec extension: ${(e as Error).message}`);
  }

  // 创建向量虚拟表(幂等)
  db.exec(`
    CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_vectors USING vec0(
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, memory_id TEXT, embedding FLOAT[${dim}],
      scope TEXT, project_id TEXT, category TEXT, importance FLOAT, created_at INTEGER
    );
    CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS code_chunks_vectors USING vec0(
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, chunk_id TEXT, embedding FLOAT[${dim}],
      project_id TEXT, language TEXT, symbol_kind TEXT, file_path TEXT, created_at INTEGER
    );
  `);
}

/**
 * 向量搜索 - 记忆
 */
export function vectorSearchMemories(
  db: Database.Database,
  queryVec: number[],
  topK = 10,
  filter?: { scope?: string; projectId?: string; category?: string }
): Array<{ memory_id: string; distance: number }> {
  const where: string[] = [];
  const params: unknown[] = [Buffer.from(new Float32Array(queryVec).buffer), topK];
  if (filter?.scope) { where.push('scope = ?'); params.push(filter.scope); }
  if (filter?.projectId) { where.push('project_id = ?'); params.push(filter.projectId); }
  if (filter?.category) { where.push('category = ?'); params.push(filter.category); }

  const whereClause = where.length ? `WHERE ${where.join(' AND ')}` : '';
  const rows = db.prepare(`
    SELECT memory_id, distance
    FROM memory_vectors
    ${whereClause}
    ORDER BY embedding <-> ?
    LIMIT ?
  `).all(...params) as Array<{ memory_id: string; distance: number }>;

  return rows;
}
```

### 2.3 FTS5 全文索引

```sql
-- ============================================================
-- FTS5 全文索引虚拟表
-- ============================================================

-- 消息全文索引
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages_fts USING fts5(
    message_id UNINDEXED,
    session_id UNINDEXED,
    role UNINDEXED,
    content,
    content_summary,
    tokenize = 'unicode61 remove_diacritics 2'
);

-- 代码块全文索引
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS code_chunks_fts USING fts5(
    chunk_id UNINDEXED,
    project_id UNINDEXED,
    file_path,
    symbol_name,
    symbol_full_name,
    source_code,
    doc_comment,
    language UNINDEXED,
    tokenize = 'unicode61 remove_diacritics 2'
);

-- 会话标题/描述索引
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions_fts USING fts5(
    session_id UNINDEXED,
    project_id UNINDEXED,
    title,
    description,
    tags,
    tokenize = 'unicode61 remove_diacritics 2'
);

-- 记忆全文索引
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories_fts USING fts5(
    memory_id UNINDEXED,
    scope UNINDEXED,
    project_id UNINDEXED,
    title,
    content,
    key,
    tags,
    category UNINDEXED,
    tokenize = 'unicode61 remove_diacritics 2'
);
```

```sql
-- FTS5 触发器: 同步 messages 表到 messages_fts
CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS messages_ai AFTER INSERT ON messages BEGIN
    INSERT INTO messages_fts(message_id, session_id, role, content, content_summary)
    VALUES (new.id, new.session_id, new.role,
            json_extract(new.content, '$.parts[0].text'),
            substr(json_extract(new.content, '$.parts[0].text'), 1, 200));
END;

CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS messages_ad AFTER DELETE ON messages BEGIN
    DELETE FROM messages_fts WHERE message_id = old.id;
END;

CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS messages_au AFTER UPDATE ON messages BEGIN
    DELETE FROM messages_fts WHERE message_id = old.id;
    INSERT INTO messages_fts(message_id, session_id, role, content, content_summary)
    VALUES (new.id, new.session_id, new.role,
            json_extract(new.content, '$.parts[0].text'),
            substr(json_extract(new.content, '$.parts[0].text'), 1, 200));
END;

-- 类似的触发器为 code_chunks / sessions / memories 创建 (略)
```

### 2.4 Migration Manager

```typescript
// src/main/db/migration-manager.ts
import path from 'node:path';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { z } from 'zod';

const MigrationSchema = z.object({
  version: z.number().int().positive(),
  description: z.string(),
  up: z.string(),
  down: z.string().optional(),
  isReversible: z.boolean().default(true),
});

type Migration = z.infer<typeof MigrationSchema>;

export interface MigrationResult {
  fromVersion: number;
  toVersion: number;
  applied: string[];
  durationMs: number;
  backupPath?: string;
}

export class MigrationManager {
  private readonly migrations: Migration[] = [];

  constructor(
    private readonly db: Database.Database,
    private readonly migrationsDir: string,
    private readonly backupDir: string
  ) {}

  /** 注册迁移(代码定义) */
  register(migration: unknown): void {
    const m = MigrationSchema.parse(migration);
    this.migrations.push(m);
  }

  /** 从目录加载迁移 SQL 文件: 001_init.sql, 002_add_xxx.sql ... */
  async loadFromDir(): Promise<void> {
    const files = await fs.readdir(this.migrationsDir);
    const sqlFiles = files
      .filter(f => f.endsWith('.sql'))
      .sort();

    for (const file of sqlFiles) {
      const match = file.match(/^(\d+)_(.+)\.sql$/);
      if (!match) continue;
      const version = parseInt(match[1], 10);
      const description = match[2].replace(/_/g, ' ');
      const content = await fs.readFile(path.join(this.migrationsDir, file), 'utf8');

      // 拆分 up/down (使用 -- @down 分隔)
      const [up, down] = content.split('-- @down');
      this.migrations.push({
        version,
        description,
        up: up.trim(),
        down: down?.trim() || '',
        isReversible: Boolean(down?.trim()),
      });
    }
  }

  /** 获取当前版本 */
  getCurrentVersion(): number {
    // schema_version 表可能不存在(首次启动)
    const tableExists = this.db.prepare(
      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='schema_version'"
    ).get();

    if (!tableExists) return 0;

    const row = this.db.prepare(
      'SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version), 0) as v FROM schema_version'
    ).get() as { v: number } | undefined;

    return row?.v ?? 0;
  }

  /** 执行迁移(带完整事务、回滚、备份) */
  async migrate(targetVersion?: number): Promise<MigrationResult> {
    const current = this.getCurrentVersion();
    const target = targetVersion ?? Math.max(...this.migrations.map(m => m.version), current);

    if (current >= target) {
      return { fromVersion: current, toVersion: current, applied: [], durationMs: 0 };
    }

    const pending = this.migrations
      .filter(m => m.version > current && m.version <= target)
      .sort((a, b) => a.version - b.version);
```
