```typescript
    if (pending.length === 0) {
      return { fromVersion: current, toVersion: current, applied: [], durationMs: 0 };
    }

    // 1. 迁移前备份
    const backupPath = await this.createBackup(current);

    const startTime = Date.now();
    const applied: string[] = [];

    // 2. 在单一事务中执行所有迁移
    try {
      const migrateAll = this.db.transaction(() => {
        for (const migration of pending) {
          const migrationStart = Date.now();
          // 执行 up SQL
          this.db.exec(migration.up);
          // 记录版本
          this.db.prepare(
            'INSERT INTO schema_version (version, description, migration_script, applied_by, execution_time_ms, is_reversible) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)'
          ).run(
            migration.version,
            migration.description,
            `v${migration.version}_${migration.description.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}.sql`,
            'system',
            Date.now() - migrationStart,
            migration.isReversible ? 1 : 0
          );
          applied.push(`v${migration.version}: ${migration.description}`);
        }
      });
      migrateAll();

      return {
        fromVersion: current,
        toVersion: target,
        applied,
        durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
        backupPath,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      // 事务自动回滚 - 数据库保持原版本
      // 但需恢复备份以防部分文件级副作用
      console.error('Migration failed, attempting to restore from backup:', error);
      if (backupPath) {
        await this.restoreBackup(backupPath);
      }
      throw new Error(`Migration failed at version ${current + 1}: ${(error as Error).message}`);
    }
  }

  /** 回滚到指定版本 */
  async rollback(targetVersion: number): Promise<MigrationResult> {
    const current = this.getCurrentVersion();
    if (targetVersion >= current) {
      return { fromVersion: current, toVersion: current, applied: [], durationMs: 0 };
    }

    const rollbackMigrations = this.migrations
      .filter(m => m.version > targetVersion && m.version <= current)
      .sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version);  // 降序回滚

    const backupPath = await this.createBackup(current);
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const applied: string[] = [];

    const rollbackAll = this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const migration of rollbackMigrations) {
        if (!migration.isReversible || !migration.down) {
          throw new Error(`Migration v${migration.version} is not reversible`);
        }
        this.db.exec(migration.down);
        this.db.prepare('DELETE FROM schema_version WHERE version = ?').run(migration.version);
        applied.push(`rollback v${migration.version}: ${migration.description}`);
      }
    });
    rollbackAll();

    return {
      fromVersion: current,
      toVersion: targetVersion,
      applied,
      durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      backupPath,
    };
  }

  /** 创建备份 (VACUUM INTO) */
  private async createBackup(currentVersion: number): Promise<string> {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
    const backupFile = path.join(this.backupDir, `backup-v${currentVersion}-${timestamp}.db`);
    await fs.mkdir(this.backupDir, { recursive: true });
    this.db.exec(`VACUUM INTO '${backupFile}'`);
    return backupFile;
  }

  /** 从备份恢复 */
  private async restoreBackup(backupPath: string): Promise<void> {
    // 简化实现: 实际需要关闭当前连接,替换文件,重新打开
    console.log(`Restoring from backup: ${backupPath}`);
    // TODO: 实现文件级恢复
  }

  /** 校验迁移完整性 */
  verifyIntegrity(): { ok: boolean; missing: number[]; issues: string[] } {
    const applied = new Set(
      this.db.prepare('SELECT version FROM schema_version').all()
        .map((r: any) => r.version as number)
    );
    const issues: string[] = [];
    const missing: number[] = [];

    for (const m of this.migrations) {
      if (!applied.has(m.version)) missing.push(m.version);
    }

    // 检查连续性
    const appliedSorted = [...applied].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    for (let i = 1; i < appliedSorted.length; i++) {
      if (appliedSorted[i] !== appliedSorted[i - 1] + 1) {
        issues.push(`Version gap: ${appliedSorted[i - 1]} -> ${appliedSorted[i]}`);
      }
    }

    return { ok: missing.length === 0 && issues.length === 0, missing, issues };
  }
}
```

### 2.5 种子数据

```typescript
// src/main/db/seed.ts
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

/**
 * 种子数据 - 首次启动时插入
 */
export function seedDatabase(db: Database.Database): void {
  const seeded = db.prepare('SELECT value FROM configs WHERE scope = ? AND path = ?')
    .get('global', 'system.seeded') as { value: string } | undefined;

  if (seeded) return; // 已种子化

  const tx = db.transaction(() => {
    // 1. 默认 Profile
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO profiles (id, name, display_name, description, config, is_default, is_active, source)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 1, 'builtin')
    `).run(
      'profile-default',
      'default',
      '默认 Profile',
      '系统默认 Profile,可被用户覆盖',
      JSON.stringify({
        model: { primary: 'anthropic:claude-sonnet-4' },
        persona: 'concise',
        tools: { enabled: ['read_file', 'write_file', 'edit_file', 'glob', 'grep', 'search_codebase', 'run_command'] },
        permission: { default: 'approve' },
      })
    );

    // 2. 默认配置项
    const defaultConfigs: Array<[string, unknown]> = [
      ['model.primary', 'anthropic:claude-sonnet-4'],
      ['model.fallback', ['openai:gpt-4o', 'ollama:qwen2.5-coder']],
      ['model.router.task_routing', true],
      ['model.router.cost_routing', true],
      ['model.budget.session_usd', 5.0],
      ['model.budget.daily_usd', 20.0],
      ['model.budget.on_exceed', 'warn'],
      ['loop.max_iterations', 50],
      ['loop.max_iterations_per_subtask', 15],
      ['loop.reflection_interval', 5],
      ['loop.planning.auto_plan_threshold', 3],
      ['loop.hitl.mode', 'key_nodes'],
      ['loop.sub_agent.max_parallel', 4],
      ['loop.checkpoint.interval', 'every_step'],
      ['prompt.persona.preset', 'concise'],
      ['prompt.token_budget_strategy', 'compress-dynamic'],
      ['context.compression.strategy', 'compress-dynamic'],
      ['context.compression.recency_window', 6],
      ['context.compression.summary_chunk', 4],
      ['context.retrieval.top_k', 10],
      ['context.retrieval.rrf_k', 60],
      ['harness.permission.default_policy', 'approve'],
      ['harness.sandbox.mode', 'native'],
      ['harness.execution.concurrency', 4],
      ['harness.execution.default_timeout', 60],
      ['ui.theme', 'tokyonight'],
      ['ui.layout', 'dev'],
    ];

    const insertConfig = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO configs (id, scope, path, value, value_type, is_default, description)
      VALUES (?, 'global', ?, ?, ?, 1, ?)
    `);

    for (const [path, value] of defaultConfigs) {
      const type = Array.isArray(value) ? 'array' : typeof value;
      insertConfig.run(
        `cfg-${path}`,
        path,
        JSON.stringify(value),
        type,
        `Default value for ${path}`
      );
    }

    // 3. 内置 Sub-Agent 定义
    const builtinSubAgents = [
      {
        id: 'subagent-code-reviewer',
        name: 'code-reviewer',
        display_name: '代码审查专家',
        persona: 'You are a strict senior code reviewer. Focus on correctness, security, performance, and maintainability. Be specific and actionable.',
        tools_whitelist: JSON.stringify(['read_file', 'grep', 'glob', 'search_codebase', 'diagnostics']),
        max_iterations: 10,
        timeout_secs: 180,
      },
      {
        id: 'subagent-test-writer',
        name: 'test-writer',
        display_name: '测试编写专家',
        persona: 'You are a TDD practitioner. Write clear, isolated, deterministic tests covering edge cases.',
        tools_whitelist: JSON.stringify(['read_file', 'write_file', 'edit_file', 'run_command', 'grep']),
        max_iterations: 15,
        timeout_secs: 300,
      },
      {
        id: 'subagent-refactorer',
        name: 'refactorer',
        display_name: '重构专家',
        persona: 'You are a refactoring expert. Apply design patterns, reduce coupling, improve cohesion. Preserve behavior.',
        tools_whitelist: JSON.stringify(['read_file', 'write_file', 'edit_file', 'move_file', 'grep', 'search_codebase']),
        max_iterations: 20,
        timeout_secs: 600,
      },
    ];

    const insertSubAgent = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO sub_agents (id, name, display_name, persona, tools_whitelist,
                               max_iterations, timeout_secs, source, record_type)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'builtin', 'definition')
    `);
    for (const sa of builtinSubAgents) {
      insertSubAgent.run(sa.id, sa.name, sa.display_name, sa.persona,
                         sa.tools_whitelist, sa.max_iterations, sa.timeout_secs);
    }

    // 4. 内置 Skills
    const builtinSkills = [
      {
        id: 'skill-tdd',
        name: 'tdd',
        display_name: 'TDD 工作流',
        description: 'Test-Driven Development: Red-Green-Refactor',
        category: 'tdd',
        prompt_template: '遵循 TDD 循环:1) 写失败测试 2) 最小实现 3) 重构',
      },
      {
        id: 'skill-refactor',
        name: 'refactor',
        display_name: '代码重构',
        description: '安全重构:保证行为不变',
        category: 'refactor',
        prompt_template: '识别代码异味,设计重构方案,逐步应用,验证等价',
      },
      {
        id: 'skill-code-review',
        name: 'code-review',
        display_name: '代码审查',
        description: '系统性 Code Review',
        category: 'review',
        prompt_template: '从正确性/安全性/性能/可维护性四维度审查',
      },
    ];
    const insertSkill = db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO skills (id, name, display_name, description, category, prompt_template, source, is_builtin)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'builtin', 1)
    `);
    for (const s of builtinSkills) {
      insertSkill.run(s.id, s.name, s.display_name, s.description, s.category, s.prompt_template);
    }

    // 5. 标记已种子化
    db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO configs (id, scope, path, value, value_type, is_default, description)
      VALUES ('cfg-system-seeded', 'global', 'system.seeded', 'true', 'boolean', 0, 'Database seeded flag')
    `).run();
  });

  tx();
}
```

### 2.6 MVP 必需表分阶段建表 (M1/M14)

> **M1/M14 修改 (v4.2 新增)**: 本节落实 07 评估报告 §1.3 与 §6.2 的"按版本阶段建表"建议,与 §2.4 Migration Manager 配合,定义 v0.1~v0.3 的分阶段 DDL 规划与 migration 文件命名规范。

#### 2.6.1 分阶段建表规划

| 阶段 | 版本 | 建表清单 (相对前一阶段增量) | 累计表数 | migration 文件 |
|------|------|------------------------------|:--------:|----------------|
| **Phase A** | v0.1 | projects, sessions, messages, loop_steps, tool_calls, checkpoints, memories, configs, profiles, audit_log, events, schema_version | 12 | `V0_1__base.sql` |
| **Phase B** | v0.2 | permission_decisions, model_calls, cost_records, skills, plugins, plugin_state, hooks, mcp_servers, mcp_tools, memory_vectors (虚拟表) | 22 | `V0_2__permission_model_plugin_mcp.sql` + `V0_2__memory_vectors.sql` |
| **Phase C** | v0.2 (P1) | index_meta, code_chunks, vector_chunks, code_chunks_vectors (虚拟表), messages_fts (虚拟表), code_chunks_fts (虚拟表) | 28 | `V0_2__code_index.sql` |
| **Phase D** | v0.3 | subagents, multiagent_sessions, agent_personas | 31 (含 §2.1 之外扩展表) | `V0_3__multiagent.sql` |

> **说明**: Phase A 即 v0.1 必需 12 表;Phase B 在 v0.2 启用 Permission 持久化、Model 成本、Plugin/Skill/Hook/MCP 模块;Phase C 为 P1 优先级的代码索引能力;Phase D 为 v0.3 的多 Agent 协作能力。

#### 2.6.2 Migration 文件命名规范

为支持按版本阶段建表,§2.4 的 migration 文件命名在原 `001_init.sql` 数字前缀基础上,**新增版本阶段命名约定** (二者可共存,版本阶段文件以 `V` 前缀区分):

```
migrations/
├── V0_1__base.sql                           # v0.1 必需 12 表 (Phase A)
├── V0_2__permission_model_plugin_mcp.sql    # v0.2 模块扩展 (Phase B 第 1 部分)
├── V0_2__memory_vectors.sql                 # v0.2 记忆向量虚拟表 (Phase B 第 2 部分,需 sqlite-vec)
├── V0_2__code_index.sql                     # v0.2 P1 代码索引 (Phase C)
├── V0_3__multiagent.sql                     # v0.3 多 Agent (Phase D)
└── (legacy) 001_init.sql, 002_*.sql         # 兼容旧命名 (按数字序执行)
```

**命名规则**: `V<版本>__<描述>.sql`,版本号下划线替代点 (如 `V0_1` = v0.1)。`__` (双下划线) 分隔版本与描述。`V` 前缀文件按版本号排序执行,数字前缀文件按数字序执行,二者混排时 `V` 前缀优先于数字前缀。

**migration 文件示例 (V0_1__base.sql 片段)**:

```sql
-- V0_1__base.sql — v0.1 必需 12 表
-- 含: projects, sessions, messages, loop_steps, tool_calls, checkpoints,
--      memories, configs, profiles, audit_log, events, schema_version

PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;
PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects ( /* 见 §2.1 DDL */ );
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions ( /* ... */ );
-- ... (其余 10 张表)

-- @down
-- 回滚: v0.1 base 不支持 down (基线)
SELECT 1;  -- no-op
```

**MigrationManager 适配 (扩展 loadFromDir)**:

```typescript
// §2.4 MigrationManager.loadFromDir 扩展:支持 V 前缀版本阶段命名
async loadFromDir(): Promise<void> {
  const files = await fs.readdir(this.migrationsDir);
  const sqlFiles = files.filter(f => f.endsWith('.sql'));

  // V 前缀文件优先 (版本阶段),数字前缀文件按数字序 (兼容)
  const versioned = sqlFiles
    .filter(f => /^V(\d+)_(\d+)__(.+)\.sql$/.test(f))
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const [ma, mb] = [a.match(/^V(\d+)_(\d+)/)!, b.match(/^V(\d+)_(\d+)/)!];
      const va = parseInt(ma[1]) * 100 + parseInt(ma[2]);
      const vb = parseInt(mb[1]) * 100 + parseInt(mb[2]);
      return va - vb;
    });
  const numeric = sqlFiles
    .filter(f => /^\d+_.+\.sql$/.test(f))
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const na = parseInt(a.match(/^(\d+)/)![1], 10);
      const nb = parseInt(b.match(/^(\d+)/)![1], 10);
      return na - nb;
    });

  for (const file of [...versioned, ...numeric]) {
    // ... 解析逻辑同 §2.4 (拆分 up/down,推入 migrations 数组)
  }
}
```

#### 2.6.3 阶段建表与 Migration Manager 协作

- **目标版本注入**: 启动时根据 `package.json` 的 `version` 字段推导目标阶段 (如 `0.1.0` ⇒ Phase A,`0.2.0` ⇒ Phase B+C,`0.3.0` ⇒ Phase D),传给 `MigrationManager.migrate(targetVersion)`。
- **跨阶段升级**: v0.1 → v0.2 升级时,自动执行 `V0_2__*.sql`;若用户跳过 v0.2 直接升 v0.3,顺序执行 V0_2 + V0_3。
- **降级保护**: 跨阶段降级 (v0.3 → v0.2) 默认禁止 (因 Phase D 引入 v0.3 专属表),`rollback(targetVersion)` 仅在同阶段内回滚。

### 2.7 Worker SQLite 访问策略 (M8)

> **M8 修改 (v4.2 新增)**: 落实 07 评估报告 §3.5 建议,明确 better-sqlite3 同步 API 在 Worker Threads 间的访问模式。better-sqlite3 的 `Database` 句柄**不可跨 Worker 序列化 / 不可共享**,需采用"主进程代理写入 + Worker 只读"模式。

#### 2.7.1 访问模式总览

| 进程/Worker | 持有 DB 连接 | 允许操作 | 实现方式 |
|-------------|:------------:|----------|----------|
| 后端主线程 (L5) | ✅ 1 个 RW 连接 | 全部 (CRUD + migration) | `better-sqlite3` 直接调用 |
| Agent Worker (L6) | ❌ 不持有 | 通过 MessagePort 请求主线程 | `worker.postMessage` + 等待回复 |
| Tool Worker (L7) | ❌ 不持有 | 同上 | 同上 |
| Indexer Worker (L7) | ✅ 1 个 RO 连接 (WAL) | 只读 (SELECT) | `better-sqlite3` readonly 模式 |
| Plugin Sandbox (L7) | ❌ 不持有 | 经 Tool Worker / Agent Worker 代理 | 不直接访问 DB |

> **设计依据**: better-sqlite3 是同步 API,Worker Threads 间无法共享 db 句柄 (V8 对象不可跨 isolate 传递);但 SQLite WAL 模式支持多连接并发读,Indexer Worker 可独立打开只读连接。

#### 2.7.2 主进程代理写入协议

```typescript
// packages/core/src/db/worker-db-proxy.ts
import { parentPort, MessagePort } from 'node:worker_threads';
import type { Database } from 'better-sqlite3';

/** Worker → 主进程的 DB 请求消息 */
type DbRequest =
  | { kind: 'prepare'; sql: string; params?: unknown[] }
  | { kind: 'exec'; sql: string }
  | { kind: 'transaction'; ops: Array<{ sql: string; params?: unknown[] }> };

/** 主进程 → Worker 的 DB 响应消息 */
type DbResponse =
  | { kind: 'rows'; rows: unknown[] }
  | { kind: 'write'; changes: number; lastInsertRowid: number | string }
  | { kind: 'ok' }
  | { kind: 'error'; message: string; code?: string };

/** 主进程侧:监听 Worker 请求并代理执行 */
export function attachDbProxyToWorker(db: Database, port: MessagePort): void {
  port.on('message', (req: DbRequest) => {
    try {
      switch (req.kind) {
        case 'prepare': {
          const stmt = db.prepare(req.sql);
          const rows = req.params ? stmt.all(...req.params) : stmt.all();
          port.postMessage({ kind: 'rows', rows } satisfies DbResponse);
          break;
        }
        case 'exec': {
          db.exec(req.sql);
          port.postMessage({ kind: 'ok' } satisfies DbResponse);
          break;
        }
        case 'transaction': {
          const tx = db.transaction(() => {
            for (const op of req.ops) {
              const stmt = db.prepare(op.sql);
              op.params ? stmt.run(...op.params) : stmt.run();
            }
          });
          tx();
          port.postMessage({ kind: 'ok' } satisfies DbResponse);
          break;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      port.postMessage({
        kind: 'error',
        message: (e as Error).message,
        code: (e as { code?: string }).code,
      } satisfies DbResponse);
    }
  });
}

/** Worker 侧:封装 DB 调用为 Promise (经 MessagePort 请求主进程) */
export class WorkerDbClient {
  constructor(private port: MessagePort) {}

  query<T = unknown>(sql: string, params?: unknown[]): Promise<T[]> {
    return this.request({ kind: 'prepare', sql, params });
  }

  exec(sql: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.request({ kind: 'exec', sql });
  }

  transaction(ops: Array<{ sql: string; params?: unknown[] }>): Promise<void> {
    return this.request({ kind: 'transaction', ops });
  }

  private request(req: DbRequest): Promise<any> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const onMsg = (res: DbResponse) => {
        this.port.off('message', onMsg);
        if (res.kind === 'error') reject(new Error(res.message));
        else resolve(res.kind === 'rows' ? res.rows : undefined);
      };
      this.port.on('message', onMsg);
      this.port.postMessage(req);
    });
  }
}
```

#### 2.7.3 访问时序图

```
 Agent Worker                Main (RW conn)              Indexer Worker (RO conn)
      │                           │                            │
      │ 1. query('SELECT ...')    │                            │
      ├──────────────────────────>│                            │
      │                           │ 2. db.prepare().all()      │
      │                           │  (同步,better-sqlite3)      │
      │ 3. {rows: [...]}          │                            │
      │<──────────────────────────┤                            │
      │                           │                            │
      │                           │                  4. query('SELECT code_chunks ...')
      │                           │<────────────────────────────┤
      │                           │ 5. (Indexer 直读 WAL,不经主进程)
      │                           │                            │
      │ 6. write('INSERT ...')    │                            │
      ├──────────────────────────>│                            │
      │                           │ 7. db.prepare().run()      │
      │                           │  (主进程串行化写入,WAL)     │
      │ 8. {changes: 1}           │                            │
      │<──────────────────────────┤                            │
```

> **关键约束**:
> 1. **写入串行化**: 所有 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE 经主进程 RW 连接,SQLite WAL 模式下写入天然串行,避免并发写冲突。
> 2. **读并发**: Indexer Worker 可独立打开 RO 连接 (WAL 允许并发读),无需经主进程代理,降低大代码库索引时的 IPC 开销。
> 3. **事务边界**: Worker 的 `transaction()` 请求整体在主进程同一事务内执行,保证原子性。
> 4. **v0.1 简化**: v0.1 单 Agent Worker + 单 Indexer Worker,无需连接池;v0.2+ 多 Worker 时考虑读连接池 (上限 4 个 RO 连接)。

#### 2.7.4 嵌入式 vs 服务式模式差异

| 模式 | DB 连接持有者 | Worker 访问 | 备注 |
|------|---------------|-------------|------|
| 嵌入式 (v0.1 默认) | Electron Main 进程 (主线程) | Agent Worker 经 IPC + MessagePort 代理 | 单 Desktop 实例 |
| 服务式 (v0.2+) | 后端 Node 进程 (主线程) | Worker 经 MessagePort 代理 (同进程内) | 多客户端共享 |
| 子进程式 (v0.2+) | 后端子进程 (主线程) | 同上 | TUI/CLI 场景 |

---

### 2.8 DDD Repository 持久化层实现详设 (v4.3 新增)

> 本节对应 04 §11.9 / §16.10 与 05 §22 的工程落地。给出 Drizzle ORM schema 同步、SqliteRepositoryProvider 实现、事务边界、InMemory 单元测试与 Port 接口的具体设计。

#### 2.8.1 工程目录 (与 04 §11.9.4 一致)

```
packages/core/src/
├── domain/
│   ├── entity/                     # 聚合根类型 (Session/Message/ToolCall/.../Goal/ScheduledTask/PromptTemplate/ProviderConfig)
│   ├── repository.ts               # Repository<T> 泛型接口
│   └── repositories/               # 各聚合根 Repository 接口
├── application/                    # 用例编排 / 事务边界 / 调用 RepositoryProvider
└── infra/db/
    ├── sqlite/                     # Sqlite *Repository 实现 (better-sqlite3 + drizzle-orm)
    │   ├── SqliteRepositoryProvider.ts
    │   ├── SqliteSessionRepository.ts ...
    ├── drizzle/
    │   ├── schema.ts              # Drizzle schema (与 §2.1 DDL ↔ Drizzle mappings 对齐)
    │   └── client.ts              # drizzle 实例工厂
    ├── in-memory/                 # 单元测试 InMemory Provider
    │   └── InMemoryRepositoryProvider.ts
    ├── ports/                     # VectorIndexPort / FtsIndexPort / KeychainPort / FileSystemPort
    └── migrations/                # drizzle-kit 生成的 SQL 迁移文件 (配合 §2.4 Migration Manager)
```

#### 2.8.2 Drizzle schema 同步

```typescript
// packages/core/src/infra/db/drizzle/schema.ts
import { sqliteTable, text, integer, blob } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
// v0.1 12 表
export const sessions   = sqliteTable('sessions',   { id: text('id').primaryKey(), project_id: text('project_id').notNull(), title: text('title'), status: text('status', { enum: ['active','archived']}).notNull(), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull(), updated_at: integer('updated_at').notNull() });
export const messages   = sqliteTable('messages',   { id: text('id').primaryKey(), session_id: text('session_id').notNull(), role: text('role').notNull(), content: text('content').notNull(), tokens: integer('tokens'), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const tool_calls = sqliteTable('tool_calls', { id: text('id').primaryKey(), session_id: text('session_id').notNull(), step_id: text('step_id').notNull(), tool_name: text('tool_name').notNull(), args: text('args').notNull(), result: text('result'), approved: integer('approved', { mode: 'boolean' }), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const loop_steps = sqliteTable('loop_steps', { id: text('id').primaryKey(), session_id: text('session_id').notNull(), phase: text('phase', { enum: ['perceive','act','observe','reflect']}).notNull(), payload: text('payload').notNull(), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const configs    = sqliteTable('configs',    { id: text('id').primaryKey(), key: text('key').notNull().unique(), value: text('value').notNull(), scope: text('scope', { enum: ['global','project','profile','session']}).notNull(), updated_at: integer('updated_at').notNull() });
export const config_history = sqliteTable('config_history', { id: text('id').primaryKey(), key: text('key').notNull(), value: text('value').notNull(), version: integer('version').notNull(), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const mcp_servers = sqliteTable('mcp_servers', { id: text('id').primaryKey(), name: text('name').notNull(), transport: text('transport').notNull(), status: text('status').notNull(), config: text('config').notNull(), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const profiles   = sqliteTable('profiles',   { id: text('id').primaryKey(), name: text('name').notNull(), extends: text('extends'), content: text('content').notNull(), active: integer('active', { mode: 'boolean' }).notNull().default(false), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const audit_log  = sqliteTable('audit_log',  { id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }), actor: text('actor').notNull(), action: text('action').notNull(), target: text('target'), detail: text('detail').notNull(), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const model_calls= sqliteTable('model_calls',{ id: text('id').primaryKey(), session_id: text('session_id'), provider: text('provider').notNull(), model: text('model').notNull(), input_tokens: integer('input_tokens').notNull(), output_tokens: integer('output_tokens').notNull(), cost: real('cost').notNull(), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const checkpoints= sqliteTable('checkpoints',{ id: text('id').primaryKey(), session_id: text('session_id').notNull(), step_id: text('step_id').notNull(), state: blob('state').notNull(), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
export const providers  = sqliteTable('providers',  { id: text('id').primaryKey(), kind: text('kind').notNull(), name: text('name').notNull(), config: text('config').notNull(), capability_mask: integer('capability_mask').notNull(), enabled: integer('enabled', { mode: 'boolean' }).notNull().default(true), created_at: integer('created_at').notNull() });
// v0.2+ 表与 v4.3 新增 5 表 (memories/skills/sub_agents/commands/hooks/goals/scheduled_tasks/scheduled_task_runs/prompt_templates/index_meta) 在对应版本启用时增补
```

> Drizzle schema 与 §2.1 物理 DDL 必须**单一真相源对齐**：推荐工作流为「Drizzle schema 为源 → `drizzle-kit generate:sqlite` 生成 SQL → 经 §2.4 Migration Manager 执行」。避免手写 SQL 与 schema 漂移。

#### 2.8.3 SqliteRepositoryProvider 实现

```typescript
// packages/core/src/infra/db/sqlite/SqliteRepositoryProvider.ts
import { drizzle, type BetterSQLite3Database } from 'drizzle-orm/better-sqlite3';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import * as schema from '../drizzle/schema';
import type { RepositoryProvider, Transaction } from '../../../domain';

export class SqliteRepositoryProvider implements RepositoryProvider {
  readonly session: SessionRepository;
  readonly message: MessageRepository;
  readonly toolCall: ToolCallRepository;
  readonly loopStep: LoopStepRepository;
  readonly config: ConfigRepository;
  readonly configHistory: ConfigHistoryRepository;
  readonly mcpServer: McpServerRepository;
  readonly profile: ProfileRepository;
  readonly auditLog: AuditLogRepository;
  readonly modelCall: ModelCallRepository;
  readonly checkpoint: CheckpointRepository;
  readonly provider: ProviderRepository;
  // v0.2+ optional (memory/hook/skill/subAgent/command/indexMeta)
  // v0.3+ v4.3 新增 (goal/scheduledTask/scheduledTaskRun/promptTemplate)

  private db: BetterSQLite3Database;

  constructor(private dbPath: string) {
    const sqlite = new Database(dbPath, { fileMustExist: true });
    sqlite.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
    this.db = drizzle(sqlite, { schema });
    this.session = new SqliteSessionRepository(this.db);
    this.message = new SqliteMessageRepository(this.db);
    // ... 注入其余 12 个 v0.1 仓储
  }

  async withTx<T>(work: (tx: Transaction) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    return this.db.transaction(async (drizzleTx) => {
      const tx = wrapDrizzleTx(drizzleTx);
      return work(tx);
    });
  }
}
```

#### 2.8.4 单个 Repository 实现示例

```typescript
// packages/core/src/infra/db/sqlite/SqliteSessionRepository.ts
import { eq, and, desc } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { sessions } from '../drizzle/schema';
import type { SessionRepository, Session, Page, FindOptions, RepositoryQuery } from '../../../domain';

export class SqliteSessionRepository implements SessionRepository {
  constructor(private db: DrizzleDB) {}

  async findById(id: string, tx?: Transaction): Promise<Session | null> {
    const db = tx ?? this.db;
    const row = await db.select().from(sessions).where(eq(sessions.id, id)).limit(1).get();
    return row ? toEntity(row) : null;
  }
  async findAll(filter?: RepositoryQuery<Session>, opts?: FindOptions): Promise<Page<Session>> {
    let q = this.db.select().from(sessions).$dynamic();
    if (filter?.where) q = q.where(/* buildWhere(filter.where) */);
    if (filter?.orderBy) q = q.orderBy(/*...*/);
    if (filter?.limit) q = q.limit(filter.limit);
    if (filter?.offset) q = q.offset(filter.offset ?? 0);
    const items = await q.all();
    const total = await this.db.$count(sessions);
    return pageOf(items, total, filter);
  }
  async create(entity: Omit<Session, 'id'>, tx?: Transaction): Promise<Session> { ... }
  async update(id: string, patch: Partial<Session>, tx?: Transaction): Promise<Session> { ... }
  async delete(id: string, tx?: Transaction): Promise<void> { ... }
  async archive(id: string): Promise<void> { ... }
  async unarchive(id: string): Promise<void> { ... }
}
```

#### 2.8.6 Port 接口

```typescript
// packages/core/src/infra/db/ports/VectorIndexPort.ts
export interface VectorIndexPort {
  upsertVec(table: string, id: string, vec: Float32Array, meta?: object): Promise<void>;
  searchVec(table: string, query: Float32Array, topK: number): Promise<ScoredId[]>;
```

#### 2.8.7 与 CS 分离的协同

- 后端唯一持有 `RepositoryProvider` 实例 (Sqlite 或 RemoteHttp 实现)
- 前端/TUI/Web/CLI 通过 §4 JSON-RPC Service 调用 (Service 内部用 Repository)，没有 Repository 句柄
- 切换远程 DB: 仅实现 `RemoteHttpRepositoryProvider` (走 §4 HTTP Transport + SSE) 并替换工厂即可，业务与客户端零改动

## 3. 核心模块详细设计

本章详细设计 11 大顶层模块 (含 17 子模块),每个模块包含:TypeScript 接口定义、关键算法伪代码、数据流图、错误处理、配置项。模块映射见 §3.0。

### 3.0 模块映射表 (M20) 与 API Key Vault (M12)

> **M20 修改 (v4.2 新增)**: 落实 07 评估报告 §7.8,统一"11 大顶层模块 vs 17 子模块"口径。04 §8 定义 11 大 Harness 模块 (顶层),06 §3.1~§3.12 拆分为 17 子模块;本表给出映射关系。

#### 3.0.1 模块映射表 (11 顶层模块 → 17 子模块)

| # | 04 §8 顶层模块 (11) | 06 子模块 (17) | 06 章节 | v0.1 是否交付 | 备注 |
|---|---------------------|----------------|:-------:|:-------------:|------|
| 1 | Agent Loop Engine | LoopEngine | §3.1 | ✅ | PAOR 主循环 + 5 子循环 |
| 2 | (L6 子引擎) | PromptEngine | §3.2 | ✅ | L6 子引擎,非 11 模块 |
| 3 | Context | ContextEngine | §3.3 | ⏸ v0.2 (P1) | 代码索引依赖 |
| 4 | Tool Use Layer | ToolRegistry + ToolDispatcher | §3.4 | ✅ | 拆为注册 + 调度 2 子模块 |
| 5 | Permission | PermissionGateway | §3.5 | ✅ | L0-L3 风险分级 + 审批 |
| 6 | Memory | MemorySystem | §3.6 | ⏸ v0.2 (基础) | v0.1 仅写入门槛,向量检索 v0.2 |
| 7 | MCP | MCPClient | §3.7 | ✅ (基础) | v0.1 仅 stdio + 内置 server |
| 8 | Hooks | HooksRuntime | §3.8 | ⏸ v0.2 | 13+ Hook 点 |
| 9 | Skills | SkillsRuntime | §3.9 | ⏸ v0.2 | Skill 执行引擎 |
| 10 | Plugin | PluginSandbox | §3.10 | ⏸ v0.2 | isolated-vm 沙箱 |
| 11 | Subagent | SubagentManager | §3.11 | ⏸ v0.3 | 派生 + 上下文隔离 |
| 12 | Multi-agent | MultiagentCoordinator | §3.12 | ⏸ v0.3 | 主从/并行/专家 |
| — | (横切) | API Key Vault | §3.0.2 (本节) | ✅ | 横切模块,非 11 之列 |
| — | (横切) | AuditLogger | §3.5 内 | ✅ | 由 PermissionGateway 调用 |
| — | (横切) | EventBus | §3.13 | ✅ | 前后端事件桥接 |

> **口径统一**: "11 大顶层模块"是 04 §8 的架构视图;"17 子模块"是 06 §3 的实现视图 (含 PromptEngine 子引擎 + 3 个横切模块)。本文档后续出现"模块"未加修饰时,默认指 06 子模块;出现"顶层模块"时指 04 §8。

> **v0.1 交付子模块 (5 个)**: LoopEngine + PromptEngine + ToolRegistry/ToolDispatcher + PermissionGateway + MCPClient (基础) + API Key Vault + AuditLogger + EventBus = 共 8 子模块 (含 3 横切),覆盖 4 大顶层模块 (Loop/Tool/MCP/Permission)。

#### 3.0.2 API Key Vault (M12)

> **M12 修改 (v4.2 新增)**: 落实 07 评估报告 §5.3,设计独立的 API Key Vault 横切模块,负责 LLM Provider 密钥 (OpenAI / Anthropic / 本地模型) 与第三方服务密钥 (GitHub / Linear / Slack 等) 的加密存储、运行时解密、轮换与导出。

##### 设计目标

1. **静态加密**: 密钥在 SQLite 中以密文存储,即使数据库文件被复制离机也无法解密。
2. **运行时安全**: 解密后的密钥仅存在于后端进程内存,经 `MemorySystem` 限制不写入记忆;前端永不接触明文。
3. **多客户端安全**: Profile 同步事件 (`config.changed`) 不携带密钥明文,仅同步脱敏标识。
4. **可轮换**: 支持密钥轮换 (re-encrypt with new master key) 而不改动用户配置。
5. **可导出**: 支持加密包导出 (用户迁移设备),导出包使用用户口令派生密钥。

##### 加密算法与密钥派生

```typescript
// packages/core/src/vault/crypto.ts
import crypto from 'node:crypto';

/**
 * Master Key 派生: 优先从 OS keychain (macOS Keychain / Windows DPAPI / Linux Secret Service)
 * 读取 32 字节随机密钥;若 OS keychain 不可用 (如 CI),回退到用户口令 + Argon2id 派生。
 */
export async function deriveMasterKey(opts: {
  keychainKey?: string;       // 'openwork.master' (优先)
  userPassphrase?: string;    // 回退口令
  salt?: Buffer;              // 首次启动随机生成并存储于 ~/.openwork/salt.bin
}): Promise<Buffer> {
  if (opts.keychainKey) {
    const key = await readFromKeychain(opts.keychainKey);
    if (key) return Buffer.from(key, 'base64');
  }
  if (!opts.userPassphrase) {
    throw new Error('Master key unavailable: no keychain entry and no passphrase');
  }
  // Argon2id 派生 (m=64MB, t=3, p=4)
  const argon2 = await import('argon2');
  return argon2.hash(opts.userPassphrase, {
    type: argon2.argon2id,
    salt: opts.salt!,
    raw: true,
    hashLength: 32,
    memoryCost: 65536,
    timeCost: 3,
    parallelism: 4,
  });
}

/** AES-256-GCM 加密 (认证加密,防篡改) */
export function encrypt(plaintext: string, masterKey: Buffer): {
  ciphertext: string;  // base64
  iv: string;          // base64, 12 字节
  tag: string;         // base64, 16 字节
  alg: 'AES-256-GCM';
} {
  const iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm', masterKey, iv);
  const enc = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, 'utf8'), cipher.final()]);
  const tag = cipher.getAuthTag();
  return {
    ciphertext: enc.toString('base64'),
    iv: iv.toString('base64'),
    tag: tag.toString('base64'),
    alg: 'AES-256-GCM',
  };
}

export function decrypt(payload: { ciphertext: string; iv: string; tag: string }, masterKey: Buffer): string {
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(
    'aes-256-gcm', masterKey, Buffer.from(payload.iv, 'base64')
  );
  decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(payload.tag, 'base64'));
  return Buffer.concat([
    decipher.update(Buffer.from(payload.ciphertext, 'base64')),
    decipher.final(),
  ]).toString('utf8');
}
```

##### Vault 接口

```typescript
// packages/core/src/vault/vault.ts
export interface SecretEntry {
  id: string;                 // nanoid
  provider: string;           // 'openai' | 'anthropic' | 'github' | 'linear' | ...
  label: string;              // 用户可读标识,如 'OpenAI Production'
  ciphertext: string;         // AES-256-GCM 密文
  iv: string;
  tag: string;
  keyHint?: string;           // 脱敏标识,如 'sk-...abc' (前缀 + 后 3 位)
  metadata?: Record<string, string>;  // 非敏感元数据 (org_id 等)
  createdAt: number;
  rotatedAt?: number;
  expiresAt?: number;         // 可选过期时间,触发轮换提醒
}

export interface ApiKeyVault {
  /** 存储密钥 (经 master key 加密后写 SQLite `secrets` 表,v0.2 建表) */
  store(provider: string, label: string, plaintext: string, opts?: { expiresAt?: number }): Promise<string>;
  /** 运行时解密并缓存 (TTL 5 分钟,LRU 上限 8 条) */
  retrieve(id: string): Promise<string>;
  /** 按 provider + label 查询 (供 LLM 调用前选择密钥) */
  listByProvider(provider: string): Promise<Array<{ id: string; label: string; keyHint?: string }>>;
  /** 轮换: 用新 master key 重新加密所有密钥 */
  rotateMasterKey(newMasterKey: Buffer): Promise<{ rotated: number; failed: string[] }>;
  /** 删除密钥 (从 SQLite 移除,不可恢复) */
  revoke(id: string): Promise<void>;
  /** 导出加密包 (用用户口令派生密钥加密所有密钥) */
  exportArchive(passphrase: string): Promise<{ blob: Buffer; version: 1; createdAt: number }>;
  /** 导入加密包 */
  importArchive(blob: Buffer, passphrase: string): Promise<{ imported: number; skipped: string[] }>;
}
```

##### 运行时解密缓存

```typescript
// packages/core/src/vault/cache.ts
export class VaultCache {
  private cache = new Map<string, { value: string; expiresAt: number }>();
  private readonly ttlMs = 5 * 60 * 1000;  // 5 分钟
  private readonly maxSize = 8;             // LRU 上限

  get(id: string): string | undefined {
    const entry = this.cache.get(id);
    if (!entry) return undefined;
    if (Date.now() > entry.expiresAt) {
      this.cache.delete(id);
      return undefined;
    }
    // LRU: 重新插入到末尾
    this.cache.delete(id);
    this.cache.set(id, entry);
    return entry.value;
  }

  set(id: string, value: string): void {
    if (this.cache.size >= this.maxSize) {
      // 淘汰最久未用
      const oldestKey = this.cache.keys().next().value;
      if (oldestKey) this.cache.delete(oldestKey);
    }
    this.cache.set(id, { value, expiresAt: Date.now() + this.ttlMs });
  }

  /** 进程退出 / 锁屏时调用,清空内存中的明文密钥 */
  purge(): void {
    for (const [k, v] of this.cache) {
      // 覆盖内存,降低 swap 泄漏风险
      (v as any).value = '\x00'.repeat(v.value.length);
    }
    this.cache.clear();
  }
}
```

##### Profile 同步脱敏策略

| 字段 | Profile 同步事件 (`config.changed`) | 备注 |
|------|------------------------------------|------|
| `provider` | ✅ 明文 | 'openai' / 'anthropic' |
| `model` | ✅ 明文 | 'gpt-4o' / 'claude-3-5-sonnet' |
| `apiKey` | ❌ 不同步,仅同步 `secretId` (Vault 引用) | 前端展示 `keyHint` 脱敏标识 |
| `baseUrl` | ✅ 明文 | 自定义 endpoint |
| `params` | ✅ 明文 | temperature / max_tokens 等 |

```typescript
// 脱敏规则: 密钥明文 → 'sk-...abc' 形式 (前缀 2 字符 + ... + 后 3 字符)
export function maskApiKey(plaintext: string): string {
  if (plaintext.length < 8) return '***';
  return `${plaintext.slice(0, 2)}...${plaintext.slice(-3)}`;
}
```

##### 密钥轮换流程

1. 管理员触发 `vault.rotateMasterKey(newKey)` (CLI / 配置变更)。
2. Vault 读取所有 `SecretEntry`,逐条用旧 master key 解密 → 用新 master key 加密 → 更新 SQLite。
3. 全过程在单一事务内,失败回滚 (保留旧密文)。
4. 轮换成功后更新 OS keychain 中的 master key 条目。
5. 清空 `VaultCache`,后续请求用新 master key 解密。

##### 密钥导出加密包

```typescript
// 导出: 所有 SecretEntry 用用户口令派生密钥再加密为单个 blob
async exportArchive(passphrase: string): Promise<{ blob: Buffer; version: 1; createdAt: number }> {
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16);
  const archiveKey = await deriveMasterKey({ userPassphrase: passphrase, salt });
  const entries = await this.db.prepare('SELECT * FROM secrets').all();
  const payload = JSON.stringify(entries);
  const { ciphertext, iv, tag } = encrypt(payload, archiveKey);
  // 包格式: magic(4) | version(1) | salt(16) | iv(12) | tag(16) | ciphertext(N)
  const blob = Buffer.concat([
    Buffer.from('OCVT'), salt, Buffer.from(iv, 'base64'),
    Buffer.from(tag, 'base64'), Buffer.from(ciphertext, 'base64'),
  ]);
  return { blob, version: 1, createdAt: Date.now() };
}
```

##### 数据表 (v0.2 建表)

```sql
-- V0_2__vault.sql — API Key Vault 表 (M12)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS secrets (
    id           TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    provider     TEXT NOT NULL,
    label        TEXT NOT NULL,
    ciphertext   TEXT NOT NULL,            -- AES-256-GCM 密文 (base64)
    iv           TEXT NOT NULL,             -- 12 字节 IV (base64)
    tag          TEXT NOT NULL,             -- 16 字节认证标签 (base64)
    key_hint     TEXT,                      -- 脱敏标识 'sk-...abc'
    metadata     TEXT,                      -- JSON 非敏感元数据
    created_at   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%s', 'now')),
    rotated_at   INTEGER,
    expires_at   INTEGER
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_secrets_provider ON secrets(provider);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_secrets_provider_label ON secrets(provider, label);
```

> **v0.1 简化**: v0.1 密钥暂存于 `configs` 表 (加密 JSON),无独立 `secrets` 表;v0.2 起 §2.6 Phase B 建 `secrets` 表并迁移。

---

### 3.1 LoopEngine (PAOR 主循环 + 5 子循环 + 中断/恢复 + HITL)

#### 3.1.1 核心类型定义

```typescript
// src/main/agent/types.ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { Message, ToolCall, ToolResult } from './message-types';

export const LoopPhaseSchema = z.enum([
  'perceive', 'act', 'observe', 'reflect',
  'plan', 'verify', 'recover'
]);
export type LoopPhase = z.infer<typeof LoopPhaseSchema>;

export const LoopStatusSchema = z.enum([
  'idle', 'running', 'paused', 'completed', 'failed', 'cancelled', 'awaiting_approval'
]);
export type LoopStatus = z.infer<typeof LoopStatusSchema>;

export const AgentModeSchema = z.enum(['ask', 'plan', 'build', 'review']);
export type AgentMode = z.infer<typeof AgentModeSchema>;

export const HITLModeSchema = z.enum(['every_step', 'key_nodes', 'trust']);
export type HITLMode = z.infer<typeof HITLModeSchema>;

export const TerminationReasonSchema = z.enum([
  'completed', 'user_stop', 'max_iterations', 'cost_exceeded',
  'error', 'cancelled', 'timeout', 'approval_denied'
]);
export type TerminationReason = z.infer<typeof TerminationReasonSchema>;

export interface LoopConfig {
  maxIterations: number;
  maxIterationsPerSubtask: number;
  reflectionInterval: number;
  planning: {
    autoPlanThreshold: number;
    requireUserApprovePlan: boolean;
  };
  verification: {
    autoRunTests: boolean;
    autoLint: boolean;
    autoTypecheck: boolean;
  };
  recovery: {
    maxRetries: number;
    onPersistentFailure: 'ask' | 'abort' | 'escalate';
  };
  hitl: {
    mode: HITLMode;
    confirmOn: string[];
  };
  subAgent: {
    enabled: boolean;
    maxParallel: number;
    contextIsolation: boolean;
  };
  checkpoint: {
    enabled: boolean;
    interval: 'every_step' | 'every_n' | 'on_demand';
    n?: number;
  };
  trace: {
    enabled: boolean;
    captureObservations: boolean;
  };
}

export interface LoopState {
  sessionId: string;
  status: LoopStatus;
  iteration: number;
  currentStep: number;
  currentPhase: LoopPhase;
  mode: AgentMode;
  messageHistory: Message[];
  workingMemory: Map<string, unknown>;
  taskPlan?: TaskPlan;
  tokenUsage: TokenUsage;
  costUsd: number;
  activeSubAgents: string[];
  pendingToolCalls: PendingToolCall[];
  lastError?: AgentError;
  lastCheckpointId?: string;
  pendingApprovalId?: string;
  cancellationRequested: boolean;
  pauseRequested: boolean;
}

export interface TokenUsage {
  inputTokens: number;
  outputTokens: number;
  cacheReadTokens: number;
  cacheWriteTokens: number;
  reasoningTokens: number;
}

export interface TaskPlan {
  id: string;
  steps: TaskStep[];
  dependencies: Array<[string, string]>;  // DAG edges
  createdAt: number;
  approvedByUser?: boolean;
}

export interface TaskStep {
  id: string;
  description: string;
  status: 'pending' | 'in_progress' | 'completed' | 'failed' | 'skipped';
  assignedTo?: string;  // sub_agent instance_id
  result?: unknown;
  startedAt?: number;
  completedAt?: number;
}

export interface PendingToolCall {
  toolCallId: string;
  toolName: string;
  args: unknown;
  status: 'pending_approval' | 'ready_to_execute' | 'executing';
}

export interface AgentError {
  code: string;
  message: string;
  phase: LoopPhase;
  step: number;
  recoverable: boolean;
  context?: unknown;
}

export interface SessionResult {
  sessionId: string;
  status: LoopStatus;
  terminationReason?: TerminationReason;
  finalMessage?: Message;
  tokenUsage: TokenUsage;
  costUsd: number;
  stepsExecuted: number;
  durationMs: number;
}
```

#### 3.1.2 LoopEngine 类定义

```typescript
// src/main/agent/loop-engine.ts
import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';
import type { Database } from 'better-sqlite3';
import type { PromptEngine } from './prompt-engine';
import type { ContextEngine } from './context-engine';
import type { ModelRouter } from '../models/router';
import type { ToolRegistry, ToolDispatcher } from '../tools/registry';
import type { PermissionGateway } from '../permission/gateway';
import type { HooksRuntime } from '../hooks/runtime';
import type { SubagentManager } from './subagent-manager';
import type { MultiagentCoordinator } from './multiagent-coordinator';
import type { EventBus } from '../events/event-bus';
import type {
  LoopConfig, LoopState, LoopPhase, AgentMode,
  SessionResult, TerminationReason, TaskPlan
} from './types';

export interface LoopEngineDeps {
  db: Database;
  promptEngine: PromptEngine;
  contextEngine: ContextEngine;
  modelRouter: ModelRouter;
  toolRegistry: ToolRegistry;
  toolDispatcher: ToolDispatcher;
  permissionGateway: PermissionGateway;
  hooksRuntime: HooksRuntime;
  subagentManager: SubagentManager;
  multiagentCoordinator: MultiagentCoordinator;
  eventBus: EventBus;
}

export interface LoopStartOptions {
  sessionId: string;
  input: string;
  mode: AgentMode;
  resumeFromCheckpointId?: string;
  parentLoopStepId?: string;  // Sub-Agent 调用时
}

export class LoopEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private state: LoopState;
  private cancelRequested = false;
  private pauseRequested = false;

  constructor(
    private deps: LoopEngineDeps,
    private config: LoopConfig
  ) {
    super();
    this.state = this.createInitialState();
  }

  /**
   * 主循环入口 - PAOR
   *
   * 数据流:
   *   input → [Perceive: 组装上下文+Prompt]
   *         → [Act: LLM 推理+工具调用]
   *         → [Observe: 解析结果]
   *         → [Reflect: 自评+重规划] (条件)
   *         → 持久化 loop_step
   *         → 检查终止条件
   *         → 循环
   */
  async run(options: LoopStartOptions): Promise<SessionResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();

    try {
      // 1. 触发 on_session_start hooks
      await this.deps.hooksRuntime.trigger('on_session_start', {
        sessionId: options.sessionId,
        mode: options.mode,
      });

      // 2. 初始化或恢复状态
      if (options.resumeFromCheckpointId) {
        await this.restoreFromCheckpoint(options.resumeFromCheckpointId);
      } else {
        await this.initializeLoop(options);
      }

      // 3. 主循环
      while (true) {
        // 检查取消/暂停
        if (this.cancelRequested) {
          return await this.handleCancellation(startTime);
        }
        if (this.pauseRequested) {
          await this.createCheckpoint('user_pause');
          return this.buildResult(startTime, 'user_stop');
        }

        // 检查终止条件
        const termination = this.checkTermination();
        if (termination) {
          return await this.finalize(termination, startTime);
        }

        // 增加迭代
        this.state.iteration += 1;
        this.state.currentStep += 1;

        // === Phase 1: Perceive ===
        await this.emitPhase('perceive', 'start');
        const context = await this.perceive(options);
        await this.emitPhase('perceive', 'end');

        // === Phase 2: Act ===
        await this.emitPhase('act', 'start');
        const action = await this.act(context);
        await this.emitPhase('act', 'end');

        // === Phase 3: Observe ===
        await this.emitPhase('observe', 'start');
        const observation = await this.observe(action);
        await this.emitPhase('observe', 'end');

        // === Phase 4: Reflect (条件触发) ===
        if (this.state.iteration % this.config.reflectionInterval === 0) {
          await this.emitPhase('reflect', 'start');
          await this.reflect(observation);
          await this.emitPhase('reflect', 'end');
        }

        // === 子循环触发 ===
        await this.maybeRunSubLoops(observation);

        // 持久化 loop step
        await this.persistLoopStep(context, action, observation);

        // 自动检查点
        if (this.shouldCheckpoint()) {
          await this.createCheckpoint('auto');
        }

        // HITL 检查点
        if (this.shouldHITL()) {
          await this.requestHITL();
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      await this.handleLoopError(error);
      return this.buildResult(startTime, 'error');
    } finally {
      await this.deps.hooksRuntime.trigger('on_session_end', {
        sessionId: options.sessionId,
      });
    }
  }

  /** 请求取消 */
  cancel(): void {
    this.cancelRequested = true;
  }

  /** 请求暂停 */
  pause(): void {
    this.pauseRequested = true;
  }

  // ===== Phase 实现 =====

  /** Perceive: 组装上下文与 Prompt */
  private async perceive(options: LoopStartOptions): Promise<AssembledContext> {
    // 1. Context Engine 组装上下文
    const context = await this.deps.contextEngine.assemble({
      sessionId: options.sessionId,
      input: options.input,
      history: this.state.messageHistory,
    });

    // 2. Prompt Engine 组装 Prompt (七层洋葱)
    const prompt = await this.deps.promptEngine.assemble({
      context,
      history: this.state.messageHistory,
      mode: this.state.mode,
    });

    // 3. 构建完整 messages 数组
    const messages = this.buildMessages(prompt, context);

    // 4. 持久化 prompt 快照
    await this.savePromptSnapshot(prompt, messages);

    return { context, prompt, messages };
  }

  /** Act: LLM 推理 + 工具调用 */
  private async act(ctx: AssembledContext): Promise<ActionResult> {
    // 1. pre_model_call hooks (可拦截/修改)
    const messages = await this.deps.hooksRuntime.triggerIntercept(
      'pre_model_call', ctx.messages
    );

    // 2. 模型推理(流式)
    const response = await this.deps.modelRouter.chatStream({
      messages,
      tools: await this.getAvailableTools(),
      config: { mode: this.state.mode },
    });

    // 3. post_model_call hooks
    const processed = await this.deps.hooksRuntime.triggerIntercept(
      'post_model_call', response
    );

    // 4. 解析响应并执行
    return await this.parseAndExecute(processed);
  }

  /** Observe: 解析结果到结构化观察 */
  private async observe(action: ActionResult): Promise<Observation> {
    if (action.type === 'text') {
      return { type: 'text', content: action.text };
    }

    if (action.type === 'tool_results') {
      // 写入工作记忆
      for (const result of action.results) {
        this.state.workingMemory.set(
          `last_tool_result_${result.toolName}`,
          result.content
        );
      }
      return { type: 'tool_results', results: action.results };
    }

    return { type: 'mixed', text: action.text, results: action.results ?? [] };
  }

  /** Reflect: LLM 自评 + 重规划 */
  private async reflect(observation: Observation): Promise<void> {
    const reflectionPrompt = this.buildReflectionPrompt(observation);

    const reflection = await this.deps.modelRouter.chatSync({
      messages: [
        { role: 'system', content: reflectionPrompt },
        { role: 'user', content: '评估当前进度,是否需要调整?' }
      ],
      config: { maxTokens: 1024 }
    });

    // 判断是否需要重规划
    if (reflection.text.includes('调整计划') || reflection.text.includes('重规划')) {
      await this.triggerReplanning();
    }

    this.state.workingMemory.set('last_reflection', reflection.text);
  }

  // ===== 子循环 =====

  /** 触发子循环(Planning/Verification/Recovery) */
  private async maybeRunSubLoops(observation: Observation): Promise<void> {
    // Planning Loop: 复杂任务自动规划
    if (this.shouldPlan(observation)) {
      await this.runPlanningLoop();
    }

    // Verification Loop: 代码改动后验证
    if (this.shouldVerify(observation)) {
      await this.runVerificationLoop();
    }

    // Recovery Loop: 错误恢复
    if (this.state.lastError && this.state.lastError.recoverable) {
      await this.runRecoveryLoop();
    }
  }

  /** Planning 子循环 */
  private async runPlanningLoop(): Promise<TaskPlan> {
    const plan = await this.deps.promptEngine.generatePlan({
      input: this.state.messageHistory[0]?.content,
      context: this.state.workingMemory,
    });

    if (this.config.planning.requireUserApprovePlan) {
      const approved = await this.requestPlanApproval(plan);
      if (!approved) {
        this.cancelRequested = true;
      }
    }

    this.state.taskPlan = plan;
    return plan;
  }

  /** Verification 子循环 */
  private async runVerificationLoop(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.config.verification.autoRunTests) {
      await this.deps.toolDispatcher.execute('run_command', {
        command: this.detectTestCommand(),
        cwd: this.getProjectRoot(),
      });
    }
    if (this.config.verification.autoLint) {
      await this.deps.toolDispatcher.execute('run_command', {
        command: this.detectLintCommand(),
      });
    }
  }

  /** Recovery 子循环 */
  private async runRecoveryLoop(): Promise<void> {
    let retries = 0;
    while (retries < this.config.recovery.maxRetries) {
      try {
        await this.attemptRecovery(this.state.lastError!);
        this.state.lastError = undefined;
        return;
      } catch (e) {
        retries += 1;
        if (retries >= this.config.recovery.maxRetries) {
          if (this.config.recovery.onPersistentFailure === 'ask') {
            await this.requestUserIntervention(e as Error);
          } else if (this.config.recovery.onPersistentFailure === 'abort') {
            throw e;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ===== 终止与状态管理 =====

  private checkTermination(): TerminationReason | null {
    if (this.state.iteration >= this.config.maxIterations) {
      return 'max_iterations';
    }
    if (this.state.costUsd >= this.config.budgetLimit) {
      return 'cost_exceeded';
    }
    // 任务完成检测: LLM 输出无 tool_calls 且 finish_reason=stop
    if (this.state.lastFinishReason === 'stop' && !this.state.pendingToolCalls.length) {
      return 'completed';
    }
    return null;
  }

  private async finalize(reason: TerminationReason, startTime: number): Promise<SessionResult> {
    const result = this.buildResult(startTime, reason);
    // 更新 session 终态
    await this.deps.db.prepare(`
      UPDATE sessions SET status = ?, termination_reason = ?, termination_detail = ?,
            token_total_in = ?, token_total_out = ?, cost_total_usd = ?,
            step_count = ?, updated_at = strftime('%s','now')
      WHERE id = ?
    `).run(
      reason === 'completed' ? 'archived' : 'abandoned',
      reason,
      JSON.stringify({ lastError: this.state.lastError }),
      this.state.tokenUsage.inputTokens,
      this.state.tokenUsage.outputTokens,
      this.state.costUsd,
      this.state.currentStep,
      this.state.sessionId
    );
    return result;
  }
}

interface AssembledContext {
  context: unknown;
  prompt: unknown;
  messages: unknown[];
}

type ActionResult =
  | { type: 'text'; text: string }
  | { type: 'tool_results'; results: ToolResult[] }
  | { type: 'mixed'; text: string; results: ToolResult[] };

interface Observation {
  type: 'text' | 'tool_results' | 'mixed';
  content?: string;
  results?: ToolResult[];
}
```

#### 3.1.3 数据流图

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                         LoopEngine.run()                          │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  ┌─────────┐    ┌─────────┐    ┌─────────┐    ┌─────────┐       │
│  │Perceive │───▶│  Act    │───▶│ Observe │───▶│ Reflect │       │
│  │ 组装上下文│    │ LLM+工具 │    │ 解析结果 │    │  自评   │       │
│  └─────────┘    └────┬────┘    └─────────┘    └────┬────┘       │
│                      │                              │            │
│                      ▼                              ▼            │
│              ┌──────────────┐              ┌──────────────┐     │
│              │ HooksRuntime │              │  子循环触发   │     │
│              │  pre/post    │              │ Plan/Verify/ │     │
│              └──────────────┘              │ Recovery     │     │
│                                            └──────────────┘     │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    持久化 loop_step + checkpoint                   │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              检查终止条件 / HITL / 暂停 / 取消                    │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 3.2 PromptEngine (七层模型 + 模板 + 模型适配)

```typescript
// src/main/agent/prompt-engine.ts
import Handlebars from 'handlebars';
import { z } from 'zod';

export const PromptLayerSchema = z.enum([
  'L1_base_identity', 'L2_persona', 'L3_capabilities',
  'L4_constraints', 'L5_task_template', 'L6_dynamic_context', 'L7_user_override'
]);
export type PromptLayer = z.infer<typeof PromptLayerSchema>;

export interface LayerContent {
  layer: PromptLayer;
  content: string;
  source: 'builtin' | 'profile' | 'project' | 'user' | 'dynamic';
  canOverride: boolean;
  tokenCount: number;
}

export interface MergedPrompt {
  sections: LayerContent[];
  systemPrompt: string;
  totalTokens: number;
  layersUsed: PromptLayer[];
}

export interface PromptAssemblyContext {
  context: unknown;
  history: unknown[];
  mode: AgentMode;
  availableTools?: ToolMeta[];
  mcpTools?: Map<string, ToolMeta[]>;
  activeSkills?: Skill[];
  profile?: ProfileConfig;
  projectInfo?: ProjectInfo;
  gitInfo?: GitInfo;
  userOverrides?: string[];
  taskType?: TaskType;
}

export interface PromptEngine {
  assemble(ctx: PromptAssemblyContext): Promise<MergedPrompt>;
  generatePlan(input: unknown): Promise<TaskPlan>;
  renderTemplate(template: string, vars: Record<string, unknown>): string;
  getModelAdapter(): ModelAdapter;
}

export class PromptEngineImpl implements PromptEngine {
  private layerManager: LayerManager;
  private modelAdapter: ModelAdapter;
  private budgetAllocator: TokenBudgetAllocator;
  private templateRegistry: TemplateRegistry;

  constructor(private db: Database, private config: PromptConfig) {
    this.layerManager = new LayerManager(db, config);
    this.modelAdapter = new ModelAdapter();
    this.budgetAllocator = new TokenBudgetAllocator(config);
    this.templateRegistry = new TemplateRegistry(config.templateDir);
    this.registerHelpers();
  }

  async assemble(ctx: PromptAssemblyContext): Promise<MergedPrompt> {
    // 1. 计算各层 token 预算
    const budgets = this.budgetAllocator.allocate(ctx);

    // 2. 并行构建各层
    const layers = await Promise.all([
      this.layerManager.buildL1Base(ctx, budgets['L1_base_identity']),
      this.layerManager.buildL2Persona(ctx, budgets['L2_persona']),
      this.layerManager.buildL3Capabilities(ctx, budgets['L3_capabilities']),
      this.layerManager.buildL4Constraints(ctx, budgets['L4_constraints']),
      this.layerManager.buildL5TaskTemplate(ctx, budgets['L5_task_template']),
      this.layerManager.buildL6Dynamic(ctx, budgets['L6_dynamic_context']),
      this.layerManager.buildL7UserOverride(ctx, budgets['L7_user_override']),
    ]);

    // 3. 合并(后者覆盖前者, 不可覆盖层除外)
    const merged = this.layerManager.merge(layers);

    // 4. 模型适配
    const modelFamily = this.detectModelFamily(ctx);
    const adapted = this.modelAdapter.adapt(merged, modelFamily);

    return adapted;
  }

  async generatePlan(input: unknown): Promise<TaskPlan> {
    const template = await this.templateRegistry.get('plan_generation');
    const prompt = this.renderTemplate(template, { input });
    const response = await this.callLLM(prompt);
    return this.parsePlan(response);
  }

  renderTemplate(template: string, vars: Record<string, unknown>): string {
    const compiled = Handlebars.compile(template, { strict: true, noEscape: true });
    return compiled(vars);
  }

  getModelAdapter(): ModelAdapter {
    return this.modelAdapter;
  }

  private registerHelpers(): void {
    Handlebars.registerHelper('json', (v: unknown) => JSON.stringify(v, null, 2));
    Handlebars.registerHelper('list', (arr: unknown[]) => arr.map((v, i) => `${i + 1}. ${v}`).join('\n'));
    Handlebars.registerHelper('truncate', (s: string, n: number) => s.slice(0, n));
  }
}
```

#### 3.2.1 Token 预算分配算法

```typescript
export class TokenBudgetAllocator {
  constructor(private config: PromptConfig) {}

  allocate(ctx: PromptAssemblyContext): Record<PromptLayer, number> {
    const window = this.getModelWindow(ctx);
    const available = window
      - this.config.outputReserve      // 16000
      - this.config.toolReserve        // 8000
      - this.config.dynamicBuffer;     // 21800

    // 固定优先级(不可压缩)
    const budget: Record<PromptLayer, number> = {
      L1_base_identity: Math.min(500, available),
      L2_persona: Math.min(300, available),
      L7_user_override: Math.min(ctx.userOverrides?.join('').length ?? 0, 5000),
    };

    const fixedUsed = Object.values(budget).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const remaining = available - fixedUsed;

    // 按比例分配弹性层
    budget.L6_dynamic_context = Math.floor(remaining * 0.70);
    budget.L3_capabilities = Math.floor(remaining * 0.15);
    budget.L4_constraints = Math.floor(remaining * 0.10);
    budget.L5_task_template = Math.floor(remaining * 0.05);

    return budget;
  }
}
```

### 3.3 ContextEngine (10 类来源 + 压缩 + Hybrid RAG + 窗口)

```typescript
// src/main/agent/context-engine.ts
export interface ContextItem {
  source: ContextSourceType;
  content: string;
  weight: number;
  tokens: number;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
  isPinned: boolean;
  id: string;
}

export type ContextSourceType =
  | 'explicit' | 'file' | 'repo_index' | 'git' | 'terminal'
  | 'editor' | 'browser' | 'project_config' | 'session_history' | 'memory';

export interface AssembledContext {
  items: ContextItem[];
  totalTokens: number;
  budget: TokenBudget;
  compressed: boolean;
  evictedItems: ContextItem[];
  facts: Fact[];
  summaries: string[];
}

export interface ContextEngine {
  assemble(input: ContextAssemblyInput): Promise<AssembledContext>;
  pinItem(id: string): void;
  unpinItem(id: string): void;
  getEvictedItems(): ContextItem[];
}

export class ContextEngineImpl implements ContextEngine {
  private collectors: ContextCollector[];
  private retriever: HybridRetriever;
  private compressor: ContextCompressor;
  private memorySystem: MemorySystem;

  constructor(deps: ContextEngineDeps) {
    this.collectors = [
      new ExplicitContextCollector(deps),
      new FileContextCollector(deps),
      new RepoIndexContextCollector(deps),
      new GitContextCollector(deps),
      new TerminalContextCollector(deps),
      new EditorContextCollector(deps),
      new BrowserContextCollector(deps),
      new ProjectConfigCollector(deps),
      new SessionHistoryCollector(deps),
      new MemoryContextCollector(deps),
    ];
    this.retriever = new HybridRetrieverImpl(deps);
    this.compressor = new ContextCompressorImpl(deps.modelRouter);
    this.memorySystem = deps.memorySystem;
  }

  async assemble(input: ContextAssemblyInput): Promise<AssembledContext> {
    let allItems: ContextItem[] = [];

    // 1. 从所有采集器并行收集
    const enabledCollectors = this.collectors.filter(c => c.isEnabled());
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
      enabledCollectors.map(c => c.collect(input))
    );

    for (const r of results) {
      if (r.status === 'fulfilled') {
        allItems.push(...r.value);
      }
    }

    // 2. Hybrid RAG 检索
    const retrieved = await this.retriever.search(input.query, 10);
    allItems.push(...retrieved);

    // 3. 记忆检索
    const memories = await this.memorySystem.retrieveRelevant(input.query, 5);
    allItems.push(...memories);

    // 4. 计算权重并排序
    allItems.sort((a, b) => b.weight - a.weight);

    // 5. 应用 token 预算(必要时压缩)
    const budget = this.calculateBudget(input);
    const compressed = await this.compressor.compress(allItems, budget, input.strategy);

    return compressed;
  }
}
```

### 3.4 ToolRegistry + ToolDispatcher (18+ 工具 + 注册/调度/沙箱)

```typescript
// src/main/tools/registry.ts
export interface ToolMeta {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  parameters: JSONSchema;       // JSON Schema
  riskLevel: 'L0' | 'L1' | 'L2' | 'L3';
  requiresApproval: boolean;
  defaultTimeoutMs: number;
  isStreaming: boolean;
  category: ToolCategory;
  source: ToolSource;
  sourceId?: string;
  version?: string;
}

export type ToolCategory =
  | 'file' | 'shell' | 'search' | 'git' | 'browser' | 'web'
  | 'memory' | 'todo' | 'task' | 'mcp' | 'skill' | 'editor' | 'system';

export interface ToolSource {
  type: 'builtin' | 'mcp' | 'plugin' | 'skill' | 'custom' | 'sub_agent';
  id?: string;
}

export interface ToolContext {
  sessionId: string;
  projectId: string;
  workingDir: string;
  sandbox: Sandbox;
  permissions: string[];
  worker?: Worker;
}

export interface ToolResult {
  toolId: string;
  toolName: string;
  success: boolean;
  content: unknown;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
  errorCode?: string;
  errorMessage?: string;
  durationMs: number;
}

export interface Tool {
  meta: ToolMeta;
  invoke(args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult>;
  validateArgs(args: unknown): Promise<ValidationResult>;
}

export interface ToolRegistry {
  register(tool: Tool): Promise<void>;
  unregister(id: string): Promise<void>;
  get(id: string): Tool | undefined;
  getByName(name: string): Tool | undefined;
  list(): Tool[];
  listByCategory(category: ToolCategory): Tool[];
  listBySource(source: ToolSource['type']): Tool[];
  listNames(): string[];
  clear(): void;
}

export interface ToolDispatcher {
  execute(toolName: string, args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult>;
  executeBatch(calls: ToolCallRequest[], ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult[]>;
  executeStream(toolName: string, args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): AsyncGenerator<ToolEvent>;
}

export interface ToolCallRequest {
  id: string;
  toolName: string;
  args: unknown;
  timeoutMs?: number;
}

export class ToolRegistryImpl implements ToolRegistry {
  private tools = new Map<string, Tool>();
  private byName = new Map<string, Tool>();

  async register(tool: Tool): Promise<void> {
    this.tools.set(tool.meta.id, tool);
    this.byName.set(tool.meta.name, tool);
    // 持久化到 DB (用于回放)
  }

  async unregister(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const tool = this.tools.get(id);
    if (tool) {
      this.tools.delete(id);
      this.byName.delete(tool.meta.name);
    }
  }

  get(id: string): Tool | undefined { return this.tools.get(id); }
  getByName(name: string): Tool | undefined { return this.byName.get(name); }
  list(): Tool[] { return [...this.tools.values()]; }
  listByCategory(cat: ToolCategory): Tool[] {
    return this.list().filter(t => t.meta.category === cat);
  }
  listBySource(src: ToolSource['type']): Tool[] {
    return this.list().filter(t => t.meta.source.type === src);
  }
  listNames(): string[] { return [...this.byName.keys()]; }
  clear(): void { this.tools.clear(); this.byName.clear(); }
}

export class ToolDispatcherImpl implements ToolDispatcher {
  constructor(
    private registry: ToolRegistry,
    private permissionGateway: PermissionGateway,
    private sandbox: Sandbox,
    private auditLogger: AuditLogger,
    private hooksRuntime: HooksRuntime,
    private config: { concurrency: number; defaultTimeout: number }
  ) {}

  async execute(name: string, args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult> {
    const start = Date.now();
    // 1. 查找工具
    const tool = this.registry.getByName(name);
    if (!tool) {
      return this.fail(name, 'E-TOOL-001', `Tool '${name}' not found`, start);
    }

    // 2. 参数校验
    const validation = await tool.validateArgs(args);
    if (!validation.valid) {
      return this.fail(name, 'E-TOOL-002', validation.error!, start);
    }

    // 3. 权限检查
    const decision = await this.permissionGateway.check({
      toolName: name, args, ctx,
    });
    if (decision.decision === 'denied') {
      return this.fail(name, 'E-PERM-001', decision.reason, start);
    }
    if (decision.decision === 'requires_approval') {
      const approved = await this.requestApproval(name, args, decision.dryRun);
      if (!approved) {
        return this.fail(name, 'E-PERM-001', 'User denied', start);
      }
    }

    // 4. pre_act hooks
    const interceptedArgs = await this.hooksRuntime.triggerIntercept('pre_act', args);

    // 5. 沙箱执行
    let result: ToolResult;
    try {
      result = await this.sandbox.execute(
        () => tool.invoke(interceptedArgs, ctx),
        { timeoutMs: tool.meta.defaultTimeoutMs }
      );
    } catch (e) {
      result = this.fail(name, 'E-TOOL-004', (e as Error).message, start);
    }

    // 6. post_observe hooks
    result = await this.hooksRuntime.triggerIntercept('post_observe', result);

    // 7. 审计日志
    await this.auditLogger.log({
      action: 'tool_call',
      toolName: name,
      argsSummary: this.summarize(args),
      resultSummary: this.summarize(result.content),
      durationMs: Date.now() - start,
      status: result.success ? 'success' : 'error',
    });

    return result;
  }

  async executeBatch(calls: ToolCallRequest[], ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult[]> {
    // 并发执行独立工具调用(限制并发数)
    const semaphore = new Semaphore(this.config.concurrency);
    return Promise.all(calls.map(async call => {
      return semaphore.run(() => this.execute(call.toolName, call.args, ctx));
    }));
  }

  async *executeStream(name: string, args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): AsyncGenerator<ToolEvent> {
    // 流式工具执行(如长命令输出)
    const tool = this.registry.getByName(name);
    if (!tool || !tool.meta.isStreaming) {
      const result = await this.execute(name, args, ctx);
      yield { type: 'result', result };
      return;
    }
    // ... 流式实现
  }

  private fail(name: string, code: string, msg: string, start: number): ToolResult {
    return {
      toolId: '', toolName: name, success: false, content: null,
      errorCode: code, errorMessage: msg, durationMs: Date.now() - start,
    };
  }

  private async requestApproval(name: string, args: unknown, dryRun?: string): Promise<boolean> {
    // 创建 approval_request, 等待 UI 响应
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      // emit event, set timeout
    });
  }

  private summarize(v: unknown): string {
    const s = typeof v === 'string' ? v : JSON.stringify(v);
    return s.length > 200 ? s.slice(0, 200) + '...' : s;
  }
}
```

### 3.5 PermissionGateway (L0-L3 + 审批缓存 + dry-run)

```typescript
// src/main/permission/gateway.ts
export type RiskLevel = 'L0' | 'L1' | 'L2' | 'L3';
export type PermissionDecision =
  | { decision: 'allowed' }
  | { decision: 'denied'; reason: string }
  | { decision: 'requires_approval'; reason: string; dryRun?: string };

export type ApprovalLevel =
  | { type: 'once' }
  | { type: 'session'; sessionId: string }
  | { type: 'project'; projectId: string }
  | { type: 'permanent' };

export interface PermissionCheckInput {
  toolName: string;
  args: unknown;
  ctx: ToolContext;
}

export interface PermissionGateway {
  check(input: PermissionCheckInput): Promise<PermissionDecision>;
  recordApproval(toolName: string, level: ApprovalLevel): Promise<void>;
  clearApprovals(scope: 'session' | 'project' | 'all'): void;
  dryRun(toolName: string, args: unknown): Promise<string | undefined>;
}

export class PermissionGatewayImpl implements PermissionGateway {
  private approvalCache = new Map<string, ApprovalEntry>();
  private rules: PermissionRule[] = [];

  constructor(
    private db: Database,
    private auditLogger: AuditLogger,
    private config: PermissionConfig
  ) {
    this.loadRules();
  }

  async check(input: PermissionCheckInput): Promise<PermissionDecision> {
    const tool = await this.getToolMeta(input.toolName);
    if (!tool) {
      return { decision: 'denied', reason: `Tool '${input.toolName}' not found` };
    }

    // 1. 风险等级判断
    if (tool.riskLevel === 'L0') {
      return { decision: 'allowed' };
    }
    if (tool.riskLevel === 'L3') {
      // 永远需要确认 + 强制审计
      await this.auditLogger.logCritical({
        toolName: input.toolName, args: input.args, ctx: input.ctx,
      });
      const dryRun = await this.dryRun(input.toolName, input.args);
      return {
        decision: 'requires_approval',
        reason: '关键操作 (L3),需要人工确认',
        dryRun,
      };
    }

    // 2. 匹配权限规则 (优先级从高到低)
    for (const rule of this.rules.sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority)) {
      if (this.matchRule(rule, input)) {
        switch (rule.action) {
          case 'allow':
            return { decision: 'allowed' };
          case 'deny':
            return { decision: 'denied', reason: `Blocked by rule: ${rule.name}` };
          case 'ask':
          case 'ask_once':
            const dryRun = await this.dryRun(input.toolName, input.args);
            return {
              decision: 'requires_approval',
              reason: `Rule '${rule.name}' requires approval`,
              dryRun,
            };
          case 'dry_run':
            return {
              decision: 'requires_approval',
              reason: 'Dry-run preview',
              dryRun: await this.dryRun(input.toolName, input.args),
            };
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. 检查审批缓存
    const cached = this.approvalCache.get(input.toolName);
    if (cached && this.isCacheValid(cached, input.ctx)) {
      return { decision: 'allowed' };
    }

    // 4. 默认策略
    return {
      decision: 'requires_approval',
      reason: `Tool '${input.toolName}' requires approval (risk: ${tool.riskLevel})`,
      dryRun: await this.dryRun(input.toolName, input.args),
    };
  }

  async recordApproval(toolName: string, level: ApprovalLevel): Promise<void> {
    this.approvalCache.set(toolName, {
      toolName, level, grantedAt: Date.now(),
    });
  }

  async dryRun(toolName: string, args: unknown): Promise<string | undefined> {
    const a = args as Record<string, unknown>;
    switch (toolName) {
      case 'delete_file':
        return `Will delete file: ${a.path}`;
      case 'run_command':
        return `Will execute: ${a.command}`;
      case 'edit_file':
        return `Will edit file: ${a.path} (${(a.edits as unknown[])?.length ?? 0} edits)`;
      case 'write_file':
        return `Will write file: ${a.path}`;
      default:
        return undefined;
    }
  }

  private matchRule(rule: PermissionRule, input: PermissionCheckInput): boolean {
    // glob 匹配 + 条件表达式评估
    return minimatch(input.toolName, rule.targetPattern);
  }

  private isCacheValid(entry: ApprovalEntry, ctx: ToolContext): boolean {
    switch (entry.level.type) {
      case 'session': return entry.level.sessionId === ctx.sessionId;
      case 'project': return entry.level.projectId === ctx.projectId;
      case 'permanent': return true;
      case 'once': return false; // 一次性,用完即失效
    }
  }
}
```

#### 3.5.1 多客户端审批并发一致性 (M11)

> **M11 修改 (v4.2 新增)**: 落实 07 评估报告 §5.2,设计多客户端并发审批一致性机制。在服务式模式 + 多客户端场景 (Web + CLI + TUI 同时连接),同一破坏性操作触发 `tool.approval_needed` 后,可能出现多客户端同时响应的冲突。本机制采用**乐观锁 + 版本号**,保证审批决策的强一致性与可追溯性。

##### 一致性模型

| 维度 | 策略 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|
| 决策一致性 | **第一个提交者赢** | 第一个返回 `permission.decision` 的客户端成功,其余收到 `409 Conflict` |
| 并发控制 | **乐观锁 + 版本号** | 审批请求带 `version`,提交时校验,版本不匹配则拒绝 |
| 防重放 | **审批令牌 (approvalToken)** | 一次性令牌,提交后失效,不可重复使用 |
| 超时策略 | **默认拒绝** | 审批 TTL 默认 60s,超时后默认 `denied` (安全最小权限原则) |
| 审批可见性 | **广播 + 锁定** | `permission.requested` 广播到所有客户端;第一个响应到达后,广播 `permission.locked` 通知其他客户端"已锁定到客户端 X" |

##### 审批状态机

```
                    ┌──────────────┐
   tool.call(L3)    │   pending    │ ← 审批令牌 issued,version=1
   ────────────────>│              │
                    └──────┬───────┘
                           │
              ┌────────────┼────────────┐
              │            │            │
       client A        client B     client C
       decision         decision     decision
       (v=1, ok)        (v=1, ok)    (v=1, ok)
              │            │            │
              ▼            ▼            ▼
       ┌──────────────────────────────────┐
       │  第一个到达者 (A) 写入决策         │
       │  version=1 → version=2 (committed)│
       └──────────────┬───────────────────┘
                      │
                      ▼
              ┌──────────────┐
              │   resolved   │ ← 决策已应用,令牌失效
              │  (allowed /  │
              │   denied)    │
              └──────────────┘
              ▲           ▲           ▲
              │           │           │
       B 收到 409    C 收到 409    其他客户端收到 permission.resolved 通知
       (版本冲突)   (版本冲突)    (决策广播)
```

##### 类型定义

```typescript
// packages/core/src/permission/approval-consistency.ts

export interface ApprovalRequest {
  approvalToken: string;       // 一次性令牌,nanoid(21)
  toolCallId: string;          // 关联 tool_calls.id
  toolName: string;
  args: unknown;
  risk: RiskLevel;
  version: number;             // 乐观锁版本号,初始 1
  status: 'pending' | 'locked' | 'resolved' | 'expired';
  lockedToClientId?: string;   // 第一个响应者,锁定后其余客户端只读
  decision?: 'allowed' | 'denied';
  decidedBy?: string;          // client_id of decider
  decidedAt?: number;
  createdAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;           // createdAt + 60s
}

export interface DecisionSubmission {
  approvalToken: string;
  version: number;             // 客户端提交时携带的版本号
  decision: 'allowed' | 'denied';
  clientId: string;            // 提交者
  reason?: string;
}

export type DecisionResult =
  | { ok: true; approval: ApprovalRequest }
  | { ok: false; code: 409; reason: 'version_conflict' | 'already_resolved' | 'expired' | 'locked_to_other'; currentVersion: number; lockedTo?: string };
```

##### 提交流程 (乐观锁 + 版本号)

```typescript
export class ApprovalCoordinator {
  private pending = new Map<string, ApprovalRequest>();  // approvalToken → request

  /** 发起审批 (LoopEngine 调用) */
  async requestApproval(input: {
    toolCallId: string; toolName: string; args: unknown; risk: RiskLevel;
  }): Promise<ApprovalRequest> {
    const token = nanoid(21);
    const now = Date.now();
    const req: ApprovalRequest = {
      approvalToken: token,
      toolCallId: input.toolCallId,
      toolName: input.toolName,
      args: input.args,
      risk: input.risk,
      version: 1,
      status: 'pending',
      createdAt: now,
      expiresAt: now + 60_000,  // 60s TTL
    };
    this.pending.set(token, req);
    // 广播 permission.requested 到所有订阅客户端
    this.eventBus.emit('permission.requested', {
      approvalToken: token, tool: input.toolName, args: input.args, risk: input.risk,
      version: req.version, expiresAt: req.expiresAt,
    });
    // 启动超时定时器
    setTimeout(() => this.expire(token), 60_000);
    return req;
  }

  /** 客户端提交决策 (乐观锁校验) */
  async submitDecision(sub: DecisionSubmission): Promise<DecisionResult> {
    const req = this.pending.get(sub.approvalToken);
    if (!req) {
      return { ok: false, code: 409, reason: 'already_resolved', currentVersion: 0 };
    }
    // 1. 过期检查
    if (Date.now() > req.expiresAt) {
      this.pending.delete(sub.approvalToken);
      return { ok: false, code: 409, reason: 'expired', currentVersion: req.version };
    }
    // 2. 版本号校验 (乐观锁)
    if (sub.version !== req.version) {
      return {
        ok: false, code: 409,
        reason: req.status === 'locked' ? 'locked_to_other' : 'version_conflict',
        currentVersion: req.version, lockedTo: req.lockedToClientId,
      };
    }
    // 3. 第一个提交者:锁定并应用决策
    req.version += 1;
    req.status = 'resolved';
    req.lockedToClientId = sub.clientId;
    req.decision = sub.decision;
    req.decidedBy = sub.clientId;
    req.decidedAt = Date.now();
    // 持久化 (v0.2 写 permission_decisions 表;v0.1 仅内存)
    // 广播 permission.resolved 通知所有客户端
    this.eventBus.emit('permission.resolved', {
      approvalToken: sub.approvalToken,
      decision: sub.decision, decidedBy: sub.clientId, reason: sub.reason,
    });
    // 清理 (保留 30s 供迟到客户端查询,然后删除)
    setTimeout(() => this.pending.delete(sub.approvalToken), 30_000);
    return { ok: true, approval: req };
  }

  /** 超时处理:默认拒绝 */
  private expire(token: string): void {
    const req = this.pending.get(token);
    if (!req || req.status === 'resolved') return;
    req.status = 'expired';
    req.decision = 'denied';
    req.decidedBy = 'system';
    req.decidedAt = Date.now();
    this.eventBus.emit('permission.resolved', {
      approvalToken: token, decision: 'denied',
      decidedBy: 'system', reason: 'approval_timeout',
    });
    setTimeout(() => this.pending.delete(token), 30_000);
  }
}
```

##### JSON-RPC 错误响应 (409 Conflict)

```json
{
  "jsonrpc": "2.0",
  "id": "req-42",
  "error": {
    "code": -32050,
    "message": "Approval version conflict",
    "data": {
      "reason": "locked_to_other",
      "currentVersion": 2,
      "lockedTo": "client_web_01"
    }
  }
}
```

> **错误码 -32050** (新增): ApprovalConflict — 审批版本冲突,客户端应放弃重试,可订阅 `permission.resolved` 等待最终决策。

##### 客户端 UX 建议

- 收到 `permission.requested` 后,所有客户端展示审批对话框,显示 `version` 与 `expiresAt` 倒计时。
- 第一个提交者看到"决策已应用",其余客户端收到 409 后展示"已被客户端 X 决策为 allow/deny",并自动关闭对话框。
- 收到 `permission.resolved` 通知后,所有客户端同步关闭对话框并展示最终决策。

> **v0.1 简化**: v0.1 单客户端 (Desktop 嵌入式),无并发审批冲突,本机制向后兼容 (单客户端时第一个提交者必然成功)。v0.2+ 服务式 + 多客户端时本机制生效。
