### 3.6 MemorySystem (三级 + 分类 + 向量 + 整合)

```typescript
// src/main/memory/system.ts
export interface Memory {
  id: string;
  scope: 'work' | 'project' | 'global' | 'session';
  projectId?: string;
  sessionId?: string;
  category: 'decision' | 'fact' | 'preference' | 'lesson' | 'todo' | 'persona' | 'relationship' | 'pattern';
  key?: string;
  title?: string;
  content: string;
  contentFormat: 'markdown' | 'plain' | 'json' | 'yaml';
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
  tags?: string[];
  importance: number;       // 0-1
  confidence: number;       // 0-1
  accessCount: number;
  lastAccessedAt?: number;
  expiresAt?: number;
  isPinned: boolean;
  createdAt: number;
  updatedAt: number;
}

export interface MemorySystem {
  write(input: MemoryWriteInput): Promise<Memory>;
  read(id: string): Promise<Memory | null>;
  search(query: MemoryQuery): Promise<Memory[]>;
  retrieveRelevant(query: string, topK: number): Promise<Memory[]>;
  update(id: string, patch: Partial<Memory>): Promise<Memory>;
  delete(id: string): Promise<void>;
  consolidate(scope: 'project' | 'global', strategy?: ConsolidationStrategy): Promise<void>;
  decay(scope: 'project' | 'global'): Promise<void>;
}

export interface MemoryWriteInput {
  scope: Memory['scope'];
  projectId?: string;
  category: Memory['category'];
  content: string;
  title?: string;
  key?: string;
  importance?: number;
  tags?: string[];
  sourceSessionId?: string;
  sourceStepId?: string;
}

export interface MemoryQuery {
  scope?: Memory['scope'];
  projectId?: string;
  category?: Memory['category'];
  key?: string;
  tags?: string[];
  textSearch?: string;
  minImportance?: number;
  limit?: number;
  offset?: number;
}

export type ConsolidationStrategy =
  | 'summarize' | 'merge' | 'deduplicate' | 'promote' | 'decay' | 'archive';

export class MemorySystemImpl implements MemorySystem {
  constructor(
    private db: Database,
    private embeddingProvider: EmbeddingProvider,
    private modelRouter: ModelRouter,
    private config: MemoryConfig
  ) {}

  async write(input: MemoryWriteInput): Promise<Memory> {
    const id = nanoid();
    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    // 1. 写入 memories 表
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO memories (id, scope, project_id, session_id, category, key, title,
                            content, content_format, importance, source_session_id, source_step_id,
                            tags, created_at, updated_at)
      VALUES (@id, @scope, @projectId, @sessionId, @category, @key, @title,
              @content, 'markdown', @importance, @sourceSessionId, @sourceStepId,
              @tags, @now, @now)
    `).run({
      id, scope: input.scope,
      projectId: input.projectId ?? null,
      sessionId: null, category: input.category,
      key: input.key ?? null, title: input.title ?? null,
      content: input.content, importance: input.importance ?? 0.5,
      sourceSessionId: input.sourceSessionId ?? null,
      sourceStepId: input.sourceStepId ?? null,
      tags: JSON.stringify(input.tags ?? []),
      now,
    });

    // 2. 生成向量并写入 memory_vectors
    const embedding = await this.embeddingProvider.embed(input.content);
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO memory_vectors (id, memory_id, embedding, scope, project_id, category, importance, created_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(id, id, this.toBuffer(embedding), input.scope,
           input.projectId ?? null, input.category, input.importance ?? 0.5, now);

    // 3. 同步 FTS5
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO memories_fts (memory_id, scope, project_id, title, content, key, tags, category)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(id, input.scope, input.projectId ?? null,
           input.title ?? '', input.content, input.key ?? '',
           JSON.stringify(input.tags ?? []), input.category);

    return this.getById(id)!;
  }

  async retrieveRelevant(query: string, topK: number): Promise<Memory[]> {
    // Hybrid 检索: 向量 + FTS5 + RRF 融合
    const queryVec = await this.embeddingProvider.embed(query);

    const vectorResults = this.db.prepare(`
      SELECT memory_id, distance FROM memory_vectors
      ORDER BY embedding <-> ?
      LIMIT ?
    `).all(this.toBuffer(queryVec), topK * 2) as Array<{ memory_id: string; distance: number }>;

    const ftsResults = this.db.prepare(`
      SELECT memory_id, rank FROM memories_fts
      WHERE memories_fts MATCH ?
      ORDER BY rank LIMIT ?
    `).all(query, topK * 2) as Array<{ memory_id: string; rank: number }>;

    // RRF 融合
    const fused = this.rrfFuse(vectorResults.map(r => r.memory_id),
                                ftsResults.map(r => r.memory_id), topK);

    // 加载完整记忆
    const memories: Memory[] = [];
    for (const memoryId of fused) {
      const m = await this.read(memoryId);
      if (m) {
        // 更新访问计数
        this.db.prepare(`
          UPDATE memories SET access_count = access_count + 1, last_accessed_at = ?
          WHERE id = ?
        `).run(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), memoryId);
        memories.push(m);
      }
    }
    return memories;
  }

  async consolidate(scope: 'project' | 'global', strategy: ConsolidationStrategy = 'summarize'): Promise<void> {
    // 见 §5.7 记忆整合算法
  }

  private rrfFuse(listA: string[], listB: string[], topK: number): string[] {
    const k = 60;
    const scores = new Map<string, number>();
    listA.forEach((id, rank) => {
      scores.set(id, (scores.get(id) ?? 0) + 1 / (k + rank + 1));
    });
    listB.forEach((id, rank) => {
      scores.set(id, (scores.get(id) ?? 0) + 1 / (k + rank + 1));
    });
    return [...scores.entries()]
      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
      .slice(0, topK)
      .map(([id]) => id);
  }

  private toBuffer(vec: number[]): Buffer {
    return Buffer.from(new Float32Array(vec).buffer);
  }
}
```

### 3.7 MCPClient (stdio/SSE/HTTP + Server 管理 + 工具合并)

```typescript
// src/main/mcp/client.ts
import { Client } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client';
import { StdioClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/stdio';
import { SSEClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/sse';
import { EventEmitter } from 'node:events';

export interface MCPServerConfig {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  transport: 'stdio' | 'sse' | 'http' | 'websocket';
  command?: string;        // stdio
  args?: string[];         // stdio
  env?: Record<string, string>;
  url?: string;            // sse/http/websocket
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  auth?: { type: 'bearer' | 'basic' | 'none'; token?: string; tokenEnv?: string };
  enabled: boolean;
  autoRestart: boolean;
  requestTimeoutSecs: number;
}

export interface MCPServerStatus {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  status: 'connected' | 'disconnected' | 'connecting' | 'error' | 'restarting';
  toolsCount: number;
  resourcesCount: number;
  promptsCount: number;
  lastError?: string;
  lastConnectedAt?: number;
}

export class MCPClient extends EventEmitter {
  private connections = new Map<string, Client>();
  private statuses = new Map<string, MCPServerStatus>();
  private restartCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  private healthCheckTimers = new Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout>();

  constructor(
    private toolRegistry: ToolRegistry,
    private db: Database
  ) { super(); }

  /** 启动所有已启用的 MCP Server */
  async startAll(): Promise<MCPServerStatus[]> {
    const servers = this.db.prepare(
      'SELECT * FROM mcp_servers WHERE enabled = 1'
    ).all() as Array<any>;

    return Promise.all(servers.map(s => this.startServer(s.id, s)));
  }

  async startServer(id: string, config: any): Promise<MCPServerStatus> {
    this.updateStatus(id, { status: 'connecting' });

    try {
      const transport = this.createTransport(config);
      const client = new Client({
        name: 'OpenWork-agent-desktop',
        version: '4.0.0',
      }, { capabilities: {} });

      await client.connect(transport);
      this.connections.set(id, client);

      // 发现工具/资源/Prompts
      const tools = await client.listTools();
      const resources = await client.listResources();
      const prompts = await client.listPrompts();

      // 注册工具到 ToolRegistry
      for (const tool of tools.tools) {
        const adapted = new MCPToolAdapter(id, config.name, tool, client);
        await this.toolRegistry.register(adapted);
      }

      // 持久化状态
      this.db.prepare(`
        UPDATE mcp_servers SET status = 'connected', last_connected_at = ?,
              tools_count = ?, resources_count = ?, prompts_count = ?,
              tools_cache = ?, server_info = ?, updated_at = ?
        WHERE id = ?
      `).run(
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
        tools.tools.length, resources.resources.length, prompts.prompts.length,
        JSON.stringify(tools.tools), JSON.stringify({ name: config.name }),
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), id
      );

      const status: MCPServerStatus = {
        id, name: config.name, status: 'connected',
        toolsCount: tools.tools.length,
        resourcesCount: resources.resources.length,
        promptsCount: prompts.prompts.length,
        lastConnectedAt: Date.now(),
      };
      this.statuses.set(id, status);
      this.emit('server:connected', status);

      // 启动健康检查
      if (config.auto_restart) {
        this.startHealthCheck(id, config);
      }

      return status;
    } catch (e) {
      const status: MCPServerStatus = {
        id, name: config.name, status: 'error',
        toolsCount: 0, resourcesCount: 0, promptsCount: 0,
        lastError: (e as Error).message,
      };
      this.statuses.set(id, status);
      this.emit('server:error', status);
      return status;
    }
  }

  async stopServer(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const conn = this.connections.get(id);
    if (conn) {
      await conn.close();
      this.connections.delete(id);
    }
    const timer = this.healthCheckTimers.get(id);
    if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
    this.healthCheckTimers.delete(id);
    this.updateStatus(id, { status: 'disconnected' });
  }

  private createTransport(config: MCPServerConfig) {
    switch (config.transport) {
      case 'stdio':
        return new StdioClientTransport({
          command: config.command!,
          args: config.args ?? [],
          env: { ...process.env, ...config.env },
        });
      case 'sse':
        return new SSEClientTransport(new URL(config.url!), {
          requestInit: { headers: config.headers },
        });
      // 'http' / 'websocket' 类似
      default:
        throw new Error(`Unsupported transport: ${config.transport}`);
    }
  }

  private startHealthCheck(id: string, config: any): void {
    const interval = (config.health_check_interval_secs ?? 30) * 1000;
    const timer = setInterval(async () => {
      const conn = this.connections.get(id);
      if (!conn) return;
      try {
        await conn.ping();
        this.updateStatus(id, { status: 'connected', lastConnectedAt: Date.now() });
      } catch (e) {
        this.updateStatus(id, { status: 'error', lastError: (e as Error).message });
        const count = (this.restartCounts.get(id) ?? 0) + 1;
        this.restartCounts.set(id, count);
        if (count <= (config.max_restart_count ?? 3)) {
          this.updateStatus(id, { status: 'restarting' });
          await this.startServer(id, config);
        }
      }
    }, interval);
    this.healthCheckTimers.set(id, timer);
  }
}

/** MCP 工具适配器:将 MCP tool 包装为 Tool 接口 */
export class MCPToolAdapter implements Tool {
  meta: ToolMeta;
  constructor(
    private serverId: string,
    private serverName: string,
    private mcpTool: any,
    private client: Client
  ) {
    this.meta = {
      id: `mcp_${serverId}_${mcpTool.name}`,
      name: mcpTool.name,
      description: `[MCP:${serverName}] ${mcpTool.description ?? ''}`,
      parameters: mcpTool.inputSchema,
      riskLevel: 'L1',  // MCP 工具默认 L1
      requiresApproval: false,
      defaultTimeoutMs: 60000,
      isStreaming: false,
      category: 'mcp',
      source: { type: 'mcp', id: serverId },
      sourceId: serverId,
    };
  }

  async invoke(args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult> {
    const start = Date.now();
    try {
      const result = await this.client.callTool({
        name: this.mcpTool.name,
        arguments: args as Record<string, unknown>,
      });
      return {
        toolId: this.meta.id, toolName: this.meta.name,
        success: true, content: result, durationMs: Date.now() - start,
      };
    } catch (e) {
      return {
        toolId: this.meta.id, toolName: this.meta.name,
        success: false, content: null,
        errorCode: 'E-MCP-003', errorMessage: (e as Error).message,
        durationMs: Date.now() - start,
      };
    }
  }

  async validateArgs(args: unknown): Promise<ValidationResult> {
    // 使用 Zod 或 ajv 校验
    return { valid: true };
  }
}
```

### 3.8 HooksRuntime (13+ Hook 点 + 可拦截)

```typescript
// src/main/hooks/runtime.ts
export type HookTrigger =
  | 'on_session_start' | 'on_session_end'
  | 'pre_plan' | 'post_plan'
  | 'pre_act' | 'post_observe'
  | 'pre_model_call' | 'post_model_call'
  | 'on_tool_call' | 'on_tool_result'
  | 'on_file_change' | 'on_test_fail'
  | 'on_task_complete' | 'on_error'
  | 'on_memory_write' | 'on_sub_agent_spawn'
  | 'on_approval_request';

export interface HookConfig {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  trigger: HookTrigger;
  script: string;
  language: 'typescript' | 'javascript' | 'python' | 'shell';
  runtime: 'sandbox' | 'worker' | 'main' | 'external';
  enabled: boolean;
  timeoutSecs: number;
  onError: 'skip' | 'warn' | 'abort' | 'retry';
  retryCount: number;
  canIntercept: boolean;
  priority: number;
  asyncExecution: boolean;
}

export interface HookExecutionResult {
  modified: boolean;
  modifiedData?: unknown;
  abort: boolean;
  output?: unknown;
}

export interface HooksRuntime {
  trigger(event: HookTrigger, context: unknown): Promise<void>;
  triggerIntercept<T>(event: HookTrigger, data: T): Promise<T>;
  register(hook: HookConfig): Promise<void>;
  unregister(id: string): Promise<void>;
  list(): HookConfig[];
}

export class HooksRuntimeImpl implements HooksRuntime {
  private hooks: HookConfig[] = [];
  private sandbox: SandboxManager;

  constructor(
    private db: Database,
    sandboxManager: SandboxManager
  ) {
    this.sandbox = sandboxManager;
    this.loadHooks();
  }

  async trigger(event: HookTrigger, context: unknown): Promise<void> {
    const matching = this.hooks
      .filter(h => h.trigger === event && h.enabled && !h.canIntercept)
      .sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);

    for (const hook of matching) {
      if (hook.asyncExecution) {
        // 异步执行, 不等待
        this.executeHook(hook, context).catch(e => this.handleError(hook, e));
      } else {
        try {
          await this.executeHook(hook, context);
        } catch (e) {
          await this.handleError(hook, e);
          if (hook.onError === 'abort') {
            throw new Error(`Hook '${hook.name}' aborted: ${(e as Error).message}`);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  async triggerIntercept<T>(event: HookTrigger, data: T): Promise<T> {
    const matching = this.hooks
      .filter(h => h.trigger === event && h.enabled && h.canIntercept)
      .sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);

    let current = data;
    for (const hook of matching) {
      const result = await this.executeHook(hook, { input: current });
      if (result.abort) {
        throw new Error(`Hook '${hook.name}' intercepted and aborted`);
      }
      if (result.modified && result.modifiedData !== undefined) {
        current = result.modifiedData as T;
      }
    }
    return current;
  }

  private async executeHook(hook: HookConfig, input: unknown): Promise<HookExecutionResult> {
    const start = Date.now();
    const execId = nanoid();

    // 记录执行开始
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO hook_executions (id, hook_id, trigger, trigger_context, input_data, status, started_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'running', ?)
    `).run(execId, hook.id, hook.trigger, JSON.stringify(input).slice(0, 1000),
           JSON.stringify(input), Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));

    try {
      const output = await this.sandbox.executeScript({
        script: hook.script,
        language: hook.language,
        input,
        timeoutSecs: hook.timeoutSecs,
      });

      const result: HookExecutionResult = typeof output === 'object' && output !== null
        ? output as HookExecutionResult
        : { modified: false, abort: false, output };

      // 更新执行记录
      this.db.prepare(`
        UPDATE hook_executions SET status = 'success', output_data = ?, duration_ms = ?,
              completed_at = ? WHERE id = ?
      `).run(JSON.stringify(result), Date.now() - start, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), execId);

      return result;
    } catch (e) {
      // 失败也记录
      this.db.prepare(`
        UPDATE hook_executions SET status = 'failed', error_message = ?, duration_ms = ?,
              completed_at = ? WHERE id = ?
      `).run((e as Error).message, Date.now() - start, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), execId);
      throw e;
    }
  }

  private async handleError(hook: HookConfig, e: unknown): Promise<void> {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO hook_errors (id, hook_id, error_message, occurred_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(nanoid(), hook.id, (e as Error).message, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));

    if (hook.retryPolicy?.maxRetries) {
      for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= hook.retryPolicy.maxRetries; attempt++) {
        try {
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, hook.retryPolicy!.backoffMs * attempt));
          await this.executeHook(hook, {});
          return;
        } catch {
          /* 继续重试 */
        }
      }
    }
  }

  async registerHook(config: HookConfig): Promise<void> {
    // 校验脚本语法
    await this.sandbox.validateScript(config.script, config.language);
    this.hooks.set(config.id, config);
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO hooks (id, name, trigger, enabled, priority, can_intercept, config)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(config.id, config.name, config.trigger, config.enabled ? 1 : 0,
           config.priority, config.canIntercept ? 1 : 0, JSON.stringify(config));
  }

  async unregisterHook(id: string): Promise<void> {
    this.hooks.delete(id);
    this.db.prepare('DELETE FROM hooks WHERE id = ?').run(id);
  }
}
```

##### HooksRuntime 配置

```yaml
hooks:
  enabled: true
  default_timeout_secs: 30
  default_retry:
    max_retries: 2
    backoff_ms: 500
  sandbox:
    memory_limit_mb: 64
    cpu_timeout_ms: 5000
  builtin:
    - name: auto-format-on-save
      trigger: on_file_change
      script: ./hooks/auto-format.ts
    - name: cost-cap-alert
      trigger: post_model_call
      script: ./hooks/cost-alert.ts
```

### 3.9 SkillsRuntime (Skill 执行引擎)

```typescript
// src/main/skills/runtime.ts
export interface SkillManifest {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  version: string;
  description: string;
  /** 触发模式 */
  triggers: SkillTrigger[];
  /** 入口脚本 */
  entrypoint: string;
  /** 输入 schema (Zod) */
  inputSchema: unknown;
  /** 依赖的工具列表 */
  requiredTools?: string[];
  /** 权限声明 */
  permissions: SkillPermission[];
  /** 超时 (秒) */
  timeoutSecs?: number;
}

export type SkillTrigger =
  | { type: 'slash_command'; command: string; args?: string[] }
  | { type: 'intent'; intent: string; confidence: number }
  | { type: 'keyword'; pattern: string }
  | { type: 'scheduled'; cron: string }
  | { type: 'manual' };

export interface SkillPermission {
  resource: string;        // 'file:write' | 'shell:exec' | 'mcp:*'
  scope?: string;          // 路径 / 命令白名单
  riskLevel: 'L0' | 'L1' | 'L2' | 'L3';
}

export interface SkillExecutionRecord {
  id: string;
  skillId: string;
  sessionId: string;
  input: unknown;
  output: unknown;
  status: 'success' | 'failed' | 'timeout' | 'aborted';
  durationMs: number;
  costUsd?: number;
  error?: string;
  startedAt: number;
  completedAt?: number;
}

export class SkillsRuntimeImpl {
  private skills = new Map<string, SkillManifest>();
  private executions = new Map<string, AbortController>();

  constructor(
    private db: Database,
    private sandbox: PluginSandbox,
    private toolRegistry: ToolRegistry,
    private permission: PermissionGateway,
  ) {}

  async loadSkill(manifest: SkillManifest): Promise<void> {
    // 校验 manifest schema
    const valid = await this.validateManifest(manifest);
    if (!valid.ok) throw new Error(`Invalid manifest: ${valid.error}`);

    // 校验权限声明
    await this.permission.declareSkillPermissions(manifest.id, manifest.permissions);

    // 校验入口脚本可加载
    await this.sandbox.validateScript(manifest.entrypoint, 'typescript');

    this.skills.set(manifest.id, manifest);
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO skills (id, name, version, description, manifest, enabled, installed_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, ?)
    `).run(manifest.id, manifest.name, manifest.version, manifest.description,
           JSON.stringify(manifest), Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
  }

  async matchTriggers(input: { text: string; intent?: string }): Promise<SkillManifest[]> {
    const matched: SkillManifest[] = [];
    for (const skill of this.skills.values()) {
      for (const trigger of skill.triggers) {
        if (this.matchTrigger(trigger, input)) {
          matched.push(skill);
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    // 优先级: slash_command > intent > keyword
    return matched.sort((a, b) => this.triggerPriority(a) - this.triggerPriority(b));
  }

  private matchTrigger(trigger: SkillTrigger, input: { text: string; intent?: string }): boolean {
    switch (trigger.type) {
      case 'slash_command':
        return input.text.startsWith(`/${trigger.command}`);
      case 'intent':
        return input.intent === trigger.intent;
      case 'keyword':
        return new RegExp(trigger.pattern, 'i').test(input.text);
      default:
        return false;
    }
  }

  private triggerPriority(skill: SkillManifest): number {
    const order: Record<string, number> = {
      slash_command: 0, intent: 1, keyword: 2, scheduled: 3, manual: 4,
    };
    return Math.min(...skill.triggers.map(t => order[t.type] ?? 5));
  }

  async execute(
    skillId: string,
    input: unknown,
    ctx: SkillExecutionContext,
  ): Promise<SkillExecutionRecord> {
    const skill = this.skills.get(skillId);
    if (!skill) throw new Error(`Skill not found: ${skillId}`);

    const execId = nanoid();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    this.executions.set(execId, controller);

    const start = Date.now();
    const record: SkillExecutionRecord = {
      id: execId, skillId, sessionId: ctx.sessionId,
      input, output: null, status: 'success', durationMs: 0,
      startedAt: Math.floor(start / 1000),
    };

    // 权限预检
    for (const perm of skill.permissions) {
      const decision = await this.permission.check({
        actor: { type: 'skill', id: skillId },
        action: perm.resource,
        target: perm.scope ?? '*',
        riskLevel: perm.riskLevel,
      });
      if (!decision.allowed) {
        record.status = 'failed';
        record.error = `Permission denied: ${perm.resource}`;
        await this.persistRecord(record);
        return record;
      }
    }

    // 输入校验
    const inputValid = await this.validateInput(skill, input);
    if (!inputValid.ok) {
      record.status = 'failed';
      record.error = `Input validation failed: ${inputValid.error}`;
      await this.persistRecord(record);
      return record;
    }

    // 超时定时器
    const timer = setTimeout(
      () => controller.abort(),
      (skill.timeoutSecs ?? 60) * 1000,
    );

    try {
      const output = await this.sandbox.executeScript({
        script: skill.entrypoint,
        language: 'typescript',
        input: { input, ctx, signal: controller.signal },
        api: {
          callTool: (name: string, args: unknown) => this.toolRegistry.invoke(name, args, ctx),
          log: (level: string, msg: string) => ctx.logger[level]?.(msg),
          emit: (event: string, data: unknown) => ctx.eventBus.emit(event, data),
        },
        timeoutSecs: skill.timeoutSecs ?? 60,
      });
      record.output = output;
    } catch (e) {
      record.status = (e as Error).name === 'AbortError' ? 'timeout' : 'failed';
      record.error = (e as Error).message;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      this.executions.delete(execId);
    }

    record.durationMs = Date.now() - start;
    record.completedAt = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    await this.persistRecord(record);
    return record;
  }

  async abort(execId: string): Promise<void> {
    this.executions.get(execId)?.abort();
  }

  private async persistRecord(record: SkillExecutionRecord): Promise<void> {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO skill_executions (id, skill_id, session_id, input, output, status,
        duration_ms, cost_usd, error, started_at, completed_at)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(record.id, record.skillId, record.sessionId,
           JSON.stringify(record.input), JSON.stringify(record.output),
           record.status, record.durationMs, record.costUsd ?? null,
           record.error ?? null, record.startedAt, record.completedAt ?? null);
  }

  private async validateManifest(m: SkillManifest): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }> {
    if (!m.id || !m.name || !m.entrypoint) return { ok: false, error: 'missing required fields' };
    if (m.triggers.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: 'no triggers' };
    return { ok: true };
  }

  private async validateInput(skill: SkillManifest, input: unknown): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }> {
    // Zod schema 校验,此处省略
    return { ok: true };
  }
}
```

### 3.10 PluginSandbox (isolated-vm + manifest + 生命周期)

```typescript
// src/main/plugins/sandbox.ts
import ivm from 'isolated-vm';
import { z } from 'zod';

export const PluginManifestSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  version: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  author: z.string().optional(),
  main: z.string(),
  permissions: z.array(z.object({
    resource: z.string(),
    scope: z.string().optional(),
    riskLevel: z.enum(['L0', 'L1', 'L2', 'L3']),
  })),
  apiAccess: z.array(z.string()).default([]),   // 允许调用的 API
  memoryLimitMB: z.number().default(64),
  cpuTimeoutMs: z.number().default(5000),
});

export type PluginManifest = z.infer<typeof PluginManifestSchema>;

export interface ExecuteOptions {
  script: string;
  language: 'typescript' | 'javascript';
  input?: unknown;
  api?: Record<string, unknown>;
  timeoutSecs?: number;
  memoryLimitMB?: number;
}

export interface ExecuteResult {
  ok: boolean;
  output?: unknown;
  error?: string;
  durationMs: number;
  memoryUsedMB: number;
}

export class PluginSandboxImpl {
  private isolates = new Map<string, ivm.Isolate>();
  private contexts = new Map<string, ivm.Context>();

  async validateScript(script: string, _language: string): Promise<void> {
    // 简单语法检查 (TypeScript 编译为 JS 由 esbuild 处理)
    if (!script || script.trim().length === 0) {
      throw new Error('Empty script');
    }
  }

  async executeScript(opts: ExecuteOptions): Promise<unknown> {
    const start = Date.now();
    const isolate = new ivm.Isolate({
      memoryLimit: opts.memoryLimitMB ?? 64,
      onCatastrophicError: (err) => console.error('Isolate crash:', err),
    });
    const context = isolate.createContextSync();

    // 注入全局 API
    await this.injectApi(context, opts.api ?? {});

    // 编译 TypeScript → JS (esbuild)
    const jsCode = await this.compileTs(opts.script);

    // 注入 input
    if (opts.input !== undefined) {
      const inputRef = new ivm.ExternalCopy(opts.input);
      context.global.setSync('__input__', inputRef.copyInto());
    }

    // 执行
    const script = isolate.compileScriptSync(jsCode);
    const result = await script.run(context, {
      timeout: opts.timeoutSecs ?? 30,
      copy: true,
    });

    // 清理
    isolate.dispose();

    return result;
  }

  async loadPlugin(manifest: PluginManifest, code: string): Promise<string> {
    const valid = PluginManifestSchema.safeParse(manifest);
    if (!valid.success) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid manifest: ${valid.error.message}`);
    }

    const isolate = new ivm.Isolate({ memoryLimit: manifest.memoryLimitMB });
    const context = isolate.createContextSync();

    await this.injectApi(context, this.buildPluginApi(manifest));

    const pluginId = manifest.id;
    this.isolates.set(pluginId, isolate);
    this.contexts.set(pluginId, context);

    // 加载插件主代码
    const jsCode = await this.compileTs(code);
    const script = isolate.compileScriptSync(jsCode);
    await script.run(context, { timeout: 10 });

    return pluginId;
  }

  async callPluginMethod(pluginId: string, method: string, args: unknown[]): Promise<unknown> {
    const context = this.contexts.get(pluginId);
    if (!context) throw new Error(`Plugin not loaded: ${pluginId}`);

    const fnRef = await context.global.get(method);
    if (typeof fnRef !== 'function') {
      throw new Error(`Method not found: ${method}`);
    }

    const argsRef = new ivm.ExternalCopy(args);
    const result = await fnRef.apply(
      context.global,
      argsRef.copyInto(),
      { timeout: 30, copy: true },
    );
    argsRef.dispose();
    return result;
  }

  async unloadPlugin(pluginId: string): Promise<void> {
    const isolate = this.isolates.get(pluginId);
    if (isolate) {
      isolate.dispose();
      this.isolates.delete(pluginId);
      this.contexts.delete(pluginId);
    }
  }

  private async injectApi(context: ivm.Context, api: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(api)) {
      if (typeof value === 'function') {
        const fn = new ivm.Reference(value);
        context.global.setSync(name, fn.derefInto());
      } else {
        const copy = new ivm.ExternalCopy(value);
        context.global.setSync(name, copy.copyInto());
      }
    }
    // 注入 console.log 的受限版本
    context.evalSync(`
      globalThis.console = {
        log: (...args) => __log__('log', JSON.stringify(args)),
        error: (...args) => __log__('error', JSON.stringify(args)),
        warn: (...args) => __log__('warn', JSON.stringify(args)),
      };
    `);
    context.global.setSync('__log__', function(level: string, msg: string) {
      console[level === 'log' ? 'log' : level]?.(`[plugin] ${msg}`);
    });
  }

  private buildPluginApi(manifest: PluginManifest): Record<string, unknown> {
    const api: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const apiName of manifest.apiAccess) {
      switch (apiName) {
        case 'fs.read':
          api.fs_read = async (path: string) => { /* 受限 FS 读取 */ };
          break;
        case 'fs.write':
          api.fs_write = async (path: string, content: string) => { /* 受限 FS 写入 */ };
          break;
        case 'http.fetch':
          api.http_fetch = async (url: string, init?: unknown) => { /* 受限 HTTP */ };
          break;
        case 'tool.invoke':
          api.tool_invoke = async (name: string, args: unknown) => { /* 委托 ToolRegistry */ };
          break;
        case 'event.emit':
          api.event_emit = (event: string, data: unknown) => { /* 委托 EventBus */ };
          break;
      }
    }
    return api;
  }

  private async compileTs(code: string): Promise<string> {
    // 使用 esbuild 编译 TS → JS (production 简化版)
    const { transform } = await import('esbuild');
    const result = await transform(code, {
      loader: 'ts',
      target: 'es2022',
      format: 'cjs',
    });
    return result.code;
  }
}
```

#### 3.10.1 沙箱资源限制配置 (M13)

> **M13 修改 (v4.2 新增)**: 落实 07 评估报告 §5.4,定义插件沙箱的资源限制配置。isolated-vm 默认无 CPU/内存限制,插件代码可能死循环或分配大内存导致后端进程崩溃。本节给出可配置的资源上限与能力白名单。

##### 资源限制配置表

| 资源维度 | 配置项 | 默认值 | 上限 (硬限制) | 实现方式 | 失败行为 |
|----------|--------|:------:|:------------:|----------|----------|
| **CPU 时间片** | `cpuTimeoutMs` | 5000 | 30000 | `ivm.Isolate.compileScriptSync().run(timeout)` | 抛 `ScriptTimeoutError`,隔离区销毁 |
| **内存上限** | `memoryLimitMB` | 64 | 256 | `new ivm.Isolate({ memoryLimit })` | 触发 `onCatastrophicError`,隔离区销毁 |
| **调用超时** | `callTimeoutSecs` | 30 | 120 | `fnRef.apply({ timeout })` | 抛 `ScriptTimeoutError`,方法调用失败但隔离区保留 |
| **工具调用次数** | `maxToolCallsPerRun` | 10 | 50 | ToolDispatcher 计数器 | 抛 `ToolCallLimitExceeded`,插件方法返回错误 |
| **工具调用频率** | `maxToolCallsPerMinute` | 20 | 100 | 滑动窗口计数器 | 限流,等待 60s 重置 |
| **网络白名单** | `networkAllowList` | `[]` (禁用) | — | `http.fetch` API 在白名单外直接拒绝 | 抛 `NetworkAccessDenied` |
| **文件系统白名单** | `fsAllowList` | `[]` (禁用) | — | `fs.read`/`fs.write` 路径前缀校验 | 抛 `FsAccessDenied` |
| **事件发送频率** | `maxEventsPerSecond` | 10 | 50 | EventBus 限流器 | 丢弃超额事件,记 warning 日志 |
| **子隔离区数** | `maxIsolatesPerPlugin` | 1 | 3 | PluginSandbox 计数器 | 拒绝创建,抛 `IsolateLimitExceeded` |
| **总运行时长** | `maxRunDurationSecs` | 300 | 1800 | 全局定时器 | 强制销毁隔离区,插件标记 `unloaded` |

##### 配置示例 (PluginManifest 扩展)

```typescript
// §3.10 PluginManifestSchema 扩展 (M13)
export const PluginManifestSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  version: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  author: z.string().optional(),
  main: z.string(),
  permissions: z.array(z.object({
    resource: z.string(),
    scope: z.string().optional(),
    riskLevel: z.enum(['L0', 'L1', 'L2', 'L3']),
  })),
  apiAccess: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
  // ↓ M13 新增:资源限制
  resources: z.object({
    cpuTimeoutMs: z.number().int().positive().max(30000).default(5000),
    memoryLimitMB: z.number().int().positive().max(256).default(64),
    callTimeoutSecs: z.number().int().positive().max(120).default(30),
    maxToolCallsPerRun: z.number().int().positive().max(50).default(10),
    maxToolCallsPerMinute: z.number().int().positive().max(100).default(20),
    maxRunDurationSecs: z.number().int().positive().max(1800).default(300),
    maxEventsPerSecond: z.number().int().positive().max(50).default(10),
    maxIsolatesPerPlugin: z.number().int().positive().max(3).default(1),
  }).default({}),
  // ↓ M13 新增:能力白名单
  networkAllowList: z.array(z.string()).default([]),  // ['https://api.github.com/*']
  fsAllowList: z.array(z.string()).default([]),        // ['${workspaceRoot}/.openwork/plugins/${id}/']
});
```

##### 能力白名单与防泄漏

| Intrinsic | 是否暴露 | 风险 | 说明 |
|-----------|:--------:|------|------|
| `eval` | ❌ | 任意代码执行 | 不注入,插件代码经 esbuild 编译后无 eval |
| `Function` 构造器 | ❌ | 任意代码执行 | 不注入,`new Function()` 在沙箱内不可用 |
| `require` | ❌ | 加载原生模块 | 不注入,经 `apiAccess` 显式声明 API |
| `process` | ❌ | 环境变量 / 进程退出 | 不注入,经 `api.event_emit` 通知 |
| `globalThis` | ✅ (受限) | 沙箱全局对象 | 仅注入 `console` / `__input__` / 显式 API |
| `fetch` / `http` | ⚠ 条件 | 网络访问 | 仅 `apiAccess: ['http.fetch']` + `networkAllowList` 双重校验 |
| `fs` | ⚠ 条件 | 文件访问 | 仅 `apiAccess: ['fs.read'/'fs.write']` + `fsAllowList` 双重校验 |

> **能力泄漏防护**: isolated-vm 的 `ExternalCopy` 默认会深拷贝对象,但若 host 误传 `IntrinsicCopy` 暴露 `eval`/`Function`,插件可逃逸。PluginSandbox 严格使用 `ExternalCopy` 与 `Reference`,禁止 `IntrinsicCopy`。

##### 限流实现 (工具调用频率)

```typescript
// packages/core/src/plugin/rate-limiter.ts
export class PluginRateLimiter {
  private window = new Map<string, number[]>();  // pluginId → timestamps

  /** 检查是否允许调用,允许则记录,拒绝则抛错 */
  check(pluginId: string, maxPerMinute: number): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    const timestamps = (this.window.get(pluginId) ?? []).filter(t => now - t < 60_000);
    if (timestamps.length >= maxPerMinute) {
      throw new Error(`ToolCallLimitExceeded: plugin ${pluginId} exceeded ${maxPerMinute}/min`);
    }
    timestamps.push(now);
    this.window.set(pluginId, timestamps);
  }
}
```

#### 3.10.2 isolated-vm V8 双编译风险与回退方案 (S6)

> **S6 修改 (v4.2 新增)**: 落实 07 评估报告 §3.1,补充 isolated-vm 的 V8 版本耦合风险说明与回退方案。本节内容与 05 §12 (isolated-vm 风险小节) 配合,05 侧重风险评估,本节侧重工程实现与回退。

##### V8 版本耦合风险

isolated-vm 是 native addon,**必须**与宿主进程的 V8 版本严格匹配:

| 宿主进程 | V8 来源 | isolated-vm 编译产物 | 备注 |
|----------|---------|---------------------|------|
| **嵌入式模式** (Electron Main) | Electron 35 内置 V8 (12.x) | 需 `electron-rebuild` 编译,产物仅适用 Electron | 与 Node.js 22 LTS V8 不同 |
| **服务式 / 子进程式** (Node.js) | Node.js 22 LTS 内置 V8 (12.x) | 需 `node-gyp rebuild` 编译,产物仅适用 Node.js | 与 Electron V8 头文件不同 |
| **测试** (vitest node) | Node.js V8 | 同服务式 | CI 中跑测试用此产物 |

> **关键事实**: 同一份 `isolated-vm` 源码,在 Electron 模式与 Node.js 模式下需要**两份不同的编译产物**,不能互通。这要求 CI 同时构建两种产物,或在 `postinstall` 阶段根据宿主类型选择性编译。

##### 双编译 CI 配置

```yaml
# .github/workflows/build.yml (片段)
jobs:
  build-electron:
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest, macos-latest, windows-latest]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with: { node-version: '22' }
      - run: npm ci
      # Electron 模式:用 electron-rebuild 重编译 isolated-vm
      - run: npx electron-rebuild -f -w isolated-vm
      - run: npm run build:electron

  build-node:
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest, macos-latest, windows-latest]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with: { node-version: '22' }
      - run: npm ci
      # Node 模式:node-gyp 编译 isolated-vm (postinstall 自动)
      - run: npm run build:node
```

##### Node.js 升级风险

- Node.js 22 → 24 升级时,V8 大版本可能跨越 (如 12.x → 13.x),isolated-vm 需等上游发新版适配。
- 期间插件沙箱**不可用**,需触发回退方案 (见下)。
- 建议:Node.js 大版本升级前,先在 CI 验证 isolated-vm 是否已发布对应预编译二进制 (`prebuild-install` 支持)。

##### 回退方案: worker_threads + vm 模块

若 isolated-vm 编译失败 (Node.js 升级 / Electron 升级 / 平台不支持),自动回退到 `worker_threads` + Node.js 内置 `vm` 模块:

```typescript
// packages/core/src/plugin/sandbox-fallback.ts
import { Worker } from 'node:worker_threads';
import vm from 'node:vm';

export class FallbackPluginSandbox {
  readonly backend = 'worker_threads+vm' as const;

  async executeScript(opts: ExecuteOptions): Promise<unknown> {
    // 在 worker_threads 中创建 vm.Context
    const worker = new Worker(`
      const { parentPort } = require('worker_threads');
      const vm = require('vm');
      parentPort.on('message', ({ script, input, timeoutMs }) => {
        const ctx = vm.createContext({
          __input__: input,
          console: { log: (...a) => parentPort.postMessage({ kind: 'log', args: a }) },
        });
        try {
          const result = vm.runInContext(script, ctx, { timeout: timeoutMs / 1000 });
          parentPort.postMessage({ kind: 'result', result });
        } catch (e) {
          parentPort.postMessage({ kind: 'error', message: e.message });
        }
      });
    `, { eval: true });

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      worker.on('message', (msg) => {
        if (msg.kind === 'result') { resolve(msg.result); worker.terminate(); }
        else if (msg.kind === 'error') { reject(new Error(msg.message)); worker.terminate(); }
      });
      worker.postMessage({
        script: opts.script, input: opts.input,
        timeoutMs: opts.timeoutSecs ? opts.timeoutSecs * 1000 : 30000,
      });
    });
  }
}
```

##### 两种后端对比

| 维度 | isolated-vm (首选) | worker_threads + vm (回退) |
|------|:------------------:|:--------------------------:|
| 隔离强度 | 强 (独立 V8 Isolate) | 弱 (同进程 V8 Context) |
| 内存上限 | ✅ `memoryLimit` | ❌ 仅靠 worker 退出 |
| CPU 超时 | ✅ `timeout` | ✅ `vm.runInContext({ timeout })` |
| 能力泄漏防护 | ✅ `ExternalCopy` 强隔离 | ⚠ `vm.Context` 仍有 `process` 等逃逸路径 |
| 跨平台编译 | ❌ 需双编译 | ✅ Node.js 内置 |
| 性能 | 高 (无 worker 通信) | 中 (worker 启动 ~50ms) |
| 适用版本 | v0.2+ (插件系统启用时) | v0.2+ 回退 / v0.1 占位 |

##### 启动时检测与自动回退

```typescript
// packages/core/src/plugin/sandbox-factory.ts
export async function createPluginSandbox(): Promise<PluginSandboxImpl | FallbackPluginSandbox> {
  try {
    // 尝试加载 isolated-vm
    const ivm = await import('isolated-vm');
    // 探测性创建 Isolate,验证 ABI 兼容
    const probe = new ivm.Isolate({ memoryLimit: 1 });
    probe.dispose();
    return new PluginSandboxImpl();
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn(`[PluginSandbox] isolated-vm unavailable (${(e as Error).message}), falling back to worker_threads+vm`);
    return new FallbackPluginSandbox();
  }
}
```

> **决策记录**: v0.1 不启用插件系统 (§3.0 模块映射表标注 ⏸ v0.2),因此 isolated-vm 双编译问题在 v0.1 不暴露。v0.2 启用插件时,CI 必须同时构建 Electron 与 Node.js 两种 isolated-vm 产物;若 CI 失败,自动降级到 `worker_threads + vm` 后端,并在启动日志中 WARNING 标注"沙箱强度降级"。05 §12 提供 isolated-vm 风险评估,本节提供工程实现。

---

### 3.11 SubagentManager (派生 + 上下文隔离 + 成本归属)

> **设计目标**: 主 Agent 可在运行中派生 (spawn) 子 Agent,以承担可隔离、可并行、可计量的子任务。子 Agent 拥有独立 Loop、独立上下文窗口、独立工具白名单与独立成本预算,但共享父会话的审计链路与项目记忆。

#### 3.11.1 派生模型

```typescript
// src/main/agent/subagent/manager.ts
import { nanoid } from 'nanoid';
import type { LoopEngine } from '../loop/engine';
import type { ContextEngine } from '../context/engine';
import type { PermissionGateway } from '../permission/gateway';
import type { Database } from '../../infra/database';
import type { EventBus } from '../../infra/event-bus';

export type SubagentRole =
  | 'explorer'      // 代码库侦察 (read-only)
  | 'planner'       // 子任务规划
  | 'executor'      // 子任务执行
  | 'verifier'      // 验证 / 测试运行
  | 'critic'        // 反思 / 批判
  | 'expert'        // 专家角色 (由 Profile 定义)
  | 'custom';       // 自定义

export interface SubAgentConfig {
  /** 父 Agent ID */
  parentId: string;
  /** 角色 */
  role: SubagentRole;
  /** 派生时显式任务描述 (system prompt 注入) */
  taskPrompt: string;
  /** 上下文继承策略 */
  contextInherit: ContextInheritStrategy;
  /** 工具白名单 (省略则继承父级) */
  toolWhitelist?: string[];
  /** 工具黑名单 */
  toolBlacklist?: string[];
  /** 模型覆盖 */
  modelOverride?: {
    provider?: string;
    model?: string;
    maxTokens?: number;
  };
  /** 成本预算 (USD) */
  costBudget?: {
    hardCap: number;
    warningThreshold: number;   // 0-1
  };
  /** Token 预算 */
  tokenBudget?: {
    input: number;
    output: number;
  };
  /** 最大迭代次数 */
  maxIterations?: number;
  /** 超时 (秒) */
  timeoutSecs?: number;
  /** 是否允许派生孙 Agent */
  allowNesting?: boolean;
  /** 最大嵌套深度 */
  maxNestingDepth?: number;
  /** 完成后是否自动归档 */
  autoArchive?: boolean;
  /** 结果合并策略 */
  resultMergeStrategy?: 'append' | 'summarize' | 'replace' | 'discard';
}

export interface ContextInheritStrategy {
  /** 父级会话历史的继承比例 (0-1) */
  historyRatio: number;
  /** 继承的项目级上下文 (skills / memories / preferences) */
  inheritProjectContext: boolean;
  /** 继承的全局配置 */
  inheritGlobalConfig: boolean;
  /** 显式注入的上下文项 */
  injectedContext?: Array<{
    type: 'file' | 'selection' | 'memory' | 'git' | 'custom';
    content: string;
    pinned?: boolean;
  }>;
  /** 隔离字段 - 子 Agent 不可见的内容 */
  redactPatterns?: string[];
}

export interface SubAgentHandle {
  id: string;
  config: SubAgentConfig;
  status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'paused' | 'succeeded' | 'failed' | 'aborted' | 'timeout';
  startedAt: number;
  endedAt?: number;
  parentSessionId: string;
  childSessionId: string;
  /** 当前已花费 */
  spentUsd: number;
  /** 当前已用 token */
  usedTokens: { input: number; output: number };
  /** 当前迭代数 */
  iterations: number;
  /** 子 Agent 输出 (完成后填充) */
  output?: SubAgentOutput;
  /** 中间事件流 */
  events: SubAgentEvent[];
}

export interface SubAgentOutput {
  /** 文本结论 */
  summary: string;
  /** 结构化结果 (工具产出 / 决策) */
  artifacts: Array<{
    kind: 'file' | 'diff' | 'test_result' | 'plan' | 'fact' | 'custom';
    path?: string;
    content: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
  }>;
  /** 子 Agent 提出的后续建议 */
  followUps?: string[];
  /** 给父 Agent 的消息 (合并到父上下文) */
  messageToParent?: string;
}

export interface SubAgentEvent {
  id: string;
  timestamp: number;
  kind: 'spawn' | 'model_call' | 'tool_call' | 'tool_result' | 'pause' | 'resume'
       | 'cost_warning' | 'budget_exceeded' | 'iteration' | 'complete' | 'error';
  data?: unknown;
}
```

#### 3.11.2 派生执行流程

```typescript
export class SubagentManagerImpl {
  private handles = new Map<string, SubAgentHandle>();
  private abortControllers = new Map<string, AbortController>();
  /** 当前活跃子 Agent,按 parent 分组 */
  private childrenByParent = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  /** 嵌套深度 */
  private nestingDepth = new Map<string, number>();

  constructor(
    private db: Database,
    private loopEngine: LoopEngine,
    private contextEngine: ContextEngine,
    private permission: PermissionGateway,
    private eventBus: EventBus,
    private costTracker: CostTracker,
    /** 全局并发上限 */
    private maxConcurrent: number = 8,
    /** 默认单子 Agent 超时 */
    private defaultTimeoutSecs: number = 600,
  ) {
    this.registerEventHandlers();
  }

  /**
   * 派生一个子 Agent。非阻塞,返回 handle 后由调用方决定是否 await。
   */
  async spawn(config: SubAgentConfig): Promise<SubAgentHandle> {
    // 1) 预算与并发检查
    await this.checkSpawnPreconditions(config);

    // 2) 嵌套深度检查
    const parentDepth = this.nestingDepth.get(config.parentId) ?? 0;
    const maxDepth = config.maxNestingDepth ?? 3;
    if (parentDepth >= maxDepth) {
      throw new Error(`Max nesting depth (${maxDepth}) exceeded for parent ${config.parentId}`);
    }
    if (!config.allowNesting && parentDepth > 0) {
      throw new Error('Nested Sub-Agent not allowed by config');
    }

    // 3) 创建子会话
    const childSessionId = nanoid();
    const subId = nanoid();
    const handle: SubAgentHandle = {
      id: subId,
      config,
      status: 'pending',
      startedAt: Date.now(),
      parentSessionId: config.parentId,
      childSessionId,
      spentUsd: 0,
      usedTokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
      iterations: 0,
      events: [],
    };

    // 4) 持久化 handle
    await this.persistHandle(handle);

    // 5) 注册到映射表
    this.handles.set(subId, handle);
    if (!this.childrenByParent.has(config.parentId)) {
      this.childrenByParent.set(config.parentId, new Set());
    }
    this.childrenByParent.get(config.parentId)!.add(subId);
    this.nestingDepth.set(subId, parentDepth + 1);

    // 6) 构建 AbortController
    const controller = new AbortController();
    this.abortControllers.set(subId, controller);

    // 7) 触发 spawn 事件 (供 hooks / 监控使用)
    await this.eventBus.emit('sub_agent.spawn', { subId, parentId: config.parentId, role: config.role });
    handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'spawn', data: { config } });

    // 8) 异步启动子 Loop
    handle.status = 'running';
    this.runSubAgent(handle, controller.signal).catch(err => {
      handle.status = 'failed';
      handle.events.push({
        id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(),
        kind: 'error', data: { error: (err as Error).message },
      });
    });

    return handle;
  }

  private async runSubAgent(handle: SubAgentHandle, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
    const { config, childSessionId } = handle;
    const start = Date.now();
    const timeoutMs = (config.timeoutSecs ?? this.defaultTimeoutSecs) * 1000;

    // 设置超时
    const timer = setTimeout(() => this.abort(handle.id, 'timeout'), timeoutMs);

    try {
      // 1) 构建子 Agent 上下文
      const childContext = await this.contextEngine.forkContext({
        sessionId: childSessionId,
        parentSessionId: config.parentId,
        inherit: config.contextInherit,
      });

      // 2) 注入 task prompt (作为第一条 system / user 消息)
      await this.contextEngine.injectSystemMessage(
        childSessionId,
        this.buildTaskPrompt(config, childContext),
      );

      // 3) 配置工具白名单 / 黑名单
      const toolFilter = {
        whitelist: config.toolWhitelist,
        blacklist: config.toolBlacklist,
      };

      // 4) 启动 Loop
      const loopHandle = await this.loopEngine.start({
        sessionId: childSessionId,
        modelOverride: config.modelOverride,
        toolFilter,
        maxIterations: config.maxIterations ?? 25,
        signal,
        callbacks: {
          onModelCall: (call) => this.onModelCall(handle, call),
          onToolCall: (call) => this.onToolCall(handle, call),
          onIteration: (n) => this.onIteration(handle, n),
          onComplete: (output) => this.onComplete(handle, output),
          onError: (e) => this.onSubError(handle, e),
        },
      });

      await loopHandle.done;
      handle.status = 'succeeded';
    } catch (e) {
      if (signal.aborted) {
        handle.status = 'aborted';
      } else {
        handle.status = 'failed';
        handle.events.push({
          id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'error',
          data: { error: (e as Error).message },
        });
      }
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      handle.endedAt = Date.now();
      this.abortControllers.delete(handle.id);
      await this.persistHandle(handle);
      await this.eventBus.emit('sub_agent.end', {
        subId: handle.id, parentId: handle.parentSessionId,
        status: handle.status, durationMs: handle.endedAt - handle.startedAt,
      });
    }
  }

  private buildTaskPrompt(config: SubAgentConfig, ctx: ForkedContext): string {
    const lines: string[] = [];
    lines.push(`# Sub-Agent Role: ${config.role}`);
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('## Task');
    lines.push(config.taskPrompt);
    lines.push('');
    if (config.contextInherit.injectedContext?.length) {
      lines.push('## Injected Context');
      for (const item of config.contextInherit.injectedContext!) {
        lines.push(`### ${item.type}${item.pinned ? ' (pinned)' : ''}`);
        lines.push('```');
        lines.push(item.content);
        lines.push('```');
      }
    }
    if (config.costBudget) {
      lines.push('');
      lines.push(`## Cost Budget: $${config.costBudget.hardCap.toFixed(4)} (hard cap)`);
    }
    if (config.maxIterations) {
      lines.push(`## Max Iterations: ${config.maxIterations}`);
    }
    return lines.join('\n');
  }

  private async checkSpawnPreconditions(config: SubAgentConfig): Promise<void> {
    // 并发上限
    const running = Array.from(this.handles.values()).filter(h => h.status === 'running');
    if (running.length >= this.maxConcurrent) {
      throw new Error(`Max concurrent Sub-Agents (${this.maxConcurrent}) reached`);
    }
    // 父 Agent 必须存在且活跃
    // 预算校验:子任务硬上限不能超过父会话剩余预算
    if (config.costBudget) {
      const parentRemaining = await this.costTracker.getRemainingBudget(config.parentId);
      if (config.costBudget.hardCap > parentRemaining) {
        throw new Error(`Sub-Agent hard cap ($${config.costBudget.hardCap}) exceeds parent remaining ($${parentRemaining})`);
      }
    }
  }

  // ===== 状态查询 =====
  async getStatus(subId: string): Promise<SubAgentHandle | null> {
    return this.handles.get(subId) ?? null;
  }

  async listByParent(parentId: string): Promise<SubAgentHandle[]> {
    const ids = this.childrenByParent.get(parentId);
    if (!ids) return [];
    return Array.from(ids).map(id => this.handles.get(id)!).filter(Boolean);
  }

  // ===== 控制 API =====
  async pause(subId: string): Promise<void> {
    const handle = this.handles.get(subId);
    if (!handle || handle.status !== 'running') return;
    handle.status = 'paused';
    handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'pause' });
    await this.loopEngine.pause(handle.childSessionId);
  }

  async resume(subId: string): Promise<void> {
    const handle = this.handles.get(subId);
    if (!handle || handle.status !== 'paused') return;
    handle.status = 'running';
    handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'resume' });
    await this.loopEngine.resume(handle.childSessionId);
  }

  async abort(subId: string, reason: 'user' | 'timeout' | 'budget' = 'user'): Promise<void> {
    const controller = this.abortControllers.get(subId);
    const handle = this.handles.get(subId);
    if (!controller || !handle) return;
    handle.status = reason === 'timeout' ? 'timeout' : 'aborted';
    handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'error', data: { reason } });
    controller.abort();
    await this.loopEngine.abort(handle.childSessionId);
  }

  // ===== 回调 =====
  private async onModelCall(handle: SubAgentHandle, call: ModelCallEvent): Promise<void> {
    handle.usedTokens.input += call.inputTokens;
    handle.usedTokens.output += call.outputTokens;
    const cost = this.costTracker.computeCost(call);
    handle.spentUsd += cost;

    // 预算检查
    if (handle.config.costBudget) {
      const ratio = handle.spentUsd / handle.config.costBudget.hardCap;
      if (ratio >= 1) {
        handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'budget_exceeded' });
        await this.abort(handle.id, 'budget');
      } else if (ratio >= handle.config.costBudget.warningThreshold) {
        handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'cost_warning', data: { ratio } });
        await this.eventBus.emit('sub_agent.cost_warning', {
          subId: handle.id, parentId: handle.parentSessionId, ratio, spent: handle.spentUsd,
        });
      }
    }

    handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'model_call', data: call });
    await this.persistHandle(handle);
  }

  private async onToolCall(handle: SubAgentHandle, call: ToolCallEvent): Promise<void> {
    handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'tool_call', data: call });
  }

  private async onIteration(handle: SubAgentHandle, n: number): Promise<void> {
    handle.iterations = n;
    handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'iteration', data: { n } });
    if (handle.config.maxIterations && n >= handle.config.maxIterations) {
      await this.abort(handle.id, 'timeout');
    }
  }

  private async onComplete(handle: SubAgentHandle, output: SubAgentOutput): Promise<void> {
    handle.output = output;
    handle.events.push({ id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'complete', data: output });

    // 成本归属:把子 Agent 花费登记到父会话
    await this.costTracker.attributeToParent({
      childSessionId: handle.childSessionId,
      parentSessionId: handle.parentSessionId,
      amount: handle.spentUsd,
      tokens: handle.usedTokens,
      role: handle.config.role,
    });

    // 结果合并到父上下文
    if (handle.config.resultMergeStrategy && handle.config.resultMergeStrategy !== 'discard') {
      await this.mergeToParent(handle, output);
    }
  }

  private async onSubError(handle: SubAgentHandle, e: unknown): Promise<void> {
    handle.events.push({
      id: nanoid(), timestamp: Date.now(), kind: 'error',
      data: { error: (e as Error).message },
    });
  }

  // ===== 结果合并策略 =====
  private async mergeToParent(handle: SubAgentHandle, output: SubAgentOutput): Promise<void> {
    const strategy = handle.config.resultMergeStrategy;
    switch (strategy) {
      case 'append':
        // 直接把子 Agent 的总结 + 工件作为父上下文新条目
        await this.contextEngine.appendContext(handle.parentSessionId, {
          type: 'sub_agent_result',
          label: `Sub-Agent [${handle.config.role}] ${handle.id}`,
          content: output.summary,
          artifacts: output.artifacts,
          pinned: false,
        });
        break;
      case 'summarize':
        // 用 LLM 二次摘要 (避免污染父上下文 token 预算)
        const summarized = await this.summarizeOutput(output);
        await this.contextEngine.appendContext(handle.parentSessionId, {
          type: 'sub_agent_result',
          label: `Sub-Agent [${handle.config.role}] summary`,
          content: summarized,
          pinned: false,
        });
        break;
      case 'replace':
        // 替换父级当前任务上下文 (危险,需 L2+ 权限)
        const decision = await this.permission.check({
          actor: { type: 'sub_agent', id: handle.id },
          action: 'context:replace',
          target: handle.parentSessionId,
          riskLevel: 'L2',
        });
        if (decision.allowed) {
          await this.contextEngine.replaceTaskContext(handle.parentSessionId, output);
        }
        break;
      case 'discard':
      default:
        // 不合并,仅归档
        break;
    }

    // 如果子 Agent 给父 Agent 留了消息,直接以 system 消息注入父会话
    if (output.messageToParent) {
      await this.contextEngine.injectSystemMessage(
        handle.parentSessionId,
        `[Sub-Agent ${handle.config.role}] ${output.messageToParent}`,
      );
    }
  }

  private async summarizeOutput(output: SubAgentOutput): Promise<string> {
    // 简化:实际由 LLM 完成
    return `Sub-Agent 完成 ${output.artifacts.length} 个工件。摘要: ${output.summary.slice(0, 500)}`;
  }

  // ===== 持久化 =====
  private async persistHandle(handle: SubAgentHandle): Promise<void> {
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO sub_agents
        (id, parent_session_id, child_session_id, role, status, config,
         spent_usd, input_tokens, output_tokens, iterations, started_at, ended_at, output, events)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(
      handle.id, handle.parentSessionId, handle.childSessionId,
      handle.config.role, handle.status, JSON.stringify(handle.config),
      handle.spentUsd, handle.usedTokens.input, handle.usedTokens.output,
      handle.iterations, Math.floor(handle.startedAt / 1000),
      handle.endedAt ? Math.floor(handle.endedAt / 1000) : null,
      handle.output ? JSON.stringify(handle.output) : null,
      JSON.stringify(handle.events),
    );
  }

  private registerEventHandlers(): void {
    this.eventBus.on('session.end', async (sessionId: string) => {
      // 父会话结束时,取消所有未完成子 Agent
      const children = this.childrenByParent.get(sessionId) ?? new Set();
      for (const childId of children) {
        const h = this.handles.get(childId);
        if (h && (h.status === 'running' || h.status === 'paused')) {
          await this.abort(childId, 'user');
        }
      }
    });
  }
}
```

#### 3.11.3 上下文隔离与成本归属

```typescript
// src/main/agent/subagent/cost-tracker.ts
export class CostTracker {
  constructor(private db: Database) {}

  /** 计算单次模型调用成本 */
  computeCost(call: ModelCallEvent): number {
    const pricing = this.getPricing(call.provider, call.model);
    const inputCost = (call.inputTokens / 1_000_000) * pricing.inputPerMillion;
    const outputCost = (call.outputTokens / 1_000_000) * pricing.outputPerMillion;
    return inputCost + outputCost;
  }

  private getPricing(provider: string, model: string): { inputPerMillion: number; outputPerMillion: number } {
    // 从 model_pricings 表读取
    const row = this.db.prepare(`
      SELECT input_price_per_million, output_price_per_million
      FROM model_pricings WHERE provider = ? AND model = ?
    `).get(provider, model) as any;
    return row
      ? { inputPerMillion: row.input_price_per_million, outputPerMillion: row.output_price_per_million }
      : { inputPerMillion: 0, outputPerMillion: 0 };
  }

  /** 获取会话剩余预算 */
  async getRemainingBudget(sessionId: string): Promise<number> {
    const row = this.db.prepare(`
      SELECT budget_usd, spent_usd FROM sessions WHERE id = ?
    `).get(sessionId) as any;
    if (!row || !row.budget_usd) return Infinity;
    return Math.max(0, row.budget_usd - row.spent_usd);
  }

  /** 把子 Agent 的成本归属到父会话 */
  async attributeToParent(params: {
    childSessionId: string;
    parentSessionId: string;
    amount: number;
    tokens: { input: number; output: number };
    role: string;
  }): Promise<void> {
    const tx = this.db.transaction(() => {
      // 1) 记录成本归属关系
      this.db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO cost_attributions
          (id, child_session_id, parent_session_id, amount, input_tokens, output_tokens, role, attributed_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `).run(
        nanoid(), params.childSessionId, params.parentSessionId,
        params.amount, params.tokens.input, params.tokens.output,
        params.role, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
      );
      // 2) 累加父会话 spent_usd
      this.db.prepare(`
        UPDATE sessions SET spent_usd = spent_usd + ? WHERE id = ?
      `).run(params.amount, params.parentSessionId);
      // 3) 标记子会话为已归属
      this.db.prepare(`
        UPDATE sessions SET cost_attributed_to = ?, attributed_amount = ? WHERE id = ?
      `).run(params.parentSessionId, params.amount, params.childSessionId);
    });
    tx();
  }
}

// src/main/context/fork.ts
export interface ForkedContext {
  sessionId: string;
  inheritedMessages: number;
  inheritedMemories: number;
  redactedFields: number;
}

export class ContextForker {
  constructor(
    private db: Database,
    private contextEngine: ContextEngine,
  ) {}

  async fork(params: {
    sessionId: string;
    parentSessionId: string;
    inherit: ContextInheritStrategy;
  }): Promise<ForkedContext> {
    const { sessionId, parentSessionId, inherit } = params;

    // 1) 按比例继承父会话历史
    const parentMessages = await this.db.prepare(`
      SELECT * FROM messages WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC
    `).all(parentSessionId) as any[];

    const inheritCount = Math.floor(parentMessages.length * inherit.historyRatio);
    const toInherit = parentMessages.slice(-inheritCount);

    // 2) 脱敏处理 (redactPatterns)
    let redactedCount = 0;
    for (const msg of toInherit) {
      let content = msg.content;
      if (inherit.redactPatterns) {
        for (const pattern of inherit.redactPatterns) {
          const regex = new RegExp(pattern, 'g');
          if (regex.test(content)) {
            content = content.replace(regex, '[REDACTED]');
            redactedCount++;
          }
        }
      }
      // 写入子会话 (新 id, 新 created_at, parent_msg_id 关联)
      this.db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO messages (id, session_id, role, content, parent_msg_id, created_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `).run(nanoid(), sessionId, msg.role, content, msg.id, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000));
    }

    // 3) 继承项目记忆 / 全局配置
    if (inherit.inheritProjectContext) {
      const projectMemories = await this.db.prepare(`
        SELECT * FROM memories WHERE scope = 'project' AND project_id = (
          SELECT project_id FROM sessions WHERE id = ?
        )
      `).all(parentSessionId);
      // 注入到子会话上下文 (但不写新行,只是引擎内存引用)
      this.contextEngine.attachMemories(sessionId, projectMemories);
    }

    return {
      sessionId,
      inheritedMessages: toInherit.length,
      inheritedMemories: inherit.inheritProjectContext ? 1 : 0,
      redactedFields: redactedCount,
    };
  }
}
```
