### 3.12 MultiagentCoordinator (主从 / 并行 / 专家 + 通信 + 并发)

> **设计目标**: 在 SubagentManager 提供的「单父 → 多子」派生能力之上,Coordinator 负责多 Agent 的**协同编排**:决定拓扑结构 (主从 / 并行 / 专家群 / 流水线 / 竞争)、调度策略、Agent 间通信协议、共享状态协调、冲突仲裁与全局预算控制。

#### 3.12.1 拓扑模式

```typescript
// src/main/agent/coordinator/types.ts
export type TopologyKind =
  | 'master_worker'      // 1 主 N 从: 主 Agent 拆任务并汇总
  | 'parallel_fanout'    // 扇出并行: 同一任务派给 N 个 Agent,取最佳 / 投票
  | 'expert_panel'       // 专家群: 多角色专家围绕同一任务各自产出,再由主持人融合
  | 'pipeline'           // 流水线: A→B→C 串行,各自专长
  | 'competitive'        // 竞争: N 个 Agent 同时跑,先到 / 最优胜出
  | 'hierarchical'       // 分层: 顶层 Coordinator → 中层 Lead → 底层 Worker
  | 'swarm'              // 群体: 无中心,Agent 之间自由通信
  | 'custom';            // 用户自定义拓扑

export interface TopologySpec {
  kind: TopologyKind;
  /** 参与的 Agent 角色列表 (含主从标识) */
  agents: Array<{
    id: string;
    role: string;
    isCoordinator?: boolean;
    isLead?: boolean;
    subordinates?: string[];   // hierarchical 模式下级
  }>;
  /** 通信图 (邻接表) */
  communicationGraph: Record<string, string[]>;
  /** 共享状态键 (所有 Agent 可见) */
  sharedStateKeys?: string[];
  /** 超时 (整体) */
  totalTimeoutSecs?: number;
  /** 全局预算 (USD) */
  globalBudgetUsd?: number;
  /** 冲突解决策略 */
  conflictResolution?: ConflictResolutionStrategy;
  /** 终止条件 */
  terminationCondition?: TerminationCondition;
}

export type ConflictResolutionStrategy =
  | 'last_writer_wins'
  | 'first_writer_wins'
  | 'majority_vote'      // 多数投票
  | 'coordinator_decides' // Coordinator 仲裁
  | 'human'              // 人工裁决 (HITL)
  | 'merge'              // 自动合并 (语义)
  | 'block';             // 阻塞,等待人工

export interface TerminationCondition {
  kind: 'all_complete'        // 全部完成
       | 'first_complete'     // 第一个完成 (competitive)
       | 'quorum_complete'    // 多数完成 (quorum)
       | 'budget_exhausted'   // 预算用尽
       | 'timeout'            // 超时
       | 'custom';
  quorum?: number;            // quorum_complete 时使用
  predicate?: (state: CoordinatorState) => boolean;  // custom
}

export interface CoordinatorState {
  topology: TopologySpec;
  phase: 'initializing' | 'running' | 'paused' | 'finalizing' | 'completed' | 'failed' | 'aborted';
  agents: Map<string, AgentRuntimeState>;
  sharedState: Map<string, unknown>;
  mailbox: Map<string, AgentMessage[]>;          // 各 Agent 收件箱
  conflicts: ConflictRecord[];
  startedAt: number;
  endedAt?: number;
  totalSpentUsd: number;
  totalTokens: { input: number; output: number };
}

export interface AgentRuntimeState {
  agentId: string;
  status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'paused' | 'succeeded' | 'failed' | 'aborted';
  progress: number;            // 0-1
  lastHeartbeat: number;
  currentIteration: number;
  output?: unknown;
  errors: string[];
}

export interface AgentMessage {
  id: string;
  from: string;
  to: string | 'broadcast';
  type: 'task' | 'result' | 'query' | 'answer' | 'sync' | 'handoff' | 'vote' | 'conflict' | 'terminate';
  payload: unknown;
  timestamp: number;
  /** 是否需要 ACK */
  requireAck?: boolean;
  /** TTL (秒) */
  ttlSecs?: number;
}

export interface ConflictRecord {
  id: string;
  resource: string;             // 共享状态键 / 文件路径 / 决策点
  holders: string[];            // 持有冲突版本的 Agent 列表
  versions: Array<{ agentId: string; value: unknown; reason?: string }>;
  resolution?: {
    strategy: ConflictResolutionStrategy;
    winner?: string;
    mergedValue?: unknown;
    resolvedAt: number;
  };
  detectedAt: number;
}
```

#### 3.12.2 Coordinator 主类

```typescript
// src/main/agent/coordinator/coordinator.ts
import { nanoid } from 'nanoid';
import type { SubagentManager } from '../subagent/manager';
import type { Database } from '../../infra/database';
import type { EventBus } from '../../infra/event-bus';
import type { PermissionGateway } from '../permission/gateway';

export class MultiagentCoordinator {
  private states = new Map<string, CoordinatorState>();
  private timers = new Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout>();
  private abortControllers = new Map<string, AbortController>();

  constructor(
    private db: Database,
    private subManager: SubagentManager,
    private eventBus: EventBus,
    private permission: PermissionGateway,
    private maxConcurrentAgents: number = 16,
  ) {
    this.registerEventHandlers();
  }

  /**
   * 启动一个多 Agent 协同任务
   */
  async start(topology: TopologySpec, parentSessionId: string): Promise<string> {
    const coordId = nanoid();

    // 1) 校验拓扑
    this.validateTopology(topology);

    // 2) 创建状态
    const state: CoordinatorState = {
      topology,
      phase: 'initializing',
      agents: new Map(),
      sharedState: new Map(),
      mailbox: new Map(),
      conflicts: [],
      startedAt: Date.now(),
      totalSpentUsd: 0,
      totalTokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
    };
    for (const agent of topology.agents) {
      state.agents.set(agent.id, {
        agentId: agent.id,
        status: 'pending',
        progress: 0,
        lastHeartbeat: Date.now(),
        currentIteration: 0,
        errors: [],
      });
      state.mailbox.set(agent.id, []);
    }
    this.states.set(coordId, state);

    // 3) 持久化 Coordinator 记录
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO coordinator_runs (id, parent_session_id, topology, phase, started_at, config)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'initializing', ?, ?)
    `).run(coordId, parentSessionId, topology.kind,
           Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), JSON.stringify(topology));

    // 4) 设置超时 / 预算控制器
    const controller = new AbortController();
    this.abortControllers.set(coordId, controller);
    if (topology.totalTimeoutSecs) {
      this.timers.set(coordId, setTimeout(
        () => this.terminate(coordId, 'timeout'),
        topology.totalTimeoutSecs * 1000,
      ));
    }

    // 5) 预算校验
    if (topology.globalBudgetUsd) {
      // TODO: 调用 CostTracker 校验父会话剩余预算
    }

    // 6) 触发拓扑启动事件
    await this.eventBus.emit('coordinator.start', { coordId, topology });

    // 7) 异步启动各 Agent
    state.phase = 'running';
    this.launchAgents(coordId, parentSessionId, topology).catch(err => {
      state.phase = 'failed';
      this.eventBus.emit('coordinator.error', { coordId, error: (err as Error).message });
    });

    return coordId;
  }

  /**
   * 按拓扑结构启动各 Agent
   */
  private async launchAgents(
    coordId: string,
    parentSessionId: string,
    topology: TopologySpec,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId)!;

    switch (topology.kind) {
      case 'master_worker': {
        // 先启动主 Agent,由主 Agent 在 Loop 中通过 spawn_subagent 工具派生 worker
        const master = topology.agents.find(a => a.isCoordinator)!;
        await this.spawnAgentForCoord(coordId, parentSessionId, master, {
          role: 'coordinator',
          taskPrompt: this.buildMasterPrompt(topology),
        });
        break;
      }
      case 'parallel_fanout':
      case 'competitive': {
        // 并行启动所有 Agent
        const promises = topology.agents.map(a =>
          this.spawnAgentForCoord(coordId, parentSessionId, a, {
            role: a.role,
            taskPrompt: this.buildFanoutPrompt(topology, a),
          }),
        );
        await Promise.all(promises);
        break;
      }
      case 'expert_panel': {
        // 启动各专家,然后启动主持人
        const experts = topology.agents.filter(a => !a.isCoordinator);
        const moderator = topology.agents.find(a => a.isCoordinator);
        // 专家并行启动
        await Promise.all(experts.map(a =>
          this.spawnAgentForCoord(coordId, parentSessionId, a, {
            role: a.role, taskPrompt: this.buildExpertPrompt(topology, a),
          }),
        ));
        // 主持人延后启动 (等专家产出第一轮)
        if (moderator) {
          setTimeout(() => {
            this.spawnAgentForCoord(coordId, parentSessionId, moderator, {
              role: 'moderator', taskPrompt: this.buildModeratorPrompt(topology),
            });
          }, 5000);
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'pipeline': {
        // 串行启动,后一个等待前一个完成
        let prevId: string | null = null;
        for (const agent of topology.agents) {
          if (prevId) {
            // 等待前一个完成
            await this.waitForAgentComplete(coordId, prevId);
          }
          await this.spawnAgentForCoord(coordId, parentSessionId, agent, {
            role: agent.role,
            taskPrompt: this.buildPipelinePrompt(topology, agent, prevId),
          });
          prevId = agent.id;
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'hierarchical': {
        // 顶层 Coordinator 先启动,然后递归派生 Lead → Worker
        const root = topology.agents.find(a => a.isCoordinator)!;
        await this.spawnAgentForCoord(coordId, parentSessionId, root, {
          role: 'root_coordinator',
          taskPrompt: this.buildHierarchicalPrompt(topology, root),
        });
        break;
      }
      case 'swarm': {
        // 全部并行启动,Agent 之间自由通信
        await Promise.all(topology.agents.map(a =>
          this.spawnAgentForCoord(coordId, parentSessionId, a, {
            role: a.role, taskPrompt: this.buildSwarmPrompt(topology, a),
          }),
        ));
        break;
      }
      default:
        throw new Error(`Unsupported topology: ${topology.kind}`);
    }
  }

  private async spawnAgentForCoord(
    coordId: string,
    parentSessionId: string,
    agentSpec: TopologySpec['agents'][number],
    config: { role: string; taskPrompt: string },
  ): Promise<void> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId)!;
    state.agents.get(agentSpec.id)!.status = 'running';

    await this.subManager.spawn({
      parentId: parentSessionId,
      role: 'custom',
      taskPrompt: config.taskPrompt,
      contextInherit: {
        historyRatio: 0.3,
        inheritProjectContext: true,
        inheritGlobalConfig: true,
      },
      toolWhitelist: ['send_message', 'read_shared_state', 'write_shared_state', 'spawn_subagent'],
      maxIterations: 50,
      timeoutSecs: 600,
      allowNesting: true,
      maxNestingDepth: 2,
      resultMergeStrategy: 'summarize',
    });
  }

  // ===== 通信协议 =====

  /**
   * Agent 之间发送消息
   */
  async sendMessage(coordId: string, msg: AgentMessage): Promise<void> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId);
    if (!state) throw new Error(`Coordinator not found: ${coordId}`);

    // 投递到收件箱
    if (msg.to === 'broadcast') {
      for (const agentId of state.mailbox.keys()) {
        if (agentId !== msg.from) {
          state.mailbox.get(agentId)!.push(msg);
        }
      }
    } else {
      state.mailbox.get(msg.to)?.push(msg);
    }

    // 持久化消息
    this.db.prepare(`
      INSERT INTO agent_messages (id, coord_id, from_agent, to_agent, type, payload, timestamp)
      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `).run(msg.id, coordId, msg.from, msg.to, msg.type,
           JSON.stringify(msg.payload), Math.floor(msg.timestamp / 1000));

    // 触发事件,通知接收方
    await this.eventBus.emit('agent.message', { coordId, msg });
  }

  /**
   * Agent 读取自己的收件箱 (并清空)
   */
  async readMail(coordId: string, agentId: string): Promise<AgentMessage[]> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId);
    if (!state) return [];
    const inbox = state.mailbox.get(agentId) ?? [];
    const msgs = [...inbox];
    inbox.length = 0;
    return msgs;
  }

  // ===== 共享状态协调 =====

  async readSharedState(coordId: string, key: string): Promise<unknown> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId);
    return state?.sharedState.get(key);
  }

  /**
   * 写入共享状态 - 触发冲突检测
   */
  async writeSharedState(
    coordId: string,
    key: string,
    value: unknown,
    writer: string,
    reason?: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId);
    if (!state) throw new Error(`Coordinator not found: ${coordId}`);

    const existing = state.sharedState.get(key);

    // 检测冲突:同一 key 已被其他 Agent 写过且值不同
    if (existing !== undefined && existing !== value) {
      // 找出上一个写者
      const lastWriter = this.getLastWriter(coordId, key);
      if (lastWriter && lastWriter !== writer) {
        // 记录冲突
        const conflict: ConflictRecord = {
          id: nanoid(),
          resource: key,
          holders: [lastWriter, writer],
          versions: [
            { agentId: lastWriter, value: existing },
            { agentId: writer, value, reason },
          ],
          detectedAt: Date.now(),
        };
        state.conflicts.push(conflict);
        await this.eventBus.emit('coordinator.conflict', { coordId, conflict });

        // 触发冲突解决
        const resolution = await this.resolveConflict(coordId, conflict);
        if (resolution.action === 'block') {
          // 阻塞写入,等待人工
          return;
        }
        if (resolution.winner === writer) {
          state.sharedState.set(key, value);
          this.recordWrite(coordId, key, writer);
        }
        return;
      }
    }

    // 无冲突,直接写入
    state.sharedState.set(key, value);
    this.recordWrite(coordId, key, writer);
    await this.eventBus.emit('coordinator.state_update', { coordId, key, writer });
  }

  private writeHistory = new Map<string, Map<string, string>>(); // coordId → key → agentId

  private getLastWriter(coordId: string, key: string): string | undefined {
    return this.writeHistory.get(coordId)?.get(key);
  }

  private recordWrite(coordId: string, key: string, writer: string): void {
    if (!this.writeHistory.has(coordId)) this.writeHistory.set(coordId, new Map());
    this.writeHistory.get(coordId)!.set(key, writer);
  }

  // ===== 冲突仲裁 =====

  private async resolveConflict(
    coordId: string,
    conflict: ConflictRecord,
  ): Promise<{ action: 'proceed' | 'block'; winner?: string }> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId)!;
    const strategy = state.topology.conflictResolution ?? 'coordinator_decides';

    switch (strategy) {
      case 'last_writer_wins':
        return { action: 'proceed', winner: conflict.holders[1] };
      case 'first_writer_wins':
        return { action: 'proceed', winner: conflict.holders[0] };
      case 'majority_vote':
        // 询问所有 Agent 投票
        const votes = await Promise.all(
          conflict.holders.map(id => this.requestVote(id, conflict)),
        );
        const winner = this.tallyVotes(votes);
        return { action: 'proceed', winner };
      case 'coordinator_decides':
        const decision = await this.coordinatorDecide(conflict);
        return { action: decision.action, winner: decision.winner };
      case 'human':
        const ruling = await this.requestHumanRuling(conflict);
        return { action: ruling.action, winner: ruling.winner };
      case 'merge':
        const merged = await this.mergeVersions(conflict);
        if (merged.ok) {
          state.sharedState.set(conflict.resource, merged.value);
          return { action: 'proceed' };
        }
        return { action: 'block' };
      case 'block':
      default:
        return { action: 'block' };
    }
  }

  private async requestVote(agentId: string, conflict: ConflictRecord): Promise<{ agentId: string; choice: string }> {
    // 通过 agent_messages 让 Agent 表决
    return { agentId, choice: conflict.holders[0] };
  }

  private tallyVotes(votes: Array<{ agentId: string; choice: string }>): string {
    const tally = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const v of votes) tally.set(v.choice, (tally.get(v.choice) ?? 0) + 1);
    let winner = votes[0]?.choice;
    let max = 0;
    for (const [choice, count] of tally) {
      if (count > max) { max = count; winner = choice; }
    }
    return winner!;
  }

  private async coordinatorDecide(conflict: ConflictRecord): Promise<{ action: 'proceed' | 'block'; winner?: string }> {
    // 简化:选第一个
    return { action: 'proceed', winner: conflict.holders[0] };
  }

  private async requestHumanRuling(conflict: ConflictRecord): Promise<{ action: 'proceed' | 'block'; winner?: string }> {
    // 通过 IPC 通知 UI,等待用户裁决
    return { action: 'block' };
  }

  private async mergeVersions(conflict: ConflictRecord): Promise<{ ok: boolean; value?: unknown }> {
    // 简化:返回 false,需人工合并
    return { ok: false };
  }

  // ===== 终止与汇总 =====

  /**
   * 主动终止整个 Coordinator
   */
  async terminate(coordId: string, reason: 'user' | 'timeout' | 'budget' | 'complete'): Promise<void> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId);
    if (!state) return;
    if (state.phase === 'completed' || state.phase === 'aborted') return;

    state.phase = reason === 'complete' ? 'completed' : 'aborted';
    state.endedAt = Date.now();

    // 终止所有子 Agent
    for (const [agentId, agentState] of state.agents) {
      if (agentState.status === 'running' || agentState.status === 'paused') {
        // 通过 SubagentManager 终止
        // (实际实现需保留 subAgentId 映射)
      }
    }

    // 清理 timer
    const timer = this.timers.get(coordId);
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    this.timers.delete(coordId);
    this.abortControllers.delete(coordId);

    // 持久化最终状态
    this.db.prepare(`
      UPDATE coordinator_runs
      SET phase = ?, ended_at = ?, total_spent_usd = ?, total_input_tokens = ?, total_output_tokens = ?
      WHERE id = ?
    `).run(state.phase, Math.floor(state.endedAt / 1000),
           state.totalSpentUsd, state.totalTokens.input, state.totalTokens.output, coordId);

    await this.eventBus.emit('coordinator.end', { coordId, reason, state });
  }

  /**
   * 检查终止条件
   */
  private async checkTermination(coordId: string): Promise<void> {
    const state = this.states.get(coordId);
    if (!state || state.phase !== 'running') return;

    const cond = state.topology.terminationCondition ?? { kind: 'all_complete' };

    let shouldTerminate = false;
    let reason: 'complete' | 'timeout' = 'complete';

    switch (cond.kind) {
      case 'all_complete': {
        const allDone = Array.from(state.agents.values())
          .every(a => a.status === 'succeeded' || a.status === 'failed' || a.status === 'aborted');
        if (allDone) shouldTerminate = true;
        break;
      }
      case 'first_complete': {
        const anyDone = Array.from(state.agents.values()).some(a => a.status === 'succeeded');
        if (anyDone) shouldTerminate = true;
        break;
      }
      case 'quorum_complete': {
        const completed = Array.from(state.agents.values()).filter(a => a.status === 'succeeded').length;
        if (completed >= (cond.quorum ?? Math.ceil(state.agents.size / 2) + 1)) {
          shouldTerminate = true;
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'custom':
        if (cond.predicate?.(state)) shouldTerminate = true;
        break;
    }

    if (shouldTerminate) await this.terminate(coordId, reason);
  }

  // ===== 提示词构建 =====

  private buildMasterPrompt(topology: TopologySpec): string {
    return [
      '# Coordinator Role: Master',
      'You are the master agent. Decompose the task and spawn workers via spawn_subagent tool.',
      `Workers available: ${topology.agents.filter(a => !a.isCoordinator).map(a => a.role).join(', ')}`,
      'After all workers complete, synthesize their outputs into a final answer.',
    ].join('\n');
  }

  private buildFanoutPrompt(topology: TopologySpec, agent: TopologySpec['agents'][number]): string {
    return [
      `# Agent Role: ${agent.role}`,
      'Work on the task independently. Your output will be compared with other agents.',
      topology.kind === 'competitive'
        ? 'First correct / best output wins. Be fast but accurate.'
        : 'Your output will be merged via voting / fusion.',
    ].join('\n');
  }

  private buildExpertPrompt(topology: TopologySpec, agent: TopologySpec['agents'][number]): string {
    return [
      `# Expert Role: ${agent.role}`,
      'Provide your expert opinion on the task. Focus on your domain.',
      'Use send_message to share findings with the moderator.',
    ].join('\n');
  }

  private buildModeratorPrompt(topology: TopologySpec): string {
    return [
      '# Moderator Role',
      'Collect expert opinions via read_mail. Synthesize a final answer.',
      'Resolve conflicts between experts via majority vote.',
    ].join('\n');
  }

  private buildPipelinePrompt(
    topology: TopologySpec,
    agent: TopologySpec['agents'][number],
    prevId: string | null,
  ): string {
    return [
      `# Pipeline Stage: ${agent.role}`,
      prevId ? `Previous stage output is available in shared_state key "pipeline.${prevId}".` : 'You are the first stage.',
      'Write your output to shared_state key "pipeline.<your_id>".',
    ].join('\n');
  }

  private buildHierarchicalPrompt(topology: TopologySpec, root: TopologySpec['agents'][number]): string {
    return [
      '# Root Coordinator Role',
      'Decompose the task and spawn Leads. Each Lead may further spawn Workers.',
      `Subordinates: ${(root.subordinates ?? []).join(', ')}`,
    ].join('\n');
  }

  private buildSwarmPrompt(topology: TopologySpec, agent: TopologySpec['agents'][number]): string {
    return [
      `# Swarm Agent: ${agent.role}`,
      'No central coordinator. Communicate freely with peers via send_message.',
      `Peers: ${topology.agents.filter(a => a.id !== agent.id).map(a => a.id).join(', ')}`,
      'Reach consensus through peer communication.',
    ].join('\n');
  }

  // ===== 工具方法 =====

  private validateTopology(topology: TopologySpec): void {
    if (topology.agents.length === 0) throw new Error('No agents in topology');
    if (topology.agents.length > this.maxConcurrentAgents) {
      throw new Error(`Too many agents (${topology.agents.length} > ${this.maxConcurrentAgents})`);
    }
    // 检查通信图与 agents 一致
    const agentIds = new Set(topology.agents.map(a => a.id));
    for (const [from, tos] of Object.entries(topology.communicationGraph)) {
      if (!agentIds.has(from)) throw new Error(`Unknown agent in comm graph: ${from}`);
      for (const to of tos) {
        if (!agentIds.has(to)) throw new Error(`Unknown agent in comm graph: ${to}`);
      }
    }
  }

  private async waitForAgentComplete(coordId: string, agentId: string): Promise<void> {
    // 轮询子 Agent 状态
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const check = () => {
        const state = this.states.get(coordId);
        const agent = state?.agents.get(agentId);
        if (agent && (agent.status === 'succeeded' || agent.status === 'failed' || agent.status === 'aborted')) {
          resolve();
        } else {
          setTimeout(check, 1000);
        }
      };
      check();
    });
  }

  // ===== 事件处理 =====

  private registerEventHandlers(): void {
    // 监听 SubAgent 状态变更,更新 Coordinator 状态
    this.eventBus.on('sub_agent.end', async (payload: { subId: string; parentId: string; status: string }) => {
      for (const [coordId, state] of this.states) {
        if (state.agents.has(payload.subId)) {
          const agent = state.agents.get(payload.subId)!;
          agent.status = payload.status as any;
          agent.lastHeartbeat = Date.now();
          await this.checkTermination(coordId);
        }
      }
    });

    this.eventBus.on('sub_agent.cost_warning', async (payload: { subId: string; parentId: string; ratio: number; spent: number }) => {
      // 累加到 Coordinator 全局成本
      for (const [coordId, state] of this.states) {
        if (state.agents.has(payload.subId)) {
          state.totalSpentUsd += payload.spent;
          if (state.topology.globalBudgetUsd && state.totalSpentUsd >= state.topology.globalBudgetUsd) {
            await this.terminate(coordId, 'budget');
          }
        }
      }
    });
  }

  // ===== 状态查询 =====
  async getStatus(coordId: string): Promise<CoordinatorState | null> {
    return this.states.get(coordId) ?? null;
  }
}
```

#### 3.12.3 全局成本治理

```typescript
// src/main/agent/coordinator/global-cost-governor.ts
export class GlobalCostGovernor {
  private sessionBudgets = new Map<string, SessionBudget>();

  /** 检查协作会话是否超预算 */
  checkSession(sessionId: string): CostCheckResult {
    const budget = this.sessionBudgets.get(sessionId);
    if (!budget) return { exceeded: false, remaining: Infinity };

    const totalSpent = budget.spent;
    const remaining = budget.limit - totalSpent;

    return {
      exceeded: remaining <= 0,
      remaining,
      warning: remaining < budget.limit * 0.2, // 20% 预警
      perAgent: this.getPerAgentSpent(sessionId),
    };
  }

  /** 批准 SubAgent spawn (基于预算) */
  approveSpawn(sessionId: string, config: SubAgentConfig): ApprovalDecision {
    const check = this.checkSession(sessionId);
    if (check.exceeded) {
      return { approved: false, reason: 'budget_exceeded' };
    }
    if (config.costBudget && config.costBudget.hardCap > check.remaining) {
      return { approved: false, reason: 'insufficient_remaining' };
    }
    return { approved: true };
  }
}
```

#### 3.12.4 配置

```yaml
coordinator:
  enabled: true
  max_concurrent_agents: 16
  default_total_timeout_secs: 1800
  default_global_budget_usd: 5.0
  default_conflict_resolution: coordinator_decides
  default_termination: all_complete
  heartbeat_interval_secs: 5
  mailbox_retention_secs: 3600
  topologies:
    master_worker:
      default_worker_count: 3
    parallel_fanout:
      default_agent_count: 3
      result_strategy: best_of_n
    expert_panel:
      default_experts: [architect, security_expert, perf_expert]
    pipeline:
      default_stages: 4
```

### 3.14 定时任务与调度详设 (SCHED, v4.3 新增)

> 对 02-PRD v2.4 REQ-SCHED-001~004 与 01 v2.4 §4.14 的落地详细设计；技术选型见 05 §23.1。

#### 3.14.1 模块结构与依赖

```
packages/core/src/domain/repositories/
  ScheduledTaskRepository.ts        // 接口
  ScheduledTaskRunRepository.ts    // 接口
packages/core/src/application/scheduler/
  SchedulerService.ts               // 调度器核心 (cron/interval/event/once)
  SchedulerEngine.ts                // node-cron 包装
  SchedulerExecutor.ts              // 在 Worker/Subprocess 内执行任务
  SchedulerState.ts                 // 调度状态机 (idle/due/running/done/failed/cancelled)
packages/core/src/infra/db/sqlite/
  SqliteScheduledTaskRepository.ts  // 实现
  SqliteScheduledTaskRunRepository.ts // 实现
```

#### 3.14.2 表结构 (DAG 实体) — 见 §2.1 表 `scheduled_tasks` / `scheduled_task_runs`

#### 3.14.3 调度状态机

```
      [create]            [enable]         [due/cron tick]       [exec success]
disabled ──────► enabled ──────► due ──────────► running ──────────► done
   ▲                  │             │                       │
   │ [disable]        │ [disable]   │                       │ [failed (retry<max)]
   │                  ▼             │                       ▼
disabled          disabled        skipped                 failed ──► (escalate/halt per policy)
                                                                              
           ────► cancelled
```

#### 3.14.4 关键算法

1. **启动恢复**: 服务启动时加载 `scheduled_tasks WHERE enabled=1`，按 cron 表达式重建 next_run_at；丢失的 due 检查并按失败策略处理 (skip / retry)。
2. **触发分发**: SchedulerEngine 用 `node-cron` 注册每个 cron 表达式；interval/once 模式用 setTimeout + 持久化校验避免重复执行。
3. **事件触发**: 复用 §3.x EventBus，监听 `on_commit / on_idle / on_cost_warn` 等事件，按 scheduled_tasks.event_name 字段匹配触发。
4. **执行隔离**: SchedulerExecutor 通过 Worker Threads 启动执行 Run；任务默认在受限 Sandbox (对应 §3.10 PluginSandbox 同框架)，权限策略 (默认只读) 经 PermissionGateway (§3.5) 校验。
5. **审计**: 所有 Run 调用经审计日志通道 (audit_log)，与手工调用同型 (append-only)。
6. **失败处理**: `recovery.max_retries`, 失败后按 `failure_policy` (retry/skip/escalate/halt) 处理；连续 K 次 escalate 触发系统通知。

#### 3.14.5 安全约束详设

- 启用周期性破坏性任务前强制 `dry_run_persist_id` 必须存在 (预演一次成功才能 enable)
- 默认权限集: `read_only`；显式声明的 L2/L3 操作需经 `pre_approval_tokens` 表/字段预审批
- 全局并发上限: `scheduler.max_concurrent` (默认 2)
- 单任务速率限制: `scheduler.per_task_rate_limit` (tokens/min)

#### 3.14.6 与 Goal/LoopEvent 联动

- 当 scheduled_task 关联到 `goal_id` 时，Run 完成/失败写入 goal 进度 (见 §3.15)
- 操作完成触发 `scheduler.run.completed` 事件供 Hooks 监听

### 3.15 目标管理详设 (GOAL, v4.3 新增)

> 对 REQ-GOAL-001~005 与 01 v2.4 §4.15 的落地详细设计。

#### 3.15.1 模块结构

```
packages/core/src/domain/
  entity/Goal.ts                    // 目标 + 验收标准 + 父子 + 进度
  repositories/GoalRepository.ts
packages/core/src/application/goal/
  GoalService.ts                    // CRUD / 分解 / 进度评估 / 升级
  GoalProgressEvaluator.ts         // Reflect Loop 评估器
  GoalDecomposeTool.ts             // Agent 工具: goal_decompose
packages/core/src/infra/db/sqlite/
  SqliteGoalRepository.ts
```

#### 3.15.2 表结构 — 见 §2.1 `goals` 表

字段要点: `id / title / description / acceptance_criteria(JSON) / parent_id / status / priority / time_window(JSON) / progress(JSON / linked_session_ids(JSON) / linked_memory_ids(JSON) / milestone(bool) / context_snapshot(blob, 跨会话续作) / created_at / updated_at`

#### 3.15.3 跨会话续作算法

```typescript
async resumeActiveGoals(sessionId: string) {
  const active = await goalRepo.findActive();
  for (const g of active) {
    const snapshot = g.context_snapshot; // 缓存 JSON
    await contextManager.inject(g.snapshot); // 注入工作上下文
    await sessionRepo.linkGoal(sessionId, g.id);
  }
}
```

#### 3.15.4 进度自评流程

Reflect Loop (§3.1 反思阶段) 触发 `GoalProgressEvaluator.evaluate(goal)`:
1. 拉取关联 session_ids 与 loop_steps
2. 调用 LLM 对照 acceptance_criteria 自评分数与状态
3. 更新 `goals.progress` (score / acceptance_pass_count / acceptance_total / last_evaluated_at)
4. 连续 K 次 `progress_no_delta` → 升级事件 `goal.blocked` 触发通知 + Hooks

#### 3.15.5 goal_decompose 工具契约

```json
{
  "name": "goal_decompose",
  "description": "将目标分解为子目标 + 验收标准",
  "inputSchema": { "goal_id": "string", "depth": "int" },
  "returns": [{ "sub_goal": { "title": "...", "acceptance_criteria": ["..."] } }]
}
```

### 3.16 提示词管理详设 (PROMPT, v4.3 新增)

> 对 REQ-PROMPT-001~006 与 01 v2.4 §4.16 的落地详细设计。

#### 3.16.1 模块结构

```
packages/core/src/domain/
  entity/PromptTemplate.ts          // 七层洋葱模型 + 模型变体 + 版本
  repositories/PromptTemplateRepository.ts
packages/core/src/application/prompt/
  PromptService.ts                  // 模板 CRUD / 七层编辑 / 渲染
  PromptRenderer.ts                 // Handlebars 插值 + 模型变体选择
  PromptPlaygroundService.ts        // 离线 dry-run 调试
  PromptABExperimenter.ts           // A/B 实验分流
packages/core/src/infra/db/sqlite/
  SqlitePromptTemplateRepository.ts
  SqlitePromptTemplateVariantRepository.ts   // prompt_template_variants 子表
```

#### 3.16.2 模板存储结构

- 主表 `prompt_templates`: id / name / scope(global|project|persona|session) / owner_id / 7 层内容 JSON / version / released(bool) / created_at / updated_at / created_by
- 子表 `prompt_template_variants`: template_id / provider / variant_content(JSON) / capability_mask
- 子表 `prompt_template_revisions` (可选历史版本化): template_id / version / content / released_at
- 子表 `prompt_ab_assignments` (A/B): experiment_id / template_id / weight / session_id / served_at

#### 3.16.3 七层渲染管线 (从 04 §5)

```
context_pipeline ─► compose(prompt_layers) ─► PickVariant(provider) ─► Render(handlebars, vars) ─► final prompt
```

#### 3.16.4 Playground (离线 dry-run)

RPC 方法 `prompt.playground(req)`:
1. 加载 template + variants + 用户 vars
2. 选定 provider 实例化模型
3. 不写入主 Loop，调用计 cost 直接归到 `audit_log` + `model_calls.tag='playground'`
4. 返回: render 后的 prompt、token 预算、模型输出、用时

#### 3.16.5 A/B 实验

A/B 分流: `hash(sessionId + experimentId) % 100 < weight` 选 variant；记录 assignment；后续 evaluation 按实验统计 success rate / cost / satisfaction。

### 3.17 Provider 抽象接入详设 (PROVIDER, v4.3 新增)

> 对 REQ-PROVIDER-001~004 与 01 v2.4 §4.3.5 的落地详细设计；技术选型见 05 §23.4。

#### 3.17.1 模块结构

```
packages/core/src/domain/
  entity/ProviderConfig.ts          // 含 capability_mask / models / pricing
  entity/ProviderCapability.ts
  repositories/ProviderRepository.ts
packages/ai-sdk/src/                // 自研 @openwork/ai-sdk 包
  types.ts                          // AIProviderAdapter 契约
  capabilities.ts                   // 能力位常量 + 检测
  baseAdapter.ts                    // 抽象 Adapter 基类 (流式/工具协议 polyfill)
  registry.ts                       // ProviderRegistry
  router.ts                         // ProviderRouter (按任务/成本/能力位选择)
  fallback.ts                       // FallbackChain
  pricing.ts                        // cost 计算
  builtinAdapters/
    openaiSchemaAdapter.ts          // 任意 OpenAI 兼容端点
    openaiAdapter.ts
    anthropicAdapter.ts
    googleAdapter.ts
    ollamaAdapter.ts
packages/core/src/infra/db/sqlite/
  SqliteProviderRepository.ts
```

#### 3.17.2 AIProviderAdapter 契约 (与 04 §10 协同)

```typescript
export interface AIProviderAdapter {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly kind: 'openai' | 'anthropic' | 'google' | 'ollama' | 'azure' | 'bedrock' | 'mistral' | 'cohere' | 'deepseek' | 'grok' | 'openrouter' | 'custom';
  readonly capabilities: ProviderCapability;
  readonly models: ModelSpec[];
  chat(req: ChatRequest): Promise<ChatResponse>;
  chatStream(req: ChatRequest): AsyncIterable<ChatChunk>;
  embed?(req: EmbedRequest): Promise<EmbedResponse>;
  countTokens(text: string, model: string): number;
  estimateCost(usage: TokenUsage, model: string): number;
  healthCheck(): Promise<HealthStatus>;
}

export type ProviderCapability = {
  streaming?: boolean;
  tool_call?: boolean;
  vision?: boolean;
  json_mode?: boolean;
  parallel_tool?: boolean;
  prompt_caching?: boolean;
  thinking?: boolean;
  system_as_role?: boolean;     // Anthropic = true; OpenAI 兼容 = false (system message)
};
```

#### 3.17.3 Registry 热插拔

- 启动时: 从 `providers` 表加载 active Providers，按 `kind` 实例化对应 builtin Adapter；自建 Adapter 通过 Plugin 系统在 init 时调用 `registry.register(adapter)`
- 运行时 enable/disable: Capability 检测与 healthCheck，写入 `providers.status`
- 冲突检测: `registry.assertUnique(id)`

#### 3.17.4 自定义接入 (v0.3 企业版)

- 发布 `@openwork/ai-sdk` npm 包，导出 `AIProviderAdapter` 类型与 `baseAdapter` 基类
- 企业编写 `class MyGatewayAdapter extends baseAdapter` 实现 chat/chatStream
- 经 Plugin 注册 → Reservation → 同等的 cost/审计/Fallback 待遇

#### 3.17.5 路由与 Fallback

`ProviderRouter.route(taskKind, capabilityRequired, budgetWeight)` 按 Dijkstra-like 算法选 Provider；主 Provider healthCheck 失败 → 沿 `fallback_chain` 降级；Ollama 永远作为底线。

---

### 3.13 前后端边界与通信约束

> **设计目标**: 自 v4.1 起,系统采用前后端分离架构。本节明确后端 (`@openwork/core`) 与前端 thin client (Desktop / TUI / Web / CLI) 之间的边界与通信约束,确保前后端解耦、可独立部署与测试。

#### 3.13.1 数据库访问边界

| 边界 | 规则 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|
| 后端 | **唯一数据库访问者** | `better-sqlite3` 连接、29 张表读写、sqlite-vec / FTS5 索引操作均在后端进程内完成 |
| 前端 | **禁止直接访问数据库** | 前端无 `better-sqlite3` 依赖,无 SQL 字符串;所有数据通过 §4 JSON-RPC 方法获取 |
| 嵌入式模式 | 后端与前端同进程 | 数据库仍由 `@openwork/core` 持有,前端经 EmbeddedTransport 直接调用核心 API (零序列化) |
| 服务式模式 | 后端独占 DB 文件锁 | 多客户端共享同一后端,后端串行化 DB 写事务 (WAL) |

```typescript
// packages/core/src/db/index.ts — 仅后端可导入
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
export const db = new Database(DB_PATH, { fileMustExist: true });
// ❌ 前端 packages/* 不得 import 此模块;由 @openwork/api 契约层隔离
```

#### 3.13.2 EventBus 与事件推送边界

| 边界 | 规则 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|
| 后端 EventBus | 进程内同步派发 | `EventBus` (§3.x) 在后端进程内同步执行 listener,处理 HITL、审计、Memory 落库 |
| 前端订阅 | 经 Transport 层推送 | 后端 EventBus 触发的事件,经 `EventBridge` 序列化为 JSON-RPC 通知,通过 Transport 推送到已订阅前端 |
| 可靠性 | 尽力推送 + 客户端补偿 | 非关键事件丢失可容忍;关键事件 (如 `tool.approval_needed`) 由客户端通过 `agent.inspect` 主动补偿查询 |
| 背压 | 后端侧缓冲 + 丢弃策略 | 高频事件 (如 `agent.event` token 流) 由后端侧 ring buffer 缓冲,满则按策略丢弃旧值或合并 |

```typescript
// packages/server/src/event-bridge.ts
// 后端 EventBus → Transport 通知 桥接
eventBus.on('*', (eventName, payload) => {
  for (const sub of subscriptionRegistry.match(eventName)) {
    transport.notify(sub.clientId, `event.${eventName}`, payload); // JSON-RPC notification
  }
});
```

#### 3.13.3 前后端职责划分

```
┌─────────────────────────────── 前端 (thin client) ───────────────────────────────┐
│  UI 渲染 / 用户输入 / 状态管理 (zustand) / 本地偏好 / 调用 OpenCodeClient          │
│  apps: desktop (Electron) · tui (Ink) · web (React) · cli (Commander)             │
└──────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                   │ JSON-RPC 2.0 (HTTP+SSE / WS / stdio / IPC / Embed)
┌──────────────────────────────────▼────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  后端 (@openwork/core + @openwork/server)                                          │
│  LoopEngine · PromptEngine · ContextEngine · ToolRegistry · PermissionGateway      │
│  MemorySystem · MCPClient · HooksRuntime · SkillsRuntime · PluginSandbox           │
│  SubagentManager · Coordinator · Database(唯一) · EventBus · Audit · Index         │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. API 接口清单 (JSON-RPC 2.0)

> **架构基线**: 后端为 headless Agent 引擎 `@openwork/core`,前端为多个 thin client,统一通过 **JSON-RPC 2.0** 协议通信。本节定义协议规范、方法清单、事件清单、Transport 接口与客户端 SDK。契约类型由 `@openwork/api` 包共享,前后端类型安全。

### 4.1 JSON-RPC 2.0 协议定义

#### 4.1.1 请求 / 响应格式

遵循 [JSON-RPC 2.0 规范](https://www.jsonrpc.org/specification)。

```typescript
// packages/api/src/protocol.ts — 前后端共享
export interface JsonRpcRequest<P = unknown> {
  jsonrpc: '2.0';
  id: string | number | null;   // null 表示通知 (Notification)
  method: string;                // 如 "agent.run"
  params?: P;                    // 对象或数组
}

export interface JsonRpcResponse<R = unknown> {
  jsonrpc: '2.0';
  id: string | number | null;
  result?: R;
  error?: JsonRpcError;
}

export interface JsonRpcError {
  code: number;
  message: string;
  data?: unknown;                // 附加诊断信息 (堆栈、字段错误等)
}

// 通知 (Notification): id 为 null,无响应
export type JsonRpcNotification<P = unknown> = JsonRpcRequest<P> & { id: null };
```

**请求示例**:
```json
{
  "jsonrpc": "2.0",
  "id": "req_01HZ...",
  "method": "agent.run",
  "params": { "sessionId": "ses_01HZ...", "prompt": "重构 auth 模块" }
}
```

**响应示例**:
```json
{ "jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": "req_01HZ...", "result": { "runId": "run_01HZ...", "status": "running" } }
```

#### 4.1.2 错误码

JSON-RPC 标准错误码 + 业务错误码 (完整表见 §6)。Transport 层不得使用业务错误码。

| 范围 | 类别 | 示例 |
|------|------|------|
| -32700 | Parse error (JSON 解析失败) | 请求体非法 JSON |
| -32600 | Invalid Request (不符合规范) | 缺少 `jsonrpc` 字段 |
| -32601 | Method not found | 未知方法名 |
| -32602 | Invalid params | 参数 Zod 校验失败 (`data` 含字段错误清单) |
| -32603 | Internal error | 后端未捕获异常 |
| -32000 ~ -32099 | Transport 错误 | 连接断开、超时、认证失败 |
| -32100 ~ -32999 | 业务错误码 (见 §6) | `PERM_DENIED=-32101`、`BUDGET_EXCEEDED=-32110` 等 |

```typescript
export function rpcError(id: JsonRpcRequest['id'], code: number, message: string, data?: unknown): JsonRpcResponse {
  return { jsonrpc: '2.0', id, error: { code, message, data } };
}
```

#### 4.1.3 批量请求

支持单次传输携带多个请求/通知,后端并行执行并按原顺序返回响应 (通知无响应)。

```json
[
  { "jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "session.list", "params": {} },
  { "jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 2, "method": "config.get", "params": { "key": "model" } },
  { "jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": null, "method": "audit.entry", "params": { "action": "view_sessions" } }
]
```

> 批量请求上限 32 条/批;超出返回 `-32600`。stdio / IPC Transport 必须支持批量;HTTP 单 POST 请求支持批量,流式方法 (`stream:true`) 不允许进入批量。

#### 4.1.4 通知 (Notification)

`id` 为 `null` 的请求为通知,后端不返回响应。用于:事件推送 (后端→前端)、审计埋点、客户端取消信号 (`$/cancel`)。

#### 4.1.5 流式响应扩展

JSON-RPC 2.0 本身为单请求单响应。流式结果 (如 Agent token 流) 采用 **流式响应扩展**:

- **HTTP+SSE**: 客户端 POST 请求,后端返回 `Content-Type: text/event-stream`,逐条推送 `JsonRpcResponse` (相同 `id`,`result.partial` 累积),最后推送 `result.done`。
- **WebSocket / IPC / stdio**: 多条 `JsonRpcResponse` 共享同一 `id`,以 `result.stream: 'chunk' | 'end'` 标记。
- **取消**: 客户端发送通知 `$/cancel` `{ id: <原请求 id> }`。

```jsonc
// SSE 流式分片示例 (同一 id 多条)
data: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":"r1","result":{"stream":"chunk","delta":"重构"}}
data: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":"r1","result":{"stream":"chunk","delta":"方案:"}}
data: {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":"r1","result":{"stream":"end","runId":"run_01","status":"completed","usage":{"input":1200,"output":800}}}
```

### 4.2 API 方法清单 (按域分组)

> 命名约定: `<domain>.<action>`。所有方法参数与返回值由 `@openwork/api` 的 Zod schema 定义,前后端共享。下表标注: **流式** (是否支持流式响应)、**权限** (调用所需权限等级,见 §3.5 PermissionGateway L0-L3)。

#### 4.2.1 Agent 域 — Agent 执行控制

| 方法 | 参数 | 返回 | 流式 | 权限 |
|------|------|------|:---:|------|
| `agent.run` | `{ sessionId, prompt, mode?, tools?, budget? }` | `{ runId, status }` | ✅ (token 流) | L1 |
| `agent.stop` | `{ runId, reason? }` | `{ stopped: true }` | ❌ | L1 |
| `agent.pause` | `{ runId }` | `{ status: 'paused' }` | ❌ | L1 |
| `agent.resume` | `{ runId }` | `{ status: 'running' }` | ❌ | L1 |
| `agent.step` | `{ runId, kind: 'next'\|'over'\|'into' }` | `{ stepId, action }` | ❌ | L1 |
| `agent.inspect` | `{ runId }` | `{ status, loop, context, toolCalls, cost }` | ❌ | L0 |

**`agent.run` 详细契约**:

```typescript
// packages/api/src/domains/agent.ts
import { z } from 'zod';

export const AgentRunParams = z.object({
  sessionId: z.string().min(1),
  prompt: z.string().min(1),
  mode: z.enum(['chat','build','debug','review','plan']).default('chat'),
  tools: z.array(z.string()).optional(),          // 允许使用的工具白名单
  budget: z.object({
    hardCapUsd: z.number().positive().optional(),
    maxIterations: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
  }).optional(),
  stream: z.boolean().default(true),              // 是否流式返回 token
});

export const AgentRunResult = z.object({
  runId: z.string(),
  status: z.enum(['running','paused','completed','stopped','error']),
});

// 流式分片 result (stream:'chunk')
export const AgentStreamChunk = z.object({
  stream: z.literal('chunk'),
  delta: z.string(),                              // 增量文本
  toolCallId: z.string().optional(),              // 工具调用中间态
});

// 流式结束 result (stream:'end')
export const AgentStreamEnd = z.object({
  stream: z.literal('end'),
  runId: z.string(),
  status: z.enum(['completed','stopped','error']),
  usage: z.object({ input: z.number(), output: z.number(), costUsd: z.number() }),
  finalText: z.string().optional(),
});
```

**调用示例** (OpenCodeClient):
```typescript
const stream = client.call('agent.run', { sessionId, prompt: '重构 auth 模块', stream: true });
for await (const chunk of stream) {
  if (chunk.stream === 'chunk') process.stdout.write(chunk.delta);
  else console.log('\n完成:', chunk.usage);
}
```

#### 4.2.2 Session 域 — 会话管理

| 方法 | 参数 | 返回 | 流式 | 权限 |
|------|------|------|:---:|------|
| `session.create` | `{ projectId, title?, mode? }` | `{ sessionId, createdAt }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `session.list` | `{ projectId?, limit?, cursor? }` | `{ items: Session[], nextCursor? }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `session.get` | `{ sessionId }` | `{ session, messages, stats }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `session.delete` | `{ sessionId }` | `{ deleted: true }` | ❌ | L2 (删除需确认) |
| `session.export` | `{ sessionId, format: 'md'\|'json'\|'jsonl' }` | `{ url, bytes }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `session.fork` | `{ sessionId, fromMessageId? }` | `{ newSessionId }` | ❌ | L0 |

#### 4.2.3 Tool 域 — 工具调用与审批

| 方法 | 参数 | 返回 | 流式 | 权限 |
|------|------|------|:---:|------|
| `tool.list` | `{ category?, enabled? }` | `{ tools: ToolDef[] }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `tool.execute` | `{ tool, args, sessionId?, approvalToken? }` | `{ callId, output, meta }` | ✅ (长任务) | L2 (按工具) |
| `tool.approve` | `{ approvalToken, decision: 'allow'\|'deny', scope? }` | `{ granted: true }` | ❌ | L2 |

#### 4.2.4 Config 域 — 配置读写

| 方法 | 参数 | 返回 | 流式 | 权限 |
|------|------|------|:---:|------|
| `config.get` | `{ key?, profile? }` | `{ value }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `config.set` | `{ key, value, profile? }` | `{ validated: true, oldValue }` | ❌ | L1 |
| `config.list` | `{ profile? }` | `{ entries: ConfigEntry[] }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `config.validate` | `{ config }` | `{ valid, errors: ZodError[] }` | ❌ | L0 |

#### 4.2.5 Memory 域 — 记忆系统

| 方法 | 参数 | 返回 | 流式 | 权限 |
|------|------|------|:---:|------|
| `memory.write` | `{ sessionId, category, content, metadata? }` | `{ memoryId }` | ❌ | L1 |
| `memory.search` | `{ query, k?, filter? }` | `{ items: MemoryHit[] }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `memory.list` | `{ sessionId?, category?, limit? }` | `{ items: Memory[] }` | ❌ | L0 |
| `memory.delete` | `{ memoryId }` | `{ deleted: true }` | ❌ | L2 |
| `memory.consolidate` | `{ sessionId, strategy? }` | `{ consolidated: number }` | ❌ | L1 |

| 方法 | 参数 | 返回 | 流式 | 权限 |

| `skill.install` | `{ source: 'npm'\|'git'\|'path', ref }` | `{ skillId, version }` | ❌ | L2 |

| 方法 | 参数 | 返回 | 流式 | 权限 |

| 方法 | 参数 | 返回 | 流式 | 权限 |
|------|------|------|:---:|------|
